# PART 6: PRACTICAL SECURITY PROJECTS & REAL-WORLD IMPLEMENTATIONS

## Introduction: From Theory to Practice

**You've learned the theory. Now it's time to build.**

Parts 1-5 gave you the knowledge:

- **Part 4:** Advanced topics (AI, blockchain, IoT, zero trust)

**Part 6 is different.** This is your hands-on workshop.

## What You'll Build in Part 6

### **12 Complete Security Projects:**

1. **Enterprise Security Operations Center (SOC)**
   - Build a complete SOC from scratch
   - SIEM deployment (Elastic Stack)
   - Detection rules and playbooks
   - Incident response automation
   - Cost: ~$50k annual, supports 1,000 employees

2. **Secure Cloud Infrastructure (AWS)**
   - Multi-account AWS architecture
   - Zero trust networking
   - Automated compliance checking
   - Infrastructure as Code (Terraform)
   - Production-ready, enterprise-grade

3. **DevSecOps Pipeline**
   - Complete CI/CD with security gates
   - SAST, DAST, SCA, container scanning
   - Automated security testing
   - Policy enforcement
   - GitHub Actions + Jenkins

4. **Bug Bounty Hunting Lab**
   - Vulnerable application environment
   - Reconnaissance automation
   - Exploitation frameworks
   - Report writing templates
   - Practice environment for OSCP/CEH

5. **Incident Response Platform**
   - Case management system
   - Digital forensics toolkit
   - Memory analysis automation
   - Evidence collection pipeline
   - Chain of custody tracking

6. **Security Awareness & Phishing Platform**
   - Custom phishing simulation tool
   - Training content delivery
   - Gamification engine
   - Reporting dashboard
   - Alternative to KnowBe4 ($25k/year savings)

7. **Threat Intelligence Platform**
   - OSINT collection automation
   - Threat feed aggregation
   - IOC management

   - Free alternative to commercial platforms

8. **Vulnerability Management System**
   - Automated scanning (Nessus, OpenVAS)

9. **Identity & Access Management (IAM)**

10. **Application Security Testing Lab**

11. **Network Security Monitoring**

    - Security dashboards
    - East-west traffic visibility

12. **Compliance Automation Framework**
    - Evidence collection automation

    - Audit preparation toolkit

## Important Notes

### **Legal and Ethical Considerations:**

⚠️ **Only test on systems you own or have permission to test**

⚠️ **Bug bounty projects:** Use only on designated vulnerable labs

⚠️ **Penetration testing:** Never test production systems without authorization

⚠️ **Data privacy:** Use synthetic data, never real customer data

⚠️ **Compliance:** Ensure projects meet your regulatory requirements

### **Production Deployment:**

🔒 **Security review:** Have projects reviewed before production use

🔒 **Change management:** Follow proper change control procedures

🔒 **Backup:** Always backup before deploying to production

🔒 **Rollback plan:** Have a way to undo changes if something breaks

🔒 **Testing:** Test in dev/staging before production

## Chapter 6.1: Building an Enterprise Security Operations Center (SOC)

### Executive Summary

**What You'll Build:**
- Complete 24/7 SOC capability
- Elastic Stack SIEM (ELK + Security)
- 50+ detection rules
- Automated incident response
- Threat hunting platform

**Business Value:**
- Detect threats in minutes vs. hours/days
- Reduce MTTD from 207 days to <1 hour
- Save $150k/year vs. commercial SIEM
- Enable compliance (SOC 2, ISO 27001)

**Time to Complete:** 8-12 hours
**Estimated Cost:** $5,000 setup + $4,000/month operations

**vs. Commercial Alternative:**
- Splunk: $150k-$300k/year
- QRadar: $100k-$200k/year
- Sentinel: $200-$500/day
- **Savings: $100k-$250k/year**

---

### Architecture Overview

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                     DATA SOURCES                             │
├──────────┬──────────┬──────────┬──────────┬─────────────────┤
│ Endpoints│  Servers │  Network │   Cloud  │  Applications   │
│  (EDR)   │  (Logs)  │ (Flows)  │  (AWS)   │   (Custom)      │
└────┬─────┴────┬─────┴────┬─────┴────┬─────┴────┬────────────┘
     │          │          │          │          │
     └──────────┴──────────┴──────────┴──────────┘
                         │
                    ┌────▼────┐
                    │ Beats   │ (Data Shippers)
                    │ Agents  │
                    └────┬────┘
                         │
                    ┌────▼────────────────────┐
                    │   LOGSTASH/KAFKA        │
                    │   (Data Processing)     │
                    │   - Parse               │
                    │   - Enrich              │
                    │   - Filter              │
                    └────┬────────────────────┘
                         │
                    ┌────▼────────────────────┐
                    │   ELASTICSEARCH         │
                    │   (Storage & Search)    │
                    │   - Index logs          │
                    │   - Search/query        │
                    │   - Analytics           │
                    └────┬────────────────────┘
                         │
         ┌───────────────┼───────────────┐
         │               │               │
    ┌────▼────┐    ┌────▼────┐    ┌────▼────┐
    │ KIBANA  │    │ ELASTIC │    │  SIEM   │
    │(Dashbd) │    │  AGENT  │    │  Rules  │
    │         │    │(Response)│    │         │
    └─────────┘    └─────────┘    └─────────┘
```

**Component Breakdown:**

1. **Data Collection Layer:**
   - Beats (Filebeat, Winlogbeat, Packetbeat, Metricbeat)
   - Elastic Agent (unified agent)
   - Custom shippers for specialized sources

2. **Data Processing Layer:**
   - Logstash for parsing and enrichment
   - Kafka for buffering (optional, high-volume)
   - Enrichment: GeoIP, threat intel, asset context

3. **Storage & Search Layer:**
   - Elasticsearch cluster (3+ nodes)
   - Index lifecycle management
   - Hot-warm-cold architecture

4. **Analysis & Visualization Layer:**
   - Kibana for dashboards
   - Elastic Security app
   - Custom detection rules

5. **Response & Automation Layer:**
   - Elastic Agent for response actions
   - Integration with ticketing (ServiceNow, Jira)
   - SOAR capabilities (TheHive, Cortex)

### Prerequisites

**Knowledge Requirements:**
- Linux system administration (intermediate)
- Networking fundamentals
- Security concepts (SIEM, IOCs, threat hunting)
- Basic scripting (Python or Bash)

**Infrastructure Requirements:**

**Minimum (Lab/POC):**
- 1 server: 16GB RAM, 8 CPU cores, 500GB SSD
- Handles ~10GB/day logs
- Cost: ~$100/month cloud or existing hardware

**Production (Small - 500 employees):**
- 3 servers: 32GB RAM each, 16 CPU cores, 1TB SSD
- Handles ~50GB/day logs
- Cost: ~$800/month cloud or $15k one-time hardware

**Production (Medium - 2,000 employees):**
- 6 servers: 64GB RAM each, 32 CPU cores, 2TB SSD
- Handles ~200GB/day logs
- Cost: ~$2,500/month cloud or $50k one-time hardware

**Production (Large - 10,000+ employees):**
- 12+ servers: 128GB RAM each, 64 CPU cores, 4TB SSD
- Handles ~1TB/day logs
- Cost: ~$8,000/month cloud or $150k one-time hardware

**Software Requirements:**
- Elasticsearch 8.x
- Kibana 8.x
- Logstash 8.x
- Beats (various)
- Elastic Security (included)

**Accounts Needed:**
- Cloud provider (AWS/Azure/GCP) or on-prem servers
- Elastic Cloud account (optional, alternative to self-hosted)
- Threat intelligence feeds (optional, many free options)

### Step-by-Step Implementation

#### Phase 1: Infrastructure Setup (2-3 hours)

**Step 1.1: Provision Infrastructure**

For this guide, we'll use AWS (adjust for your provider):

```bash
# Infrastructure as Code - Terraform
# File: elasticsearch-cluster.tf

terraform {
  required_version = ">= 1.0"
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source  = "hashicorp/aws"
      version = "~> 5.0"
    }
  }
}

provider "aws" {
  region = var.aws_region
}

# Variables
variable "aws_region" {
  default = "us-east-1"
}

variable "cluster_name" {
  default = "soc-elasticsearch"
}

variable "instance_type" {
  default = "m5.2xlarge"  # 8 vCPU, 32GB RAM
}

variable "node_count" {
  default = 3
}

# VPC for SOC
resource "aws_vpc" "soc_vpc" {
  cidr_block           = "10.0.0.0/16"
  enable_dns_hostnames = true
  enable_dns_support   = true

  tags = {
    Name = "${var.cluster_name}-vpc"
  }
}

# Subnet
resource "aws_subnet" "soc_subnet" {
  vpc_id                  = aws_vpc.soc_vpc.id
  cidr_block              = "10.0.1.0/24"
  availability_zone       = "${var.aws_region}a"
  map_public_ip_on_launch = true

  tags = {
    Name = "${var.cluster_name}-subnet"
  }
}

# Internet Gateway
resource "aws_internet_gateway" "soc_igw" {
  vpc_id = aws_vpc.soc_vpc.id

  tags = {
    Name = "${var.cluster_name}-igw"
  }
}

# Route Table
resource "aws_route_table" "soc_rt" {
  vpc_id = aws_vpc.soc_vpc.id

  route {
    cidr_block = "0.0.0.0/0"
    gateway_id = aws_internet_gateway.soc_igw.id
  }

  tags = {
    Name = "${var.cluster_name}-rt"
  }
}

resource "aws_route_table_association" "soc_rta" {
  subnet_id      = aws_subnet.soc_subnet.id
  route_table_id = aws_route_table.soc_rt.id
}

# Security Group
resource "aws_security_group" "elasticsearch_sg" {
  name        = "${var.cluster_name}-sg"
  description = "Security group for Elasticsearch cluster"
  vpc_id      = aws_vpc.soc_vpc.id

  # Elasticsearch HTTP (9200)
  ingress {
    from_port   = 9200
    to_port     = 9200
    protocol    = "tcp"
    cidr_blocks = ["10.0.0.0/16"]  # Internal only
  }

  # Elasticsearch Transport (9300)
  ingress {
    from_port   = 9300
    to_port     = 9300
    protocol    = "tcp"
    cidr_blocks = ["10.0.0.0/16"]
  }

  # Kibana (5601)
  ingress {
    from_port   = 5601
    to_port     = 5601
    protocol    = "tcp"
    cidr_blocks = ["0.0.0.0/0"]  # Restrict to VPN/office IP
  }

  # SSH (22)
  ingress {
    from_port   = 22
    to_port     = 22
    protocol    = "tcp"
    cidr_blocks = ["0.0.0.0/0"]  # Restrict to your IP
  }

  # Logstash (5044 - Beats input)
  ingress {
    from_port   = 5044
    to_port     = 5044
    protocol    = "tcp"
    cidr_blocks = ["0.0.0.0/0"]  # All endpoints
  }

  egress {
    from_port   = 0
    to_port     = 0
    protocol    = "-1"
    cidr_blocks = ["0.0.0.0/0"]
  }

  tags = {
    Name = "${var.cluster_name}-sg"
  }
}

# EBS Volume for each node (separate data disks)
resource "aws_ebs_volume" "es_data" {
  count             = var.node_count
  availability_zone = "${var.aws_region}a"
  size              = 1000  # 1TB per node
  type              = "gp3"
  iops              = 3000
  throughput        = 125

  tags = {
    Name = "${var.cluster_name}-data-${count.index}"
  }
}

# EC2 Instances for Elasticsearch
resource "aws_instance" "elasticsearch" {
  count         = var.node_count
  ami           = data.aws_ami.ubuntu.id
  instance_type = var.instance_type
  subnet_id     = aws_subnet.soc_subnet.id
  
  vpc_security_group_ids = [aws_security_group.elasticsearch_sg.id]
  
  key_name = aws_key_pair.soc_key.key_name

  root_block_device {
    volume_size = 100  # OS disk
    volume_type = "gp3"
  }

  user_data = templatefile("${path.module}/userdata.sh", {
    node_name    = "${var.cluster_name}-${count.index}"
    cluster_name = var.cluster_name
  })

  tags = {
    Name = "${var.cluster_name}-${count.index}"
    Role = "elasticsearch"
  }
}

# Attach data volumes
resource "aws_volume_attachment" "es_data_attach" {
  count       = var.node_count
  device_name = "/dev/sdf"
  volume_id   = aws_ebs_volume.es_data[count.index].id
  instance_id = aws_instance.elasticsearch[count.index].id
}

# Get latest Ubuntu AMI
data "aws_ami" "ubuntu" {
  most_recent = true
  owners      = ["099720109477"]  # Canonical

  filter {
    name   = "name"
    values = ["ubuntu/images/hvm-ssd/ubuntu-jammy-22.04-amd64-server-*"]
  }
}

# SSH Key Pair
resource "aws_key_pair" "soc_key" {
  key_name   = "${var.cluster_name}-key"
  public_key = file("~/.ssh/id_rsa.pub")  # Your SSH public key
}

# Outputs
output "elasticsearch_nodes" {
  value = [for i in aws_instance.elasticsearch : i.public_ip]
}

output "kibana_url" {
  value = "http://${aws_instance.elasticsearch[0].public_ip}:5601"
}
```

**Deploy Infrastructure:**

```bash
# Generate SSH key if you don't have one
ssh-keygen -t rsa -b 4096 -f ~/.ssh/id_rsa

# Initialize Terraform
terraform init

# Plan deployment
terraform plan

# Deploy (takes ~5 minutes)
terraform apply -auto-approve

# Save outputs
terraform output > cluster-info.txt
```

---

**Step 1.2: Install Elasticsearch**

Create installation script:

```bash
# File: install-elasticsearch.sh
#!/bin/bash

set -e

NODE_NAME=$1
CLUSTER_NAME=$2
NODE_IP=$(hostname -I | awk '{print $1}')

echo "Installing Elasticsearch on ${NODE_NAME}"

# Update system
apt-get update
apt-get upgrade -y

# Install Java (Elasticsearch 8.x includes bundled JDK, but useful for other tools)
apt-get install -y openjdk-17-jdk

# Import Elasticsearch GPG key
wget -qO - https://artifacts.elastic.co/GPG-KEY-elasticsearch | gpg --dearmor -o /usr/share/keyrings/elasticsearch-keyring.gpg

# Add Elasticsearch repository
echo "deb [signed-by=/usr/share/keyrings/elasticsearch-keyring.gpg] https://artifacts.elastic.co/packages/8.x/apt stable main" | tee /etc/apt/sources.list.d/elastic-8.x.list

# Install Elasticsearch
apt-get update
apt-get install -y elasticsearch

# Configure Elasticsearch
cat > /etc/elasticsearch/elasticsearch.yml << EOF
# Cluster configuration
cluster.name: ${CLUSTER_NAME}
node.name: ${NODE_NAME}
network.host: ${NODE_IP}
http.port: 9200

# Discovery configuration (update with all node IPs)
discovery.seed_hosts: ["10.0.1.10", "10.0.1.11", "10.0.1.12"]
cluster.initial_master_nodes: ["${CLUSTER_NAME}-0", "${CLUSTER_NAME}-1", "${CLUSTER_NAME}-2"]

# Path configuration
path.data: /mnt/elasticsearch/data
path.logs: /var/log/elasticsearch

# Memory
bootstrap.memory_lock: true

# Security
xpack.security.enabled: true
xpack.security.enrollment.enabled: true

xpack.security.http.ssl:
  enabled: true
  keystore.path: certs/http.p12

xpack.security.transport.ssl:
  enabled: true
  verification_mode: certificate
  keystore.path: certs/transport.p12
  truststore.path: certs/transport.p12
EOF

# Prepare data directory
mkdir -p /mnt/elasticsearch/data
chown -R elasticsearch:elasticsearch /mnt/elasticsearch

# Configure JVM heap (50% of RAM, max 31GB)
TOTAL_RAM=$(free -g | awk '/^Mem:/{print $2}')
HEAP_SIZE=$((TOTAL_RAM / 2))
if [ $HEAP_SIZE -gt 31 ]; then
  HEAP_SIZE=31
fi

cat > /etc/elasticsearch/jvm.options.d/heap.options << EOF
-Xms${HEAP_SIZE}g
-Xmx${HEAP_SIZE}g
EOF

# Enable and start Elasticsearch
systemctl daemon-reload
systemctl enable elasticsearch
systemctl start elasticsearch

# Wait for Elasticsearch to start
sleep 30

# Generate enrollment token (first node only)
if [ "${NODE_NAME}" = "${CLUSTER_NAME}-0" ]; then
  echo "Generating enrollment token..."
  /usr/share/elasticsearch/bin/elasticsearch-create-enrollment-token -s node > /tmp/enrollment-token.txt
  
  # Reset elastic user password
  echo "Resetting elastic password..."
  /usr/share/elasticsearch/bin/elasticsearch-reset-password -u elastic -b > /tmp/elastic-password.txt
  
  echo "Enrollment token saved to /tmp/enrollment-token.txt"
  echo "Elastic password saved to /tmp/elastic-password.txt"
fi

echo "Elasticsearch installation complete!"
```

**Run installation on all nodes:**

```bash
# Get node IPs from Terraform output
NODES=$(terraform output -json elasticsearch_nodes | jq -r '.[]')

# Install on each node
INDEX=0
for NODE in $NODES; do
  echo "Installing on node $INDEX: $NODE"
  
  # Copy script
  scp install-elasticsearch.sh ubuntu@$NODE:/tmp/
  
  # Execute
  ssh ubuntu@$NODE "sudo bash /tmp/install-elasticsearch.sh soc-elasticsearch-$INDEX soc-elasticsearch"
  
  INDEX=$((INDEX + 1))
done

# Get elastic password from first node
FIRST_NODE=$(echo $NODES | awk '{print $1}')
ssh ubuntu@$FIRST_NODE "sudo cat /tmp/elastic-password.txt" > elastic-password.txt

echo "Elasticsearch cluster deployed!"
echo "Elastic password saved to elastic-password.txt"
```

---

**Step 1.3: Install Kibana**

```bash
# File: install-kibana.sh
#!/bin/bash

set -e

ELASTICSEARCH_HOST=$1
ELASTIC_PASSWORD=$2

echo "Installing Kibana..."

# Import GPG key (if not already done)
wget -qO - https://artifacts.elastic.co/GPG-KEY-elasticsearch | gpg --dearmor -o /usr/share/keyrings/elasticsearch-keyring.gpg

# Install Kibana
apt-get update
apt-get install -y kibana

# Configure Kibana
cat > /etc/kibana/kibana.yml << EOF
server.port: 5601
server.host: "0.0.0.0"
server.name: "soc-kibana"

elasticsearch.hosts: ["https://${ELASTICSEARCH_HOST}:9200"]
elasticsearch.username: "elastic"
elasticsearch.password: "${ELASTIC_PASSWORD}"

# Disable SSL for now (enable in production)
elasticsearch.ssl.verificationMode: none

# Enable security
xpack.security.enabled: true
xpack.encryptedSavedObjects.encryptionKey: "$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
EOF

# Enable and start Kibana
systemctl daemon-reload
systemctl enable kibana
systemctl start kibana

echo "Kibana installation complete!"
echo "Access Kibana at http://$(hostname -I | awk '{print $1}'):5601"
echo "Username: elastic"
echo "Password: ${ELASTIC_PASSWORD}"
```

**Deploy Kibana:**

```bash
# Install on first node
FIRST_NODE=$(terraform output -json elasticsearch_nodes | jq -r '.[0]')
ELASTIC_PASSWORD=$(cat elastic-password.txt)

scp install-kibana.sh ubuntu@$FIRST_NODE:/tmp/
ssh ubuntu@$FIRST_NODE "sudo bash /tmp/install-kibana.sh localhost $ELASTIC_PASSWORD"

# Wait for Kibana to start
sleep 60

# Get Kibana URL
KIBANA_URL=$(terraform output kibana_url)
echo "Kibana ready at: $KIBANA_URL"
echo "Username: elastic"
echo "Password: $ELASTIC_PASSWORD"
```

**Step 1.4: Install Logstash**

```bash
# File: install-logstash.sh
#!/bin/bash

set -e

ELASTICSEARCH_HOST=$1
ELASTIC_PASSWORD=$2

echo "Installing Logstash..."

# Install Logstash
apt-get update
apt-get install -y logstash

# Configure Logstash pipeline
cat > /etc/logstash/conf.d/beats-input.conf << 'EOF'
input {
  beats {
    port => 5044
    ssl => false
  }
}

filter {
  # GeoIP enrichment
  if [source][ip] {
    geoip {
      source => "[source][ip]"
      target => "[source][geo]"
    }
  }
  
  if [destination][ip] {
    geoip {
      source => "[destination][ip]"
      target => "[destination][geo]"
    }
  }
  
  # User agent parsing
  if [user_agent][original] {
    useragent {
      source => "[user_agent][original]"
      target => "[user_agent]"
    }
  }
  
  # Add timestamp
  mutate {
    add_field => { "[@metadata][index_date]" => "%{+YYYY.MM.dd}" }
  }
}

output {
  elasticsearch {
    hosts => ["https://${ELASTICSEARCH_HOST}:9200"]
    user => "elastic"
    password => "${ELASTIC_PASSWORD}"
    ssl => true
    ssl_certificate_verification => false
    index => "logs-%{[@metadata][index_date]}"
  }
}
EOF

# Configure JVM heap
cat > /etc/logstash/jvm.options.d/heap.options << EOF
-Xms4g
-Xmx4g
EOF

# Enable and start Logstash
systemctl daemon-reload
systemctl enable logstash
systemctl start logstash

echo "Logstash installation complete!"
```

**Deploy Logstash:**

```bash
# Install on first node (can install on multiple for redundancy)
scp install-logstash.sh ubuntu@$FIRST_NODE:/tmp/
ssh ubuntu@$FIRST_NODE "sudo bash /tmp/install-logstash.sh localhost $ELASTIC_PASSWORD"
```

#### Phase 2: Data Collection Setup (2-3 hours)

**Step 2.1: Deploy Beats to Windows Endpoints**

```powershell
# File: install-winlogbeat.ps1
# PowerShell script for Windows endpoints

$ElasticsearchHost = "10.0.1.10"  # Your Elasticsearch IP
$ElasticPassword = "your-password"  # From elastic-password.txt

# Download Winlogbeat
$WinlogbeatVersion = "8.11.0"
$DownloadUrl = "https://artifacts.elastic.co/downloads/beats/winlogbeat/winlogbeat-$WinlogbeatVersion-windows-x86_64.zip"
$DownloadPath = "$env:TEMP\winlogbeat.zip"

Write-Host "Downloading Winlogbeat..."
Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $DownloadUrl -OutFile $DownloadPath

# Extract
Expand-Archive -Path $DownloadPath -DestinationPath "C:\Program Files\" -Force
Rename-Item "C:\Program Files\winlogbeat-$WinlogbeatVersion-windows-x86_64" "C:\Program Files\Winlogbeat"

# Configure
$ConfigContent = @"
winlogbeat.event_logs:
  - name: Application
    ignore_older: 72h
  - name: System
  - name: Security
    processors:
      - script:
          lang: javascript
          id: security
          file: `${path.home}/module/security/config/winlogbeat-security.js
  - name: Microsoft-Windows-Sysmon/Operational

# Processors
processors:
  - add_host_metadata:
      when.not.contains.tags: forwarded
  - add_cloud_metadata: ~

# Output to Logstash
output.logstash:
  hosts: ["${ElasticsearchHost}:5044"]

# Logging
logging.level: info
logging.to_files: true
logging.files:
  path: C:/ProgramData/winlogbeat/Logs
"@

$ConfigContent | Out-File -FilePath "C:\Program Files\Winlogbeat\winlogbeat.yml" -Encoding UTF8

# Install as service
Set-Location "C:\Program Files\Winlogbeat"
.\install-service-winlogbeat.ps1

# Start service
Start-Service winlogbeat

Write-Host "Winlogbeat installed and started!"
```

**Deploy via GPO or SCCM to all Windows endpoints**

**Step 2.2: Deploy Filebeat to Linux Servers**

```bash
# File: install-filebeat.sh
#!/bin/bash

LOGSTASH_HOST="10.0.1.10:5044"

# Download and install Filebeat
curl -L -O https://artifacts.elastic.co/downloads/beats/filebeat/filebeat-8.11.0-amd64.deb
dpkg -i filebeat-8.11.0-amd64.deb

# Configure Filebeat
cat > /etc/filebeat/filebeat.yml << EOF
filebeat.inputs:
  - type: log
    enabled: true
    paths:
      - /var/log/syslog
      - /var/log/auth.log
    fields:
      log_type: syslog

  - type: log
    enabled: true
    paths:
      - /var/log/apache2/*.log
      - /var/log/nginx/*.log
    fields:
      log_type: web

# Modules
filebeat.modules:
  - module: system
    syslog:
      enabled: true
    auth:
      enabled: true

# Processors
processors:
  - add_host_metadata: ~
  - add_cloud_metadata: ~

# Output
output.logstash:
  hosts: ["${LOGSTASH_HOST}"]

# Logging
logging.level: info
EOF

# Enable and start Filebeat
systemctl enable filebeat
systemctl start filebeat

echo "Filebeat installed!"
```

**Step 2.3: Deploy Packetbeat for Network Traffic**

```bash
# File: install-packetbeat.sh
#!/bin/bash

LOGSTASH_HOST="10.0.1.10:5044"

# Install Packetbeat
curl -L -O https://artifacts.elastic.co/downloads/beats/packetbeat/packetbeat-8.11.0-amd64.deb
dpkg -i packetbeat-8.11.0-amd64.deb

# Configure Packetbeat
cat > /etc/packetbeat/packetbeat.yml << EOF
packetbeat.interfaces.device: any

packetbeat.protocols:
  - type: dns
    ports: [53]
  - type: http
    ports: [80, 8080, 8000, 5000, 8002]
  - type: tls
    ports: [443, 993, 995, 5223, 8443]
  - type: mysql
    ports: [3306]

packetbeat.flows:
  timeout: 30s
  period: 10s

processors:
  - add_cloud_metadata: ~

output.logstash:
  hosts: ["${LOGSTASH_HOST}"]

logging.level: info
EOF

# Enable and start
systemctl enable packetbeat
systemctl start packetbeat

echo "Packetbeat installed!"
```

**Step 3.2: Create Custom Detection Rule Script**

```python
# File: create-detection-rules.py
# Automate creation of detection rules

import requests
import json
from datetime import datetime

# Configuration
KIBANA_URL = "http://10.0.1.10:5601"
ELASTIC_USER = "elastic"
ELASTIC_PASSWORD = "your-password"

# Detection rules library
DETECTION_RULES = [
    {
        "name": "Multiple RDP Connections from Single Source",
        "description": "Detects potential RDP brute force or lateral movement",
        "query": "event.code:4624 AND winlog.logon.type:10",
        "threshold_field": "source.ip",
        "threshold_value": 5,
        "timeframe": "10m",
        "severity": "medium",
        "risk_score": 60,
        "mitre": ["T1021.001"]
    },
    {
        "name": "Account Lockouts",
        "description": "Detects multiple account lockouts (brute force indicator)",
        "query": "event.code:4740",
        "threshold_field": "user.name",
        "threshold_value": 3,
        "timeframe": "5m",
        "severity": "high",
        "risk_score": 70,
        "mitre": ["T1110"]
    },
    {
        "name": "Mimikatz Execution",
        "description": "Detects potential Mimikatz credential dumping",
        "query": 'process.name:mimikatz.exe OR process.args:(*sekurlsa* OR *lsadump*)',
        "severity": "critical",
        "risk_score": 95,
        "mitre": ["T1003.001"]
    },
    {
        "name": "Suspicious Service Installation",
        "description": "Detects installation of potentially malicious services",
        "query": "event.code:7045 AND winlog.event_data.ServiceName:(*powershell* OR *cmd* OR *vssadmin*)",
        "severity": "high",
        "risk_score": 75,
        "mitre": ["T1543.003"]
    },
    {
        "name": "Ransomware File Extension Changes",
        "description": "Detects mass file renames with ransomware extensions",
        "query": "file.extension:(.encrypted OR .locky OR .cerber OR .zepto OR .odin OR .thor OR .wallet)",
        "threshold_field": "host.name",
        "threshold_value": 50,
        "timeframe": "1m",
        "severity": "critical",
        "risk_score": 99,
        "mitre": ["T1486"]
    },
    {
        "name": "New Local Admin Account Created",
        "description": "Detects creation of new local administrator accounts",
        "query": "event.code:4720 AND group.name:*Administrators*",
        "severity": "high",
        "risk_score": 80,
        "mitre": ["T1136.001"]
    },
    {
        "name": "DCSync Attack",
        "description": "Detects potential DCSync credential theft attack",
        "query": "event.code:4662 AND winlog.event_data.Properties:(*1131f6aa* OR *1131f6ad* OR *89e95b76*)",
        "severity": "critical",
        "risk_score": 95,
        "mitre": ["T1003.006"]
    },
    {
        "name": "Suspicious DNS Queries",
        "description": "Detects DNS queries to suspicious TLDs or long domains",
        "query": "dns.question.name:(*.top OR *.xyz OR *.tk) OR dns.question.name.length > 50",
        "severity": "medium",
        "risk_score": 50,
        "mitre": ["T1071.004"]
    },
    {
        "name": "SQL Injection Attempt",
        "description": "Detects potential SQL injection in web requests",
        "query": "url.original:(*UNION*SELECT* OR *OR*1=1* OR *';DROP*TABLE* OR *exec(*sp_*)",
        "severity": "high",
        "risk_score": 75,
        "mitre": ["T1190"]
    },
    {
        "name": "Privilege Escalation via Scheduled Task",
        "description": "Detects creation of scheduled tasks for privilege escalation",
        "query": "event.code:4698 AND winlog.event_data.TaskName:(*SYSTEM* OR *Admin*)",
        "severity": "medium",
        "risk_score": 65,
        "mitre": ["T1053.005"]
    }
]

def create_detection_rule(rule_data):
    """Create a detection rule in Kibana"""
    
    url = f"{KIBANA_URL}/api/detection_engine/rules"
    headers = {
        "kbn-xsrf": "true",
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
    }
    
    # Build rule payload
    payload = {
        "name": rule_data["name"],
        "description": rule_data["description"],
        "enabled": True,
        "interval": "1m",
        "from": "now-6m",
        "to": "now",
        "severity": rule_data["severity"],
        "risk_score": rule_data["risk_score"],
        "author": ["SOC Team"],
        "tags": ["auto-generated"],
        "references": [],
        "false_positives": [],
        "threat": []
    }
    
    # Add MITRE ATT&CK techniques
    if "mitre" in rule_data:
        payload["threat"] = [
            {
                "framework": "MITRE ATT&CK",
                "technique": [{"id": tid} for tid in rule_data["mitre"]]
            }
        ]
    
    # Determine rule type
    if "threshold_field" in rule_data:
        payload["type"] = "threshold"
        payload["query"] = rule_data["query"]
        payload["threshold"] = {
            "field": rule_data["threshold_field"],
            "value": rule_data["threshold_value"]
        }
        payload["timeframe"] = rule_data.get("timeframe", "5m")
    else:
        payload["type"] = "query"
        payload["query"] = rule_data["query"]
        payload["language"] = "kuery"
    
    # Create rule
    response = requests.post(
        url,
        headers=headers,
        auth=(ELASTIC_USER, ELASTIC_PASSWORD),
        json=payload,
        verify=False
    )
    
    if response.status_code == 200:
        print(f"✓ Created rule: {rule_data['name']}")
        return True
    else:
        print(f"✗ Failed to create rule: {rule_data['name']}")
        print(f"  Error: {response.text}")
        return False

def main():
    """Create all detection rules"""
    
    print(f"Creating {len(DETECTION_RULES)} detection rules...")
    
    success_count = 0
    for rule in DETECTION_RULES:
        if create_detection_rule(rule):
            success_count += 1
    
    print(f"\nCompleted: {success_count}/{len(DETECTION_RULES)} rules created")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

**Run the script:**

```bash
python3 create-detection-rules.py
```

#### Phase 4: Threat Hunting & Analysis (2-3 hours)

**Step 4.1: Create Threat Hunting Workflows**

```python
# File: threat-hunting-framework.py
"""
Systematic threat hunting framework for SOC analysts
"""

import requests
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

class ThreatHunter:
    """
    Framework for proactive threat hunting in Elasticsearch
    """
    
    def __init__(self, es_host, es_user, es_password):
        self.es_host = es_host
        self.auth = (es_user, es_password)
        
    def hunt_persistence_mechanisms(self):
        """
        Hunt for persistence techniques (MITRE T1547, T1053, etc.)
        """
        
        hunts = {
            'registry_run_keys': {
                'name': 'Registry Run Key Persistence',
                'mitre': 'T1547.001',
                'query': '''
                    event.code:13 AND 
                    registry.path:(*\\Run* OR *\\RunOnce* OR *\\RunServices*) AND
                    NOT process.executable.keyword:("C:\\Windows\\*" OR "C:\\Program Files\\*")
                ''',
                'severity': 'high',
                'expected_results': 'Should be minimal - investigate all hits'
            },
            
            'scheduled_tasks': {
                'name': 'Suspicious Scheduled Task Creation',
                'mitre': 'T1053.005',
                'query': '''
                    event.code:4698 AND
                    (winlog.event_data.TaskName:*SYSTEM* OR 
                     winlog.event_data.TaskContent:*powershell* OR
                     winlog.event_data.TaskContent:*wscript*)
                ''',
                'severity': 'medium',
                'expected_results': 'Legitimate tasks exist, look for unusual patterns'
            },
            
            'wmi_persistence': {
                'name': 'WMI Event Subscription',
                'mitre': 'T1546.003',
                'query': '''
                    event.code:(5857 OR 5858 OR 5859 OR 5860 OR 5861) OR
                    process.command_line:*EventConsumer*
                ''',
                'severity': 'high',
                'expected_results': 'Very rare - investigate all hits'
            },
            
            'service_creation': {
                'name': 'New Service Installation',
                'mitre': 'T1543.003',
                'query': '''
                    event.code:7045 AND
                    NOT winlog.event_data.ServiceFileName.keyword:("C:\\Windows\\*")
                ''',
                'severity': 'medium',
                'expected_results': 'Software installs create services - look for suspicious paths'
            }
        }
        
        return self.execute_hunts(hunts)
    
    def hunt_credential_access(self):
        """
        Hunt for credential theft attempts
        """
        
        hunts = {
            'lsass_access': {
                'name': 'LSASS Memory Access',
                'mitre': 'T1003.001',
                'query': '''
                    event.code:10 AND
                    winlog.event_data.TargetImage:*lsass.exe* AND
                    NOT process.executable.keyword:("C:\\Windows\\System32\\*" OR 
                                                     "C:\\Program Files\\*")
                ''',
                'severity': 'critical',
                'expected_results': 'Very limited legitimate access - investigate all'
            },
            
            'credential_dumping': {
                'name': 'Credential Dumping Tools',
                'mitre': 'T1003',
                'query': '''
                    process.name:(mimikatz.exe OR procdump*.exe OR pwdump*.exe) OR
                    process.command_line:(*sekurlsa* OR *lsadump* OR *procdump*lsass*)
                ''',
                'severity': 'critical',
                'expected_results': 'Should be zero - immediate investigation'
            },
            
            'ntds_dit_access': {
                'name': 'NTDS.dit Database Access',
                'mitre': 'T1003.003',
                'query': '''
                    file.path:*NTDS.dit* OR
                    process.command_line:*ntds.dit*
                ''',
                'severity': 'critical',
                'expected_results': 'Only domain controllers - investigate any workstation access'
            },
            
            'sam_registry_access': {
                'name': 'SAM Registry Dump',
                'mitre': 'T1003.002',
                'query': '''
                    registry.path:(*SAM\\SAM\\Domains\\Account\\Users*)
                ''',
                'severity': 'high',
                'expected_results': 'System processes only - investigate user processes'
            }
        }
        
        return self.execute_hunts(hunts)
    
    def hunt_lateral_movement(self):
        """
        Hunt for lateral movement activities
        """
        
        hunts = {
            'remote_execution': {
                'name': 'Remote Service Execution',
                'mitre': 'T1569.002',
                'query': '''
                    event.code:7045 AND
                    winlog.event_data.ServiceFileName:(*\\\\127.0.0.1\\* OR *\\\\localhost\\*)
                ''',
                'severity': 'high',
                'expected_results': 'Should be rare - legitimate remote admin exists'
            },
            
            'psexec_usage': {
                'name': 'PsExec or Similar Tool Usage',
                'mitre': 'T1021.002',
                'query': '''
                    (process.name:psexec*.exe OR 
                     process.name:paexec.exe OR
                     file.name:PSEXESVC.exe)
                ''',
                'severity': 'medium',
                'expected_results': 'IT may use legitimately - verify with IT'
            },
            
            'admin_share_access': {
                'name': 'Administrative Share Access',
                'mitre': 'T1021.002',
                'query': '''
                    event.code:5140 AND
                    winlog.event_data.ShareName:(*C$ OR *ADMIN$ OR *IPC$)
                ''',
                'severity': 'low',
                'expected_results': 'Common for IT - look for unusual patterns'
            },
            
            'remote_desktop': {
                'name': 'Unusual RDP Sessions',
                'mitre': 'T1021.001',
                'query': '''
                    event.code:4624 AND
                    winlog.logon.type:10 AND
                    NOT source.ip:(10.0.0.0/8 OR 172.16.0.0/12 OR 192.168.0.0/16)
                ''',
                'severity': 'medium',
                'expected_results': 'External RDP should be blocked - investigate all'
            }
        }
        
        return self.execute_hunts(hunts)
    
    def hunt_data_exfiltration(self):
        """
        Hunt for data exfiltration indicators
        """
        
        hunts = {
            'large_uploads': {
                'name': 'Unusually Large Data Uploads',
                'query': '''
                    network.direction:outbound AND
                    network.bytes > 100000000 AND
                    NOT destination.ip:(10.0.0.0/8 OR 172.16.0.0/12 OR 192.168.0.0/16)
                ''',
                'severity': 'high',
                'expected_results': 'Backups may trigger - verify legitimate activity'
            },
            
            'cloud_storage_uploads': {
                'name': 'Uploads to Cloud Storage',
                'mitre': 'T1567.002',
                'query': '''
                    url.domain:(*.dropbox.com OR *.box.com OR *.drive.google.com OR 
                               *.onedrive.com OR *wetransfer.com OR *mega.nz) AND
                    http.request.method:POST
                ''',
                'severity': 'medium',
                'expected_results': 'Legitimate use exists - look for sensitive data'
            },
            
            'dns_tunneling': {
                'name': 'Potential DNS Tunneling',
                'mitre': 'T1071.004',
                'query': '''
                    dns.question.name.length > 50 OR
                    (dns.question.type:TXT AND dns.question.name.length > 30)
                ''',
                'severity': 'high',
                'expected_results': 'Should be rare - investigate long/unusual queries'
            },
            
            'archive_creation': {
                'name': 'Archive File Creation Before Upload',
                'mitre': 'T1560.001',
                'query': '''
                    (file.extension:(zip OR rar OR 7z OR tar.gz) AND
                     file.size > 10000000) AND
                    event.action:creation
                ''',
                'severity': 'low',
                'expected_results': 'Common activity - correlate with uploads'
            }
        }
        
        return self.execute_hunts(hunts)
    
    def execute_hunts(self, hunts):
        """
        Execute threat hunting queries against Elasticsearch
        """
        
        results = []
        
        for hunt_id, hunt_config in hunts.items():
            print(f"\n[*] Executing hunt: {hunt_config['name']}")
            
            # Build Elasticsearch query
            query = {
                "query": {
                    "bool": {
                        "must": [
                            {
                                "query_string": {
                                    "query": hunt_config['query']
                                }
                            },
                            {
                                "range": {
                                    "@timestamp": {
                                        "gte": "now-24h",
                                        "lte": "now"
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        ]
                    }
                },
                "size": 100,
                "sort": [{"@timestamp": "desc"}]
            }
            
            # Execute search
            try:
                response = requests.post(
                    f"{self.es_host}/logs-*/_search",
                    auth=self.auth,
                    json=query,
                    verify=False
                )
                
                if response.status_code == 200:
                    data = response.json()
                    hit_count = data['hits']['total']['value']
                    
                    result = {
                        'hunt_id': hunt_id,
                        'hunt_name': hunt_config['name'],
                        'mitre_technique': hunt_config.get('mitre', 'N/A'),
                        'severity': hunt_config['severity'],
                        'hit_count': hit_count,
                        'hits': data['hits']['hits'][:10]  # Top 10
                    }
                    
                    results.append(result)
                    
                    print(f"    ├─ Found {hit_count} results")
                    print(f"    ├─ Severity: {hunt_config['severity']}")
                    print(f"    └─ Expected: {hunt_config['expected_results']}")
                    
                    if hit_count > 0:
                        print(f"    ⚠ INVESTIGATE: Review all {hit_count} events")
                else:
                    print(f"    ✗ Query failed: {response.status_code}")
                    
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"    ✗ Error: {str(e)}")
        
        return results
    
    def generate_hunt_report(self, results):
        """
        Generate threat hunting report
        """
        
        report = {
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
            'total_hunts': len(results),
            'findings': [],
            'summary': {
                'critical': 0,
                'high': 0,
                'medium': 0,
                'low': 0
            }
        }
        
        for result in results:
            if result['hit_count'] > 0:
                report['findings'].append(result)
                report['summary'][result['severity']] += result['hit_count']
        
        # Save report
        report_file = f"threat-hunt-report-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')}.json"
        with open(report_file, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(report, f, indent=2)
        
        print(f"\n[+] Report saved to {report_file}")
        return report

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    hunter = ThreatHunter(
        es_host="https://10.0.1.10:9200",
        es_user="elastic",
        es_password="your-password"
    )
    
    print("=== THREAT HUNTING SESSION ===\n")
    
    # Execute hunts
    persistence_results = hunter.hunt_persistence_mechanisms()
    credential_results = hunter.hunt_credential_access()
    lateral_results = hunter.hunt_lateral_movement()
    exfil_results = hunter.hunt_data_exfiltration()
    
    # Combine and report
    all_results = (persistence_results + credential_results + 
                   lateral_results + exfil_results)
    
    report = hunter.generate_hunt_report(all_results)
    
    # Print summary
    print("\n=== HUNT SUMMARY ===")
    print(f"Total Hunts: {report['total_hunts']}")
    print(f"Findings: {len(report['findings'])}")
    print(f"  Critical: {report['summary']['critical']}")
    print(f"  High: {report['summary']['high']}")
    print(f"  Medium: {report['summary']['medium']}")
    print(f"  Low: {report['summary']['low']}")
```

**Step 4.2: Create Incident Response Playbooks**

```yaml
# File: playbooks/phishing-response.yaml
# Incident Response Playbook for Phishing

name: "Phishing Email Response"
version: "1.0"
severity: "Medium to Critical"
mitre_attack: ["T1566.001", "T1566.002"]

trigger:
  - User reports suspicious email
  - Email security alerts on malicious content
  - Multiple similar emails detected

phases:
  - phase: "Triage"
    time_limit: "15 minutes"
    steps:
      - step: "Verify the report"
        actions:
          - "Review reported email in quarantine"
          - "Check email headers for authenticity"
          - "Analyze URLs and attachments (sandbox)"
        
      - step: "Determine severity"
        criteria:
          critical: "Credential harvesting + confirmed clicks"
          high: "Malware delivery + potential execution"
          medium: "Suspicious but no confirmed compromise"
          low: "Spam with no malicious content"
        
      - step: "Initial containment"
        actions:
          - "If critical/high: Block sender domain immediately"
          - "Search for similar emails across organization"
          - "Quarantine all matching emails"

  - phase: "Investigation"
    time_limit: "1 hour"
    steps:
      - step: "Identify affected users"
        actions:
          - "Query email logs for delivery"
          - "Identify who opened/clicked"
          - "Prioritize by user role (executives, finance, IT)"
        queries:
          - elasticsearch: |
              event.category:email AND 
              email.subject:"{{phishing_subject}}" AND
              event.action:delivered
        
      - step: "Analyze malicious content"
        actions:
          - "Submit URLs to VirusTotal, URLhaus"
          - "Detonate attachments in sandbox (Any.run, Joe Sandbox)"
          - "Identify IOCs (domains, IPs, file hashes)"
        
      - step: "Check for compromise"
        actions:
          - "Search logs for IOC hits"
          - "Check authentication logs for affected users"
          - "Look for abnormal account activity"
        queries:
          - elasticsearch: |
              (destination.domain:{{malicious_domain}} OR
               destination.ip:{{malicious_ip}} OR
               file.hash.sha256:{{malicious_hash}})

  - phase: "Containment"
    time_limit: "30 minutes"
    steps:
      - step: "Block infrastructure"
        actions:
          - "Block malicious domains/IPs at firewall"
          - "Create email filter rule to block sender"
          - "Update proxy blacklist"
        
      - step: "Isolate compromised systems"
        actions:
          - "If malware detected: Isolate endpoints via EDR"
          - "If credentials stolen: Reset passwords immediately"
          - "Revoke active sessions for affected users"

  - phase: "Eradication"
    time_limit: "2 hours"
    steps:
      - step: "Remove malicious content"
        actions:
          - "Delete/quarantine all phishing emails"
          - "Remove malware from infected systems"
          - "Clear browser caches if credential theft"
        
      - step: "Patch vulnerabilities"
        actions:
          - "If exploit used: Patch affected software"
          - "Update email security rules"
          - "Enhance detection signatures"

  - phase: "Recovery"
    time_limit: "4 hours"
    steps:
      - step: "Restore normal operations"
        actions:
          - "Un-isolate cleaned endpoints"
          - "Verify systems are malware-free"
          - "Monitor for 24 hours post-recovery"
        
      - step: "Reset credentials"
        actions:
          - "Force password reset for affected users"
          - "Review and revoke suspicious MFA enrollments"
          - "Monitor for account takeover attempts"

  - phase: "Post-Incident"
    time_limit: "1 week"
    steps:
      - step: "Lessons learned"
        actions:
          - "Conduct post-incident review meeting"
          - "Document what worked and what didn't"
          - "Update playbook based on findings"
        
      - step: "User education"
        actions:
          - "Send targeted training to affected users"
          - "Organization-wide phishing awareness campaign"
          - "Update phishing simulation scenarios"
        
      - step: "Improve defenses"
        actions:
          - "Enhance email security rules"
          - "Implement additional detections"
          - "Review and improve user reporting process"

communication:
  - notify_immediately:
      - SOC Manager
      - Affected users
    
  - notify_within_1_hour:
      - CISO
      - IT Leadership
      - Legal (if data breach)
    
  - notify_within_24_hours:
      - Executives (if critical)
      - Board (if major breach)
      - Regulators (if required by law)

documentation:
  - Incident ticket with all details
  - Timeline of actions taken
  - IOCs collected
  - Users affected
  - Remediation steps
  - Lessons learned report

metrics:
  - Time to detection
  - Time to containment
  - Number of users affected
  - Number of users who clicked
  - IOCs identified
  - Similar incidents prevented
```

#### Phase 6: Testing & Validation (2 hours)

**Step 6.1: Generate Test Data**

```python
# File: generate-test-alerts.py
"""
Generate test security events for SOC validation
"""

import requests
import json
from datetime import datetime
import random

class TestDataGenerator:
    """Generate realistic test security events"""
    
    def __init__(self, logstash_host):
        self.logstash_host = logstash_host
        
    def generate_failed_login(self):
        """Simulate failed login attempts"""
        
        event = {
            "@timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "event": {
                "category": "authentication",
                "outcome": "failure",
                "code": "4625"
            },
            "source": {
                "ip": f"203.{random.randint(0,255)}.{random.randint(0,255)}.{random.randint(0,255)}"
            },
            "user": {
                "name": random.choice(["admin", "administrator", "root", "test"])
            },
            "host": {
                "name": "web-server-01"
            }
        }
        
        return event
    
    def generate_malware_detection(self):
        """Simulate malware detection"""
        
        malware_names = ["Mimikatz", "Emotet", "TrickBot", "Cobalt Strike"]
        
        event = {
            "@timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "event": {
                "category": "malware",
                "action": "detected",
                "severity": "critical"
            },
            "file": {
                "name": f"{random.choice(malware_names).lower()}.exe",
                "hash": {
                    "sha256": "a" * 64  # Fake hash
                }
            },
            "host": {
                "name": f"workstation-{random.randint(1,100):03d}"
            },
            "process": {
                "name": "powershell.exe",
                "args": ["-encodedcommand", "AAAA"]
            }
        }
        
        return event
    
    def send_event(self, event):
        """Send event to Logstash"""
        
        try:
            response = requests.post(
                f"http://{self.logstash_host}:8080",
                json=event
            )
            return response.status_code == 200
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error sending event: {e}")
            return False

# Generate test events
generator = TestDataGenerator("10.0.1.10")

print("Generating test events...")

# 100 failed logins
for i in range(100):
    event = generator.generate_failed_login()
    generator.send_event(event)

# 10 malware detections
for i in range(10):
    event = generator.generate_malware_detection()
    generator.send_event(event)

print("Test events generated!")
```

#### Phase 7: Production Hardening (2-3 hours)

**Step 7.1: Security Hardening**

```bash
# File: harden-soc.sh
#!/bin/bash

echo "Hardening SOC infrastructure..."

# 1. Enable firewall
ufw enable
ufw default deny incoming
ufw default allow outgoing
ufw allow 22/tcp  # SSH
ufw allow 5601/tcp  # Kibana
ufw allow 9200/tcp  # Elasticsearch
ufw allow 5044/tcp  # Logstash Beats

# 2. Disable unnecessary services
systemctl disable bluetooth
systemctl disable cups
systemctl stop bluetooth cups

# 3. Configure automatic updates
apt-get install -y unattended-upgrades
dpkg-reconfigure -plow unattended-upgrades

# 4. Harden SSH
cat >> /etc/ssh/sshd_config << EOF
PermitRootLogin no
PasswordAuthentication no
PubkeyAuthentication yes
MaxAuthTries 3
ClientAliveInterval 300
ClientAliveCountMax 2
EOF

systemctl restart sshd

# 5. Install fail2ban
apt-get install -y fail2ban

cat > /etc/fail2ban/jail.local << EOF
[DEFAULT]
bantime = 3600
findtime = 600
maxretry = 5

[sshd]
enabled = true
EOF

systemctl enable fail2ban
systemctl start fail2ban

# 6. Configure log rotation
cat > /etc/logrotate.d/elasticsearch << EOF
/var/log/elasticsearch/*.log {
    daily
    rotate 7
    compress
    delaycompress
    missingok
    notifempty
}
EOF

echo "SOC hardening complete!"
```

**Step 7.2: Performance Tuning**

```bash
# File: tune-elasticsearch.sh
#!/bin/bash

# Elasticsearch performance tuning

# 1. Disable swapping
swapoff -a
# Edit /etc/fstab and comment out swap

# 2. Increase file descriptors
cat >> /etc/security/limits.conf << EOF
elasticsearch soft nofile 65536
elasticsearch hard nofile 65536
elasticsearch soft memlock unlimited
elasticsearch hard memlock unlimited
EOF

# 3. Tune kernel parameters
cat >> /etc/sysctl.conf << EOF
vm.max_map_count=262144
vm.swappiness=1
net.core.somaxconn=65535
EOF

sysctl -p

# 4. Configure index lifecycle management
curl -X PUT "https://localhost:9200/_ilm/policy/logs-policy" \
  -u elastic:password \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d'
{
  "policy": {
    "phases": {
      "hot": {
        "actions": {
          "rollover": {
            "max_size": "50GB",
            "max_age": "1d"
          }
        }
      },
      "warm": {
        "min_age": "7d",
        "actions": {
          "shrink": {
            "number_of_shards": 1
          },
          "forcemerge": {
            "max_num_segments": 1
          }
        }
      },
      "cold": {
        "min_age": "30d",
        "actions": {
          "freeze": {}
        }
      },
      "delete": {
        "min_age": "90d",
        "actions": {
          "delete": {}
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
'

echo "Performance tuning complete!"
```

### Architecture Overview

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    AWS ORGANIZATIONS                             │
│                    (Root Account)                                │
└─────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┘
                          │
         ┌────────────────┼────────────────┬────────────────┐
         │                │                │                │
    ┌────▼─────┐    ┌────▼─────┐    ┌────▼─────┐    ┌────▼─────┐
    │ Security │    │  Logging │    │   Dev    │    │   Prod   │
    │ Account  │    │ Account  │    │ Account  │    │ Account  │
    └────┬─────┘    └────┬─────┘    └────┬─────┘    └────┬─────┘
         │               │               │               │
    ┌────▼──────────┐   │          ┌────▼─────┐    ┌────▼─────┐
    │ Security Hub  │   │          │ VPC      │    │ VPC      │
    │ GuardDuty     │   │          │ (10.1/16)│    │ (10.2/16)│
    │ IAM Identity  │   │          └──────────┘    └──────────┘
    │ Center        │   │
    └───────────────┘   │
                   ┌────▼──────────┐
                   │ CloudTrail    │
                   │ Config        │
                   │ S3 Logging    │
                   └───────────────┘
```

**Account Structure:**

1. **Root/Management Account:**
   - AWS Organizations management
   - Consolidated billing
   - Service Control Policies (SCPs)
   - Minimal workloads (security risk)

2. **Security Account:**
   - Security Hub (aggregation)
   - GuardDuty (threat detection)
   - IAM Identity Center (SSO)
   - Security tooling

3. **Log Archive Account:**
   - CloudTrail logs (immutable)
   - Config history
   - VPC Flow Logs
   - S3 access logs
   - Long-term retention

4. **Development Account:**
   - Non-production workloads
   - Relaxed controls (but monitored)
   - Testing and staging

5. **Production Account:**
   - Production workloads
   - Strict security controls
   - High availability
   - Automated backups

### Phase 1: Multi-Account Setup (2-3 hours)

**Step 1.1: Initialize AWS Organizations**

```bash
# File: 01-organizations-setup.sh
#!/bin/bash

set -e

echo "Setting up AWS Organizations..."

# Ensure you're using root account
ACCOUNT_ID=$(aws sts get-caller-identity --query Account --output text)
echo "Current Account ID: $ACCOUNT_ID"
read -p "Is this your root account? (yes/no): " confirm

if [ "$confirm" != "yes" ]; then
    echo "Please run from root account"
    exit 1
fi

# Enable all AWS Organizations features
aws organizations enable-all-features

# Create Organizational Units (OUs)
echo "Creating Organizational Units..."

SECURITY_OU=$(aws organizations create-organizational-unit \
    --parent-id $(aws organizations list-roots --query 'Roots[0].Id' --output text) \
    --name Security \
    --query 'OrganizationalUnit.Id' \
    --output text)

WORKLOADS_OU=$(aws organizations create-organizational-unit \
    --parent-id $(aws organizations list-roots --query 'Roots[0].Id' --output text) \
    --name Workloads \
    --query 'OrganizationalUnit.Id' \
    --output text)

echo "Security OU ID: $SECURITY_OU"
echo "Workloads OU ID: $WORKLOADS_OU"

# Create accounts
echo "Creating member accounts..."

# Security Account
SECURITY_ACCOUNT=$(aws organizations create-account \
    --email "security@yourdomain.com" \
    --account-name "Security" \
    --query 'CreateAccountStatus.Id' \
    --output text)

# Log Archive Account
LOG_ACCOUNT=$(aws organizations create-account \
    --email "logs@yourdomain.com" \
    --account-name "Log Archive" \
    --query 'CreateAccountStatus.Id' \
    --output text)

# Development Account
DEV_ACCOUNT=$(aws organizations create-account \
    --email "dev@yourdomain.com" \
    --account-name "Development" \
    --query 'CreateAccountStatus.Id' \
    --output text)

# Production Account
PROD_ACCOUNT=$(aws organizations create-account \
    --email "prod@yourdomain.com" \
    --account-name "Production" \
    --query 'CreateAccountStatus.Id' \
    --output text)

echo "Account creation initiated"
echo "Security: $SECURITY_ACCOUNT"
echo "Logs: $LOG_ACCOUNT"
echo "Dev: $DEV_ACCOUNT"
echo "Prod: $PROD_ACCOUNT"

# Wait for accounts to be created (takes 2-5 minutes)
echo "Waiting for account creation to complete..."
sleep 300

# Get account IDs
SECURITY_ACCOUNT_ID=$(aws organizations describe-create-account-status \
    --create-account-request-id $SECURITY_ACCOUNT \
    --query 'CreateAccountStatus.AccountId' \
    --output text)

LOG_ACCOUNT_ID=$(aws organizations describe-create-account-status \
    --create-account-request-id $LOG_ACCOUNT \
    --query 'CreateAccountStatus.AccountId' \
    --output text)

DEV_ACCOUNT_ID=$(aws organizations describe-create-account-status \
    --create-account-request-id $DEV_ACCOUNT \
    --query 'CreateAccountStatus.AccountId' \
    --output text)

PROD_ACCOUNT_ID=$(aws organizations describe-create-account-status \
    --create-account-request-id $PROD_ACCOUNT \
    --query 'CreateAccountStatus.AccountId' \
    --output text)

# Move accounts to appropriate OUs
echo "Moving accounts to OUs..."
aws organizations move-account --account-id $SECURITY_ACCOUNT_ID --source-parent-id $(aws organizations list-roots --query 'Roots[0].Id' --output text) --destination-parent-id $SECURITY_OU
aws organizations move-account --account-id $LOG_ACCOUNT_ID --source-parent-id $(aws organizations list-roots --query 'Roots[0].Id' --output text) --destination-parent-id $SECURITY_OU
aws organizations move-account --account-id $DEV_ACCOUNT_ID --source-parent-id $(aws organizations list-roots --query 'Roots[0].Id' --output text) --destination-parent-id $WORKLOADS_OU
aws organizations move-account --account-id $PROD_ACCOUNT_ID --source-parent-id $(aws organizations list-roots --query 'Roots[0].Id' --output text) --destination-parent-id $WORKLOADS_OU

# Save account IDs to file
cat > accounts.env << EOF
export ROOT_ACCOUNT_ID=$ACCOUNT_ID
export SECURITY_ACCOUNT_ID=$SECURITY_ACCOUNT_ID
export LOG_ACCOUNT_ID=$LOG_ACCOUNT_ID
export DEV_ACCOUNT_ID=$DEV_ACCOUNT_ID
export PROD_ACCOUNT_ID=$PROD_ACCOUNT_ID
export SECURITY_OU=$SECURITY_OU
export WORKLOADS_OU=$WORKLOADS_OU
EOF

echo "Account setup complete!"
echo "Account IDs saved to accounts.env"
echo ""
echo "Next steps:"
echo "1. Access each account and enable MFA"
echo "2. Set up cross-account access roles"
echo "3. Configure Service Control Policies"
```

**Step 1.2: Service Control Policies (SCPs)**

```json
// File: scp-deny-root-usage.json
// Prevent root account usage in member accounts
{
  "Version": "2012-10-17",
  "Statement": [
    {
      "Sid": "DenyRootAccountUsage",
      "Effect": "Deny",
      "Action": "*",
      "Resource": "*",
      "Condition": {
        "StringLike": {
          "aws:PrincipalArn": "arn:aws:iam::*:root"
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
// File: scp-require-mfa.json
// Require MFA for sensitive operations
{
  "Version": "2012-10-17",
  "Statement": [
    {
      "Sid": "DenyAllExceptListedIfNoMFA",
      "Effect": "Deny",
      "NotAction": [
        "iam:CreateVirtualMFADevice",
        "iam:EnableMFADevice",
        "iam:GetUser",
        "iam:ListMFADevices",
        "iam:ListVirtualMFADevices",
        "iam:ResyncMFADevice",
        "sts:GetSessionToken"
      ],
      "Resource": "*",
      "Condition": {
        "BoolIfExists": {
          "aws:MultiFactorAuthPresent": "false"
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
// File: scp-deny-region-access.json
// Restrict to specific AWS regions
{
  "Version": "2012-10-17",
  "Statement": [
    {
      "Sid": "DenyAccessToUnauthorizedRegions",
      "Effect": "Deny",
      "NotAction": [
        "cloudfront:*",
        "iam:*",
        "route53:*",
        "support:*",
        "budgets:*"
      ],
      "Resource": "*",
      "Condition": {
        "StringNotEquals": {
          "aws:RequestedRegion": [
            "us-east-1",
            "us-west-2",
            "eu-west-1"
          ]
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
// File: scp-prevent-guardduty-disable.json
// Prevent disabling security services
{
  "Version": "2012-10-17",
  "Statement": [
    {
      "Sid": "PreventSecurityServiceDisable",
      "Effect": "Deny",
      "Action": [
        "guardduty:DeleteDetector",
        "guardduty:DisassociateFromMasterAccount",
        "guardduty:DisassociateMembers",
        "guardduty:StopMonitoringMembers",
        "securityhub:DisableSecurityHub",
        "securityhub:DisassociateFromMasterAccount",
        "config:DeleteConfigurationRecorder",
        "config:DeleteDeliveryChannel",
        "config:StopConfigurationRecorder",
        "cloudtrail:DeleteTrail",
        "cloudtrail:StopLogging"
      ],
      "Resource": "*"
    }
  ]
}
```

**Apply SCPs:**

```bash
# File: apply-scps.sh
#!/bin/bash

source accounts.env

echo "Creating and applying Service Control Policies..."

# Create policies
DENY_ROOT=$(aws organizations create-policy \
    --content file://scp-deny-root-usage.json \
    --name DenyRootUsage \
    --type SERVICE_CONTROL_POLICY \
    --description "Deny root account usage in member accounts" \
    --query 'Policy.PolicySummary.Id' \
    --output text)

REQUIRE_MFA=$(aws organizations create-policy \
    --content file://scp-require-mfa.json \
    --name RequireMFA \
    --type SERVICE_CONTROL_POLICY \
    --description "Require MFA for sensitive operations" \
    --query 'Policy.PolicySummary.Id' \
    --output text)

DENY_REGIONS=$(aws organizations create-policy \
    --content file://scp-deny-region-access.json \
    --name DenyRegionAccess \
    --type SERVICE_CONTROL_POLICY \
    --description "Restrict to approved regions" \
    --query 'Policy.PolicySummary.Id' \
    --output text)

PREVENT_DISABLE=$(aws organizations create-policy \
    --content file://scp-prevent-guardduty-disable.json \
    --name PreventSecurityDisable \
    --type SERVICE_CONTROL_POLICY \
    --description "Prevent disabling security services" \
    --query 'Policy.PolicySummary.Id' \
    --output text)

echo "Policies created:"
echo "  Deny Root: $DENY_ROOT"
echo "  Require MFA: $REQUIRE_MFA"
echo "  Deny Regions: $DENY_REGIONS"
echo "  Prevent Disable: $PREVENT_DISABLE"

# Attach to OUs
echo "Attaching policies to OUs..."

# Apply to all workload accounts
for policy in $DENY_ROOT $REQUIRE_MFA $DENY_REGIONS $PREVENT_DISABLE; do
    aws organizations attach-policy --policy-id $policy --target-id $WORKLOADS_OU
done

echo "SCPs applied successfully!"
```

---

### Phase 2: Network Architecture (3-4 hours)

**Step 2.1: Zero Trust Network Design**

```hcl
# File: terraform/network/main.tf
# Zero trust VPC architecture with micro-segmentation

terraform {
  required_version = ">= 1.0"
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source  = "hashicorp/aws"
      version = "~> 5.0"
    }
  }
}

# Production Account VPC
module "production_vpc" {
  source = "./modules/secure-vpc"
  
  vpc_cidr            = "10.2.0.0/16"
  vpc_name            = "production"
  availability_zones  = ["us-east-1a", "us-east-1b", "us-east-1c"]
  
  # Public subnets (ALB, NAT Gateway)
  public_subnets = [
    "10.2.0.0/24",
    "10.2.1.0/24",
    "10.2.2.0/24"
  ]
  
  # Private subnets (Application tier)
  private_subnets = [
    "10.2.10.0/24",
    "10.2.11.0/24",
    "10.2.12.0/24"
  ]
  
  # Database subnets (Data tier)
  database_subnets = [
    "10.2.20.0/24",
    "10.2.21.0/24",
    "10.2.22.0/24"
  ]
  
  # Enable security features
  enable_flow_logs        = true
  enable_network_firewall = true
  enable_vpc_endpoints    = true
  
  tags = {
    Environment = "production"
    ManagedBy   = "terraform"
    Compliance  = "soc2-iso27001"
  }
}

# VPC Module
# File: terraform/network/modules/secure-vpc/main.tf
resource "aws_vpc" "main" {
  cidr_block           = var.vpc_cidr
  enable_dns_hostnames = true
  enable_dns_support   = true
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-vpc"
    }
  )
}

# Internet Gateway (for public subnets)
resource "aws_internet_gateway" "main" {
  vpc_id = aws_vpc.main.id
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-igw"
    }
  )
}

# Public Subnets
resource "aws_subnet" "public" {
  count             = length(var.public_subnets)
  vpc_id            = aws_vpc.main.id
  cidr_block        = var.public_subnets[count.index]
  availability_zone = var.availability_zones[count.index]
  
  map_public_ip_on_launch = false  # Explicit assignment only
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-public-${var.availability_zones[count.index]}"
      Tier = "public"
    }
  )
}

# Private Subnets (Application tier)
resource "aws_subnet" "private" {
  count             = length(var.private_subnets)
  vpc_id            = aws_vpc.main.id
  cidr_block        = var.private_subnets[count.index]
  availability_zone = var.availability_zones[count.index]
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-private-${var.availability_zones[count.index]}"
      Tier = "private"
    }
  )
}

# Database Subnets (Data tier)
resource "aws_subnet" "database" {
  count             = length(var.database_subnets)
  vpc_id            = aws_vpc.main.id
  cidr_block        = var.database_subnets[count.index]
  availability_zone = var.availability_zones[count.index]
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-database-${var.availability_zones[count.index]}"
      Tier = "database"
    }
  )
}

# NAT Gateways (one per AZ for high availability)
resource "aws_eip" "nat" {
  count  = length(var.availability_zones)
  domain = "vpc"
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-nat-eip-${var.availability_zones[count.index]}"
    }
  )
}

resource "aws_nat_gateway" "main" {
  count         = length(var.availability_zones)
  allocation_id = aws_eip.nat[count.index].id
  subnet_id     = aws_subnet.public[count.index].id
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-nat-${var.availability_zones[count.index]}"
    }
  )
  
  depends_on = [aws_internet_gateway.main]
}

# Route Tables
resource "aws_route_table" "public" {
  vpc_id = aws_vpc.main.id
  
  route {
    cidr_block = "0.0.0.0/0"
    gateway_id = aws_internet_gateway.main.id
  }
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-public-rt"
    }
  )
}

resource "aws_route_table" "private" {
  count  = length(var.availability_zones)
  vpc_id = aws_vpc.main.id
  
  route {
    cidr_block     = "0.0.0.0/0"
    nat_gateway_id = aws_nat_gateway.main[count.index].id
  }
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-private-rt-${var.availability_zones[count.index]}"
    }
  )
}

resource "aws_route_table" "database" {
  vpc_id = aws_vpc.main.id
  
  # No internet route - completely isolated
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-database-rt"
    }
  )
}

# Route Table Associations
resource "aws_route_table_association" "public" {
  count          = length(var.public_subnets)
  subnet_id      = aws_subnet.public[count.index].id
  route_table_id = aws_route_table.public.id
}

resource "aws_route_table_association" "private" {
  count          = length(var.private_subnets)
  subnet_id      = aws_subnet.private[count.index].id
  route_table_id = aws_route_table.private[count.index].id
}

resource "aws_route_table_association" "database" {
  count          = length(var.database_subnets)
  subnet_id      = aws_subnet.database[count.index].id
  route_table_id = aws_route_table.database.id
}

# VPC Flow Logs
resource "aws_flow_log" "main" {
  count = var.enable_flow_logs ? 1 : 0
  
  vpc_id          = aws_vpc.main.id
  traffic_type    = "ALL"
  iam_role_arn    = aws_iam_role.flow_logs[0].arn
  log_destination = aws_cloudwatch_log_group.flow_logs[0].arn
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-flow-logs"
    }
  )
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_log_group" "flow_logs" {
  count             = var.enable_flow_logs ? 1 : 0
  name              = "/aws/vpc/${var.vpc_name}/flow-logs"
  retention_in_days = 90
  
  tags = var.tags
}

resource "aws_iam_role" "flow_logs" {
  count = var.enable_flow_logs ? 1 : 0
  name  = "${var.vpc_name}-flow-logs-role"
  
  assume_role_policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [
      {
        Action = "sts:AssumeRole"
        Effect = "Allow"
        Principal = {
          Service = "vpc-flow-logs.amazonaws.com"
        }
      }
    ]
  })
  
  tags = var.tags
}

resource "aws_iam_role_policy" "flow_logs" {
  count = var.enable_flow_logs ? 1 : 0
  role  = aws_iam_role.flow_logs[0].id
  
  policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [
      {
        Action = [
          "logs:CreateLogGroup",
          "logs:CreateLogStream",
          "logs:PutLogEvents",
          "logs:DescribeLogGroups",
          "logs:DescribeLogStreams"
        ]
        Effect   = "Allow"
        Resource = "*"
      }
    ]
  })
}

# VPC Endpoints (PrivateLink)
resource "aws_vpc_endpoint" "s3" {
  count        = var.enable_vpc_endpoints ? 1 : 0
  vpc_id       = aws_vpc.main.id
  service_name = "com.amazonaws.${data.aws_region.current.name}.s3"
  
  route_table_ids = concat(
    [aws_route_table.public.id],
    aws_route_table.private[*].id,
    [aws_route_table.database.id]
  )
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-s3-endpoint"
    }
  )
}

resource "aws_vpc_endpoint" "dynamodb" {
  count        = var.enable_vpc_endpoints ? 1 : 0
  vpc_id       = aws_vpc.main.id
  service_name = "com.amazonaws.${data.aws_region.current.name}.dynamodb"
  
  route_table_ids = concat(
    [aws_route_table.public.id],
    aws_route_table.private[*].id,
    [aws_route_table.database.id]
  )
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-dynamodb-endpoint"
    }
  )
}

# Interface endpoints for AWS services
resource "aws_vpc_endpoint" "ec2" {
  count               = var.enable_vpc_endpoints ? 1 : 0
  vpc_id              = aws_vpc.main.id
  service_name        = "com.amazonaws.${data.aws_region.current.name}.ec2"
  vpc_endpoint_type   = "Interface"
  subnet_ids          = aws_subnet.private[*].id
  security_group_ids  = [aws_security_group.vpc_endpoints[0].id]
  private_dns_enabled = true
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-ec2-endpoint"
    }
  )
}

# Security Group for VPC Endpoints
resource "aws_security_group" "vpc_endpoints" {
  count       = var.enable_vpc_endpoints ? 1 : 0
  name        = "${var.vpc_name}-vpc-endpoints-sg"
  description = "Security group for VPC endpoints"
  vpc_id      = aws_vpc.main.id
  
  ingress {
    from_port   = 443
    to_port     = 443
    protocol    = "tcp"
    cidr_blocks = [var.vpc_cidr]
    description = "HTTPS from VPC"
  }
  
  egress {
    from_port   = 0
    to_port     = 0
    protocol    = "-1"
    cidr_blocks = ["0.0.0.0/0"]
    description = "Allow all outbound"
  }
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-vpc-endpoints-sg"
    }
  )
}

# Network ACLs (Defense in depth)
resource "aws_network_acl" "public" {
  vpc_id     = aws_vpc.main.id
  subnet_ids = aws_subnet.public[*].id
  
  # Allow HTTP/HTTPS inbound
  ingress {
    protocol   = "tcp"
    rule_no    = 100
    action     = "allow"
    cidr_block = "0.0.0.0/0"
    from_port  = 80
    to_port    = 80
  }
  
  ingress {
    protocol   = "tcp"
    rule_no    = 110
    action     = "allow"
    cidr_block = "0.0.0.0/0"
    from_port  = 443
    to_port    = 443
  }
  
  # Allow ephemeral ports (for responses)
  ingress {
    protocol   = "tcp"
    rule_no    = 120
    action     = "allow"
    cidr_block = "0.0.0.0/0"
    from_port  = 1024
    to_port    = 65535
  }
  
  # Allow all outbound
  egress {
    protocol   = "-1"
    rule_no    = 100
    action     = "allow"
    cidr_block = "0.0.0.0/0"
    from_port  = 0
    to_port    = 0
  }
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-public-nacl"
    }
  )
}

resource "aws_network_acl" "private" {
  vpc_id     = aws_vpc.main.id
  subnet_ids = aws_subnet.private[*].id
  
  # Allow from VPC
  ingress {
    protocol   = "-1"
    rule_no    = 100
    action     = "allow"
    cidr_block = var.vpc_cidr
    from_port  = 0
    to_port    = 0
  }
  
  # Allow ephemeral ports from internet (for NAT responses)
  ingress {
    protocol   = "tcp"
    rule_no    = 110
    action     = "allow"
    cidr_block = "0.0.0.0/0"
    from_port  = 1024
    to_port    = 65535
  }
  
  # Allow all outbound
  egress {
    protocol   = "-1"
    rule_no    = 100
    action     = "allow"
    cidr_block = "0.0.0.0/0"
    from_port  = 0
    to_port    = 0
  }
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-private-nacl"
    }
  )
}

resource "aws_network_acl" "database" {
  vpc_id     = aws_vpc.main.id
  subnet_ids = aws_subnet.database[*].id
  
  # Only allow from private subnets
  ingress {
    protocol   = "-1"
    rule_no    = 100
    action     = "allow"
    cidr_block = var.vpc_cidr
    from_port  = 0
    to_port    = 0
  }
  
  # Only allow to VPC
  egress {
    protocol   = "-1"
    rule_no    = 100
    action     = "allow"
    cidr_block = var.vpc_cidr
    from_port  = 0
    to_port    = 0
  }
  
  tags = merge(
    var.tags,
    {
      Name = "${var.vpc_name}-database-nacl"
    }
  )
}

data "aws_region" "current" {}

# Outputs
output "vpc_id" {
  value = aws_vpc.main.id
}

output "public_subnet_ids" {
  value = aws_subnet.public[*].id
}

output "private_subnet_ids" {
  value = aws_subnet.private[*].id
}

output "database_subnet_ids" {
  value = aws_subnet.database[*].id
}

output "nat_gateway_ips" {
  value = aws_eip.nat[*].public_ip
}
```
