## Chapter 6.2: Secure Cloud Infrastructure (AWS) - Continued

### Phase 3: Security Services Implementation (3-4 hours)

**Step 3.1: Enable GuardDuty Threat Detection**

```python
# File: enable-guardduty.py
"""
Enable and configure GuardDuty across all accounts
"""

import boto3
import json
from botocore.exceptions import ClientError

class GuardDutyDeployer:
    """Deploy GuardDuty across AWS Organization"""
    
    def __init__(self, security_account_id, member_accounts):
        self.security_account_id = security_account_id
        self.member_accounts = member_accounts
        self.guardduty = boto3.client('guardduty')
        
    def enable_guardduty_master(self):
        """Enable GuardDuty in Security account (master)"""
        
        print("Enabling GuardDuty in Security account...")
        
        try:
            # Create detector
            response = self.guardduty.create_detector(
                Enable=True,
                FindingPublishingFrequency='FIFTEEN_MINUTES',
                DataSources={
                    'S3Logs': {'Enable': True},
                    'Kubernetes': {
                        'AuditLogs': {'Enable': True}
                    },
                    'MalwareProtection': {
                        'ScanEc2InstanceWithFindings': {
                            'EbsVolumes': {'Enable': True}
                        }
                    }
                }
            )
            
            detector_id = response['DetectorId']
            print(f"✓ GuardDuty detector created: {detector_id}")
            
            # Configure publishing destination (S3)
            self.configure_finding_export(detector_id)
            
            # Configure alert frequency
            self.guardduty.update_detector(
                DetectorId=detector_id,
                Enable=True,
                FindingPublishingFrequency='FIFTEEN_MINUTES'
            )
            
            return detector_id
            
        except ClientError as e:
            print(f"✗ Error enabling GuardDuty: {e}")
            return None
    
    def configure_finding_export(self, detector_id):
        """Export findings to S3 for long-term storage"""
        
        bucket_name = f"guardduty-findings-{self.security_account_id}"
        
        # Create S3 bucket for findings
        s3 = boto3.client('s3')
        
        try:
            s3.create_bucket(Bucket=bucket_name)
            
            # Apply bucket policy
            bucket_policy = {
                "Version": "2012-10-17",
                "Statement": [
                    {
                        "Sid": "AllowGuardDutyPutObject",
                        "Effect": "Allow",
                        "Principal": {
                            "Service": "guardduty.amazonaws.com"
                        },
                        "Action": "s3:PutObject",
                        "Resource": f"arn:aws:s3:::{bucket_name}/*"
                    },
                    {
                        "Sid": "AllowGuardDutyGetBucket",
                        "Effect": "Allow",
                        "Principal": {
                            "Service": "guardduty.amazonaws.com"
                        },
                        "Action": "s3:GetBucketLocation",
                        "Resource": f"arn:aws:s3:::{bucket_name}"
                    }
                ]
            }
            
            s3.put_bucket_policy(
                Bucket=bucket_name,
                Policy=json.dumps(bucket_policy)
            )
            
            # Enable versioning
            s3.put_bucket_versioning(
                Bucket=bucket_name,
                VersioningConfiguration={'Status': 'Enabled'}
            )
            
            # Enable encryption
            s3.put_bucket_encryption(
                Bucket=bucket_name,
                ServerSideEncryptionConfiguration={
                    'Rules': [
                        {
                            'ApplyServerSideEncryptionByDefault': {
                                'SSEAlgorithm': 'AES256'
                            }
                        }
                    ]
                }
            )
            
            # Create publishing destination
            self.guardduty.create_publishing_destination(
                DetectorId=detector_id,
                DestinationType='S3',
                DestinationProperties={
                    'DestinationArn': f'arn:aws:s3:::{bucket_name}',
                    'KmsKeyArn': ''  # Optional: Use KMS key
                }
            )
            
            print(f"✓ Finding export configured to s3://{bucket_name}")
            
        except ClientError as e:
            print(f"⚠ Warning: Could not configure export: {e}")
    
    def invite_member_accounts(self, detector_id):
        """Invite member accounts to GuardDuty"""
        
        print("\nInviting member accounts...")
        
        for account_id, email in self.member_accounts.items():
            try:
                # Create member
                self.guardduty.create_members(
                    DetectorId=detector_id,
                    AccountDetails=[
                        {
                            'AccountId': account_id,
                            'Email': email
                        }
                    ]
                )
                
                # Invite member
                self.guardduty.invite_members(
                    DetectorId=detector_id,
                    AccountIds=[account_id],
                    DisableEmailNotification=True
                )
                
                print(f"✓ Invited account {account_id}")
                
            except ClientError as e:
                print(f"✗ Error inviting {account_id}: {e}")
    
    def accept_invitation_member(self, account_id, invitation_id):
        """Accept GuardDuty invitation (run in member account)"""
        
        # Switch to member account
        sts = boto3.client('sts')
        assumed_role = sts.assume_role(
            RoleArn=f'arn:aws:iam::{account_id}:role/OrganizationAccountAccessRole',
            RoleSessionName='GuardDutySetup'
        )
        
        credentials = assumed_role['Credentials']
        gd_member = boto3.client(
            'guardduty',
            aws_access_key_id=credentials['AccessKeyId'],
            aws_secret_access_key=credentials['SecretAccessKey'],
            aws_session_token=credentials['SessionToken']
        )
        
        try:
            # Create detector in member account
            response = gd_member.create_detector(Enable=True)
            detector_id = response['DetectorId']
            
            # Accept invitation
            gd_member.accept_invitation(
                DetectorId=detector_id,
                MasterId=self.security_account_id,
                InvitationId=invitation_id
            )
            
            print(f"✓ Account {account_id} accepted invitation")
            
        except ClientError as e:
            print(f"✗ Error in account {account_id}: {e}")
    
    def configure_suppression_rules(self, detector_id):
        """Configure finding suppression rules for known false positives"""
        
        suppression_rules = [
            {
                'Name': 'SuppressKnownGoodIPs',
                'Description': 'Suppress findings for corporate VPN IPs',
                'FindingCriteria': {
                    'Criterion': {
                        'type': {
                            'Eq': ['Recon:EC2/PortProbeUnprotectedPort']
                        },
                        'service.action.networkConnectionAction.remoteIpDetails.ipAddressV4': {
                            'Eq': ['203.0.113.0']  # Replace with your IPs
                        }
                    }
                }
            },
            {
                'Name': 'SuppressInternalScanning',
                'Description': 'Suppress findings from vulnerability scanners',
                'FindingCriteria': {
                    'Criterion': {
                        'resource.instanceDetails.tags.key': {
                            'Eq': ['Scanner']
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        ]
        
        for rule in suppression_rules:
            try:
                self.guardduty.create_filter(
                    DetectorId=detector_id,
                    Name=rule['Name'],
                    Description=rule['Description'],
                    Action='ARCHIVE',
                    FindingCriteria=rule['FindingCriteria']
                )
                print(f"✓ Created suppression rule: {rule['Name']}")
            except ClientError as e:
                print(f"⚠ Could not create rule {rule['Name']}: {e}")

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    deployer = GuardDutyDeployer(
        security_account_id='111122223333',
        member_accounts={
            '444455556666': 'logs@company.com',
            '777788889999': 'dev@company.com',
            '000011112222': 'prod@company.com'
        }
    )
    
    # Enable in master
    detector_id = deployer.enable_guardduty_master()
    
    # Invite members
    if detector_id:
        deployer.invite_member_accounts(detector_id)
        deployer.configure_suppression_rules(detector_id)
    
    print("\n✓ GuardDuty deployment complete!")
```

**Step 3.2: Enable Security Hub**

```python
# File: enable-security-hub.py
"""
Enable Security Hub for centralized security findings
"""

import boto3
from botocore.exceptions import ClientError

class SecurityHubDeployer:
    """Deploy Security Hub across organization"""
    
    def __init__(self, admin_account_id, member_accounts):
        self.admin_account_id = admin_account_id
        self.member_accounts = member_accounts
        self.securityhub = boto3.client('securityhub')
        
    def enable_security_hub(self):
        """Enable Security Hub in admin account"""
        
        print("Enabling Security Hub...")
        
        try:
            # Enable Security Hub
            self.securityhub.enable_security_hub(
                Tags={
                    'Environment': 'Production',
                    'ManagedBy': 'Terraform'
                },
                EnableDefaultStandards=True
            )
            
            print("✓ Security Hub enabled")
            
            # Enable standards
            self.enable_standards()
            
            # Enable product integrations
            self.enable_integrations()
            
        except ClientError as e:
            if 'ResourceConflictException' in str(e):
                print("✓ Security Hub already enabled")
            else:
                print(f"✗ Error enabling Security Hub: {e}")
    
    def enable_standards(self):
        """Enable security standards (CIS, PCI-DSS, AWS Best Practices)"""
        
        standards = [
            {
                'arn': 'arn:aws:securityhub:us-east-1::standards/aws-foundational-security-best-practices/v/1.0.0',
                'name': 'AWS Foundational Security Best Practices'
            },
            {
                'arn': 'arn:aws:securityhub:::ruleset/cis-aws-foundations-benchmark/v/1.2.0',
                'name': 'CIS AWS Foundations Benchmark v1.2.0'
            },
            {
                'arn': 'arn:aws:securityhub:us-east-1::standards/pci-dss/v/3.2.1',
                'name': 'PCI DSS v3.2.1'
            }
        ]
        
        for standard in standards:
            try:
                self.securityhub.batch_enable_standards(
                    StandardsSubscriptionRequests=[
                        {
                            'StandardsArn': standard['arn']
                        }
                    ]
                )
                print(f"✓ Enabled standard: {standard['name']}")
            except ClientError as e:
                print(f"⚠ Could not enable {standard['name']}: {e}")
    
    def enable_integrations(self):
        """Enable product integrations (GuardDuty, Inspector, etc.)"""
        
        integrations = [
            'arn:aws:securityhub:us-east-1::product/aws/guardduty',
            'arn:aws:securityhub:us-east-1::product/aws/inspector',
            'arn:aws:securityhub:us-east-1::product/aws/macie',
            'arn:aws:securityhub:us-east-1::product/aws/access-analyzer',
            'arn:aws:securityhub:us-east-1::product/aws/firewall-manager'
        ]
        
        for integration in integrations:
            try:
                self.securityhub.enable_import_findings_for_product(
                    ProductArn=integration
                )
                print(f"✓ Enabled integration: {integration.split('/')[-1]}")
            except ClientError as e:
                print(f"⚠ Could not enable integration: {e}")
    
    def create_custom_insights(self):
        """Create custom insights for common security patterns"""
        
        insights = [
            {
                'Name': 'Critical Unresolved Findings',
                'Filters': {
                    'SeverityLabel': [{'Value': 'CRITICAL', 'Comparison': 'EQUALS'}],
                    'RecordState': [{'Value': 'ACTIVE', 'Comparison': 'EQUALS'}],
                    'WorkflowStatus': [{'Value': 'NEW', 'Comparison': 'EQUALS'}]
                },
                'GroupByAttribute': 'ResourceType'
            },
            {
                'Name': 'High Severity EC2 Findings',
                'Filters': {
                    'SeverityLabel': [{'Value': 'HIGH', 'Comparison': 'EQUALS'}],
                    'ResourceType': [{'Value': 'AwsEc2Instance', 'Comparison': 'EQUALS'}]
                },
                'GroupByAttribute': 'ResourceId'
            },
            {
                'Name': 'Failed CIS Controls',
                'Filters': {
                    'ComplianceStatus': [{'Value': 'FAILED', 'Comparison': 'EQUALS'}],
                    'GeneratorId': [{'Value': 'cis-aws-foundations-benchmark', 'Comparison': 'PREFIX'}]
                },
                'GroupByAttribute': 'GeneratorId'
            }
        ]
        
        for insight in insights:
            try:
                self.securityhub.create_insight(
                    Name=insight['Name'],
                    Filters=insight['Filters'],
                    GroupByAttribute=insight['GroupByAttribute']
                )
                print(f"✓ Created insight: {insight['Name']}")
            except ClientError as e:
                print(f"⚠ Could not create insight: {e}")
    
    def configure_finding_aggregation(self):
        """Configure cross-region finding aggregation"""
        
        try:
            # Set up aggregation (available in one region)
            self.securityhub.create_finding_aggregator(
                RegionLinkingMode='ALL_REGIONS'
            )
            print("✓ Cross-region aggregation configured")
        except ClientError as e:
            print(f"⚠ Aggregation note: {e}")

# Usage
deployer = SecurityHubDeployer(
    admin_account_id='111122223333',
    member_accounts=['444455556666', '777788889999', '000011112222']
)

deployer.enable_security_hub()
deployer.create_custom_insights()
deployer.configure_finding_aggregation()
```

**Step 3.3: Configure AWS Config**

```hcl
# File: terraform/config/main.tf
# AWS Config for compliance tracking

resource "aws_config_configuration_recorder" "main" {
  name     = "config-recorder"
  role_arn = aws_iam_role.config.arn
  
  recording_group {
    all_supported = true
    include_global_resource_types = true
  }
}

resource "aws_config_delivery_channel" "main" {
  name           = "config-delivery"
  s3_bucket_name = aws_s3_bucket.config.bucket
  
  snapshot_delivery_properties {
    delivery_frequency = "TwentyFour_Hours"
  }
  
  depends_on = [aws_config_configuration_recorder.main]
}

resource "aws_config_configuration_recorder_status" "main" {
  name       = aws_config_configuration_recorder.main.name
  is_enabled = true
  
  depends_on = [aws_config_delivery_channel.main]
}

# S3 bucket for Config history
resource "aws_s3_bucket" "config" {
  bucket = "config-history-${data.aws_caller_identity.current.account_id}"
}

resource "aws_s3_bucket_versioning" "config" {
  bucket = aws_s3_bucket.config.id
  
  versioning_configuration {
    status = "Enabled"
  }
}

resource "aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration" "config" {
  bucket = aws_s3_bucket.config.id
  
  rule {
    apply_server_side_encryption_by_default {
      sse_algorithm = "AES256"
    }
  }
}

# IAM role for Config
resource "aws_iam_role" "config" {
  name = "aws-config-role"
  
  assume_role_policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [
      {
        Action = "sts:AssumeRole"
        Effect = "Allow"
        Principal = {
          Service = "config.amazonaws.com"
        }
      }
    ]
  })
}

resource "aws_iam_role_policy_attachment" "config" {
  role       = aws_iam_role.config.name
  policy_arn = "arn:aws:iam::aws:policy/service-role/ConfigRole"
}

# Managed Config Rules
resource "aws_config_config_rule" "encrypted_volumes" {
  name = "encrypted-volumes"
  
  source {
    owner             = "AWS"
    source_identifier = "ENCRYPTED_VOLUMES"
  }
  
  depends_on = [aws_config_configuration_recorder.main]
}

resource "aws_config_config_rule" "root_mfa" {
  name = "root-account-mfa-enabled"
  
  source {
    owner             = "AWS"
    source_identifier = "ROOT_ACCOUNT_MFA_ENABLED"
  }
  
  depends_on = [aws_config_configuration_recorder.main]
}

resource "aws_config_config_rule" "s3_public_read" {
  name = "s3-bucket-public-read-prohibited"
  
  source {
    owner             = "AWS"
    source_identifier = "S3_BUCKET_PUBLIC_READ_PROHIBITED"
  }
  
  depends_on = [aws_config_configuration_recorder.main]
}

resource "aws_config_config_rule" "s3_public_write" {
  name = "s3-bucket-public-write-prohibited"
  
  source {
    owner             = "AWS"
    source_identifier = "S3_BUCKET_PUBLIC_WRITE_PROHIBITED"
  }
  
  depends_on = [aws_config_configuration_recorder.main]
}

resource "aws_config_config_rule" "rds_encryption" {
  name = "rds-storage-encrypted"
  
  source {
    owner             = "AWS"
    source_identifier = "RDS_STORAGE_ENCRYPTED"
  }
  
  depends_on = [aws_config_configuration_recorder.main]
}

resource "aws_config_config_rule" "iam_password_policy" {
  name = "iam-password-policy"
  
  source {
    owner             = "AWS"
    source_identifier = "IAM_PASSWORD_POLICY"
  }
  
  input_parameters = jsonencode({
    RequireUppercaseCharacters = true
    RequireLowercaseCharacters = true
    RequireSymbols             = true
    RequireNumbers             = true
    MinimumPasswordLength      = 14
    PasswordReusePrevention    = 24
    MaxPasswordAge             = 90
  })
  
  depends_on = [aws_config_configuration_recorder.main]
}

# Custom Config Rule (Lambda-based)
resource "aws_config_config_rule" "required_tags" {
  name = "required-tags"
  
  source {
    owner             = "CUSTOM_LAMBDA"
    source_identifier = aws_lambda_function.required_tags_compliance.arn
    
    source_detail {
      event_source = "aws.config"
      message_type = "ConfigurationItemChangeNotification"
    }
  }
  
  scope {
    compliance_resource_types = [
      "AWS::EC2::Instance",
      "AWS::RDS::DBInstance",
      "AWS::S3::Bucket"
    ]
  }
  
  depends_on = [aws_config_configuration_recorder.main]
}

# Lambda for custom rule
resource "aws_lambda_function" "required_tags_compliance" {
  filename      = "required_tags.zip"
  function_name = "RequiredTagsCompliance"
  role          = aws_iam_role.lambda_config.arn
  handler       = "index.handler"
  runtime       = "python3.9"
  
  environment {
    variables = {
      REQUIRED_TAGS = "Environment,Owner,CostCenter"
    }
  }
}

data "aws_caller_identity" "current" {}
```

**Step 4.1: CloudTrail Configuration**

```hcl
# File: terraform/cloudtrail/main.tf

# Organization-wide CloudTrail
resource "aws_cloudtrail" "organization" {
  name                          = "organization-trail"
  s3_bucket_name                = aws_s3_bucket.cloudtrail.bucket
  include_global_service_events = true
  is_multi_region_trail         = true
  is_organization_trail         = true
  enable_log_file_validation    = true
  
  event_selector {
    read_write_type           = "All"
    include_management_events = true
    
    data_resource {
      type   = "AWS::S3::Object"
      values = ["arn:aws:s3:::"]
    }
    
    data_resource {
      type   = "AWS::Lambda::Function"
      values = ["arn:aws:lambda"]
    }
  }
  
  insight_selector {
    insight_type = "ApiCallRateInsight"
  }
  
  insight_selector {
    insight_type = "ApiErrorRateInsight"
  }
}

# S3 bucket for CloudTrail logs
resource "aws_s3_bucket" "cloudtrail" {
  bucket = "cloudtrail-logs-${data.aws_caller_identity.current.account_id}"
}

resource "aws_s3_bucket_policy" "cloudtrail" {
  bucket = aws_s3_bucket.cloudtrail.id
  
  policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [
      {
        Sid    = "AWSCloudTrailAclCheck"
        Effect = "Allow"
        Principal = {
          Service = "cloudtrail.amazonaws.com"
        }
        Action   = "s3:GetBucketAcl"
        Resource = aws_s3_bucket.cloudtrail.arn
      },
      {
        Sid    = "AWSCloudTrailWrite"
        Effect = "Allow"
        Principal = {
          Service = "cloudtrail.amazonaws.com"
        }
        Action   = "s3:PutObject"
        Resource = "${aws_s3_bucket.cloudtrail.arn}/*"
        Condition = {
          StringEquals = {
            "s3:x-amz-acl" = "bucket-owner-full-control"
          }
        }
      }
    ]
  })
}

# Enable MFA delete protection
resource "aws_s3_bucket_versioning" "cloudtrail" {
  bucket = aws_s3_bucket.cloudtrail.id
  
  versioning_configuration {
    status     = "Enabled"
    mfa_delete = "Enabled"
  }
}

# Lifecycle policy for log retention
resource "aws_s3_bucket_lifecycle_configuration" "cloudtrail" {
  bucket = aws_s3_bucket.cloudtrail.id
  
  rule {
    id     = "log-retention"
    status = "Enabled"
    
    transition {
      days          = 90
      storage_class = "STANDARD_IA"
    }
    
    transition {
      days          = 180
      storage_class = "GLACIER"
    }
    
    expiration {
      days = 2555  # 7 years for compliance
    }
  }
}

# CloudWatch Logs integration
resource "aws_cloudwatch_log_group" "cloudtrail" {
  name              = "/aws/cloudtrail/organization"
  retention_in_days = 90
}

resource "aws_iam_role" "cloudtrail_cloudwatch" {
  name = "cloudtrail-cloudwatch-role"
  
  assume_role_policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [
      {
        Action = "sts:AssumeRole"
        Effect = "Allow"
        Principal = {
          Service = "cloudtrail.amazonaws.com"
        }
      }
    ]
  })
}

resource "aws_iam_role_policy" "cloudtrail_cloudwatch" {
  role = aws_iam_role.cloudtrail_cloudwatch.id
  
  policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [
      {
        Effect = "Allow"
        Action = [
          "logs:CreateLogStream",
          "logs:PutLogEvents"
        ]
        Resource = "${aws_cloudwatch_log_group.cloudtrail.arn}:*"
      }
    ]
  })
}
```

### Phase 5: IAM Identity Center & SSO (2 hours)

**Step 5.1: Enable IAM Identity Center**

```python
# File: setup-identity-center.py
"""
Configure IAM Identity Center (AWS SSO) for centralized access
"""

import boto3
from botocore.exceptions import ClientError

class IdentityCenterSetup:
    """Setup IAM Identity Center across organization"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.sso_admin = boto3.client('sso-admin')
        self.identitystore = boto3.client('identitystore')
        self.organizations = boto3.client('organizations')
        
    def enable_identity_center(self):
        """Enable IAM Identity Center"""
        
        print("Enabling IAM Identity Center...")
        
        try:
            # Get instance details
            instances = self.sso_admin.list_instances()
            
            if instances['Instances']:
                instance_arn = instances['Instances'][0]['InstanceArn']
                identity_store_id = instances['Instances'][0]['IdentityStoreId']
                
                print(f"✓ Identity Center already enabled")
                print(f"  Instance ARN: {instance_arn}")
                print(f"  Identity Store ID: {identity_store_id}")
                
                return instance_arn, identity_store_id
            else:
                print("⚠ Please enable IAM Identity Center manually in console")
                print("  Navigate to: IAM Identity Center → Enable")
                return None, None
                
        except ClientError as e:
            print(f"✗ Error: {e}")
            return None, None
    
    def create_permission_sets(self, instance_arn):
        """Create permission sets for different roles"""
        
        permission_sets = {
            'AdministratorAccess': {
                'description': 'Full administrator access',
                'managed_policies': ['arn:aws:iam::aws:policy/AdministratorAccess'],
                'session_duration': 'PT8H'
            },
            'ReadOnlyAccess': {
                'description': 'Read-only access to all resources',
                'managed_policies': ['arn:aws:iam::aws:policy/ReadOnlyAccess'],
                'session_duration': 'PT8H'
            },
            'SecurityAuditor': {
                'description': 'Security audit permissions',
                'managed_policies': [
                    'arn:aws:iam::aws:policy/SecurityAudit',
                    'arn:aws:iam::aws:policy/ReadOnlyAccess'
                ],
                'session_duration': 'PT8H'
            },
            'DeveloperAccess': {
                'description': 'Developer access with restricted permissions',
                'managed_policies': [
                    'arn:aws:iam::aws:policy/PowerUserAccess'
                ],
                'session_duration': 'PT4H'
            },
            'BillingAccess': {
                'description': 'Billing and cost management access',
                'managed_policies': [
                    'arn:aws:iam::aws:policy/job-function/Billing'
                ],
                'session_duration': 'PT4H'
            }
        }
        
        created_sets = {}
        
        for name, config in permission_sets.items():
            try:
                # Create permission set
                response = self.sso_admin.create_permission_set(
                    Name=name,
                    Description=config['description'],
                    InstanceArn=instance_arn,
                    SessionDuration=config['session_duration']
                )
                
                permission_set_arn = response['PermissionSet']['PermissionSetArn']
                
                # Attach managed policies
                for policy_arn in config['managed_policies']:
                    self.sso_admin.attach_managed_policy_to_permission_set(
                        InstanceArn=instance_arn,
                        PermissionSetArn=permission_set_arn,
                        ManagedPolicyArn=policy_arn
                    )
                
                created_sets[name] = permission_set_arn
                print(f"✓ Created permission set: {name}")
                
            except ClientError as e:
                if 'ConflictException' in str(e):
                    print(f"⚠ Permission set {name} already exists")
                else:
                    print(f"✗ Error creating {name}: {e}")
        
        return created_sets
    
    def create_users(self, identity_store_id):
        """Create SSO users"""
        
        users = [
            {
                'username': 'john.doe',
                'email': 'john.doe@company.com',
                'first_name': 'John',
                'last_name': 'Doe',
                'groups': ['Administrators']
            },
            {
                'username': 'jane.smith',
                'email': 'jane.smith@company.com',
                'first_name': 'Jane',
                'last_name': 'Smith',
                'groups': ['SecurityTeam']
            },
            {
                'username': 'dev.user',
                'email': 'dev.user@company.com',
                'first_name': 'Dev',
                'last_name': 'User',
                'groups': ['Developers']
            }
        ]
        
        created_users = {}
        
        for user in users:
            try:
                response = self.identitystore.create_user(
                    IdentityStoreId=identity_store_id,
                    UserName=user['username'],
                    Name={
                        'FamilyName': user['last_name'],
                        'GivenName': user['first_name']
                    },
                    Emails=[
                        {
                            'Value': user['email'],
                            'Type': 'work',
                            'Primary': True
                        }
                    ]
                )
                
                user_id = response['UserId']
                created_users[user['username']] = {
                    'user_id': user_id,
                    'groups': user['groups']
                }
                
                print(f"✓ Created user: {user['username']}")
                
            except ClientError as e:
                if 'ConflictException' in str(e):
                    print(f"⚠ User {user['username']} already exists")
                else:
                    print(f"✗ Error creating user: {e}")
        
        return created_users
    
    def create_groups(self, identity_store_id):
        """Create SSO groups"""
        
        groups = [
            {'name': 'Administrators', 'description': 'System administrators'},
            {'name': 'SecurityTeam', 'description': 'Security team members'},
            {'name': 'Developers', 'description': 'Development team'},
            {'name': 'Auditors', 'description': 'Compliance auditors'}
        ]
        
        created_groups = {}
        
        for group in groups:
            try:
                response = self.identitystore.create_group(
                    IdentityStoreId=identity_store_id,
                    DisplayName=group['name'],
                    Description=group['description']
                )
                
                group_id = response['GroupId']
                created_groups[group['name']] = group_id
                
                print(f"✓ Created group: {group['name']}")
                
            except ClientError as e:
                if 'ConflictException' in str(e):
                    print(f"⚠ Group {group['name']} already exists")
                else:
                    print(f"✗ Error creating group: {e}")
        
        return created_groups
    
    def assign_users_to_groups(self, identity_store_id, users, groups):
        """Assign users to their groups"""
        
        for username, user_data in users.items():
            for group_name in user_data['groups']:
                if group_name in groups:
                    try:
                        self.identitystore.create_group_membership(
                            IdentityStoreId=identity_store_id,
                            GroupId=groups[group_name],
                            MemberId={'UserId': user_data['user_id']}
                        )
                        print(f"✓ Added {username} to {group_name}")
                    except ClientError as e:
                        print(f"✗ Error: {e}")
    
    def assign_account_access(self, instance_arn, permission_sets, groups, accounts):
        """Assign permission sets to groups for specific accounts"""
        
        assignments = [
            {
                'group': 'Administrators',
                'permission_set': 'AdministratorAccess',
                'accounts': 'all'
            },
            {
                'group': 'SecurityTeam',
                'permission_set': 'SecurityAuditor',
                'accounts': 'all'
            },
            {
                'group': 'Developers',
                'permission_set': 'DeveloperAccess',
                'accounts': ['dev']
            },
            {
                'group': 'Auditors',
                'permission_set': 'ReadOnlyAccess',
                'accounts': 'all'
            }
        ]
        
        for assignment in assignments:
            group_name = assignment['group']
            permission_set_name = assignment['permission_set']
            
            if group_name not in groups or permission_set_name not in permission_sets:
                continue
            
            target_accounts = accounts if assignment['accounts'] == 'all' else assignment['accounts']
            
            for account_id in target_accounts:
                try:
                    self.sso_admin.create_account_assignment(
                        InstanceArn=instance_arn,
                        TargetId=account_id,
                        TargetType='AWS_ACCOUNT',
                        PermissionSetArn=permission_sets[permission_set_name],
                        PrincipalType='GROUP',
                        PrincipalId=groups[group_name]
                    )
                    print(f"✓ Assigned {permission_set_name} to {group_name} for account {account_id}")
                except ClientError as e:
                    if 'ConflictException' not in str(e):
                        print(f"✗ Error: {e}")

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    setup = IdentityCenterSetup()
    
    # Enable Identity Center
    instance_arn, identity_store_id = setup.enable_identity_center()
    
    if instance_arn and identity_store_id:
        # Create permission sets
        permission_sets = setup.create_permission_sets(instance_arn)
        
        # Create groups
        groups = setup.create_groups(identity_store_id)
        
        # Create users
        users = setup.create_users(identity_store_id)
        
        # Assign users to groups
        setup.assign_users_to_groups(identity_store_id, users, groups)
        
        # Assign account access
        accounts = ['111122223333', '444455556666', '777788889999']
        setup.assign_account_access(instance_arn, permission_sets, groups, accounts)
        
        print("\n✓ IAM Identity Center setup complete!")
```

### Phase 6: Automated Remediation (2 hours)

**Step 6.1: EventBridge Rules for Automated Response**

```hcl
# File: terraform/remediation/main.tf

# Lambda for automatic remediation
resource "aws_lambda_function" "auto_remediate" {
  filename      = "auto_remediate.zip"
  function_name = "SecurityAutoRemediate"
  role          = aws_iam_role.remediation_lambda.arn
  handler       = "index.handler"
  runtime       = "python3.9"
  timeout       = 300
  
  environment {
    variables = {
      SNS_TOPIC_ARN = aws_sns_topic.security_alerts.arn
    }
  }
}

# IAM role for remediation Lambda
resource "aws_iam_role" "remediation_lambda" {
  name = "remediation-lambda-role"
  
  assume_role_policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [
      {
        Action = "sts:AssumeRole"
        Effect = "Allow"
        Principal = {
          Service = "lambda.amazonaws.com"
        }
      }
    ]
  })
}

resource "aws_iam_role_policy" "remediation_lambda" {
  role = aws_iam_role.remediation_lambda.id
  
  policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [
      {
        Effect = "Allow"
        Action = [
          "logs:CreateLogGroup",
          "logs:CreateLogStream",
          "logs:PutLogEvents",
          "ec2:*",
          "s3:*",
          "iam:*",
          "sns:Publish"
        ]
        Resource = "*"
      }
    ]
  })
}

# EventBridge rule: Public S3 bucket
resource "aws_cloudwatch_event_rule" "s3_public_bucket" {
  name        = "s3-public-bucket-remediation"
  description = "Remediate public S3 buckets"
  
  event_pattern = jsonencode({
    source      = ["aws.config"]
    detail-type = ["Config Rules Compliance Change"]
    detail = {
      configRuleName = ["s3-bucket-public-read-prohibited"]
      newEvaluationResult = {
        complianceType = ["NON_COMPLIANT"]
      }
    }
  })
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_event_target" "s3_public_remediation" {
  rule      = aws_cloudwatch_event_rule.s3_public_bucket.name
  target_id = "RemediatePublicS3"
  arn       = aws_lambda_function.auto_remediate.arn
  
  input_transformer {
    input_paths = {
      resourceId = "$.detail.resourceId"
      ruleName   = "$.detail.configRuleName"
    }
    input_template = jsonencode({
      action     = "block_public_access"
      resource   = "<resourceId>"
      rule       = "<ruleName>"
    })
  }
}

# EventBridge rule: Security group with 0.0.0.0/0
resource "aws_cloudwatch_event_rule" "open_security_group" {
  name        = "open-security-group-remediation"
  description = "Remediate overly permissive security groups"
  
  event_pattern = jsonencode({
    source      = ["aws.config"]
    detail-type = ["Config Rules Compliance Change"]
    detail = {
      configRuleName = ["restricted-ssh", "restricted-common-ports"]
      newEvaluationResult = {
        complianceType = ["NON_COMPLIANT"]
      }
    }
  })
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_event_target" "sg_remediation" {
  rule      = aws_cloudwatch_event_rule.open_security_group.name
  target_id = "RemediateSecurityGroup"
  arn       = aws_lambda_function.auto_remediate.arn
}

# EventBridge rule: Unencrypted EBS volume
resource "aws_cloudwatch_event_rule" "unencrypted_ebs" {
  name        = "unencrypted-ebs-remediation"
  description = "Remediate unencrypted EBS volumes"
  
  event_pattern = jsonencode({
    source      = ["aws.ec2"]
    detail-type = ["EBS Volume Notification"]
    detail = {
      event = ["createVolume"]
    }
  })
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_event_target" "ebs_remediation" {
  rule      = aws_cloudwatch_event_rule.unencrypted_ebs.name
  target_id = "RemediateUnencryptedEBS"
  arn       = aws_lambda_function.auto_remediate.arn
}

# SNS topic for alerts
resource "aws_sns_topic" "security_alerts" {
  name = "security-auto-remediation-alerts"
}

resource "aws_sns_topic_subscription" "email" {
  topic_arn = aws_sns_topic.security_alerts.arn
  protocol  = "email"
  endpoint  = "security@company.com"
}

# Lambda permission for EventBridge
resource "aws_lambda_permission" "allow_eventbridge" {
  statement_id  = "AllowExecutionFromEventBridge"
  action        = "lambda:InvokeFunction"
  function_name = aws_lambda_function.auto_remediate.function_name
  principal     = "events.amazonaws.com"
}
```

**Remediation Lambda Code:**

```python
# File: auto_remediate/index.py
"""
Automated security remediation Lambda
"""

import boto3
import json
import os

ec2 = boto3.client('ec2')
s3 = boto3.client('s3')
sns = boto3.client('sns')

SNS_TOPIC_ARN = os.environ['SNS_TOPIC_ARN']

def handler(event, context):
    """Main remediation handler"""
    
    print(f"Event: {json.dumps(event)}")
    
    action = event.get('action')
    resource = event.get('resource')
    
    remediation_actions = {
        'block_public_access': remediate_public_s3,
        'fix_security_group': remediate_security_group,
        'encrypt_ebs': remediate_unencrypted_ebs
    }
    
    if action in remediation_actions:
        result = remediation_actions[action](resource, event)
        send_notification(action, resource, result)
        return result
    else:
        return {'status': 'unknown_action', 'action': action}

def remediate_public_s3(bucket_name, event):
    """Block public access on S3 bucket"""
    
    try:
        # Enable block public access
        s3.put_public_access_block(
            Bucket=bucket_name,
            PublicAccessBlockConfiguration={
                'BlockPublicAcls': True,
                'IgnorePublicAcls': True,
                'BlockPublicPolicy': True,
                'RestrictPublicBuckets': True
            }
        )
        
        print(f"✓ Blocked public access on bucket: {bucket_name}")
        
        return {
            'status': 'success',
            'action': 'blocked_public_access',
            'resource': bucket_name
        }
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ Error remediating bucket {bucket_name}: {e}")
        return {
            'status': 'error',
            'error': str(e),
            'resource': bucket_name
        }

def remediate_security_group(sg_id, event):
    """Remove overly permissive security group rules"""
    
    try:
        # Get security group rules
        response = ec2.describe_security_groups(GroupIds=[sg_id])
        sg = response['SecurityGroups'][0]
        
        # Find rules with 0.0.0.0/0 on sensitive ports
        dangerous_ports = [22, 3389, 1433, 3306, 5432]
        rules_to_revoke = []
        
        for rule in sg['IpPermissions']:
            from_port = rule.get('FromPort')
            
            if from_port in dangerous_ports:
                for ip_range in rule.get('IpRanges', []):
                    if ip_range.get('CidrIp') == '0.0.0.0/0':
                        rules_to_revoke.append(rule)
                        break
        
        # Revoke dangerous rules
        if rules_to_revoke:
            ec2.revoke_security_group_ingress(
                GroupId=sg_id,
                IpPermissions=rules_to_revoke
            )
            
            print(f"✓ Removed {len(rules_to_revoke)} dangerous rules from {sg_id}")
        
        return {
            'status': 'success',
            'action': 'removed_public_rules',
            'resource': sg_id,
            'rules_removed': len(rules_to_revoke)
        }
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ Error remediating security group {sg_id}: {e}")
        return {
            'status': 'error',
            'error': str(e),
            'resource': sg_id
        }

def remediate_unencrypted_ebs(volume_id, event):
    """Create snapshot and encrypted copy of unencrypted volume"""
    
    try:
        # Create snapshot
        snapshot = ec2.create_snapshot(
            VolumeId=volume_id,
            Description=f'Pre-encryption snapshot of {volume_id}'
        )
        
        snapshot_id = snapshot['SnapshotId']
        
        # Wait for snapshot
        waiter = ec2.get_waiter('snapshot_completed')
        waiter.wait(SnapshotIds=[snapshot_id])
        
        # Create encrypted copy
        encrypted_snapshot = ec2.copy_snapshot(
            SourceSnapshotId=snapshot_id,
            SourceRegion=os.environ['AWS_REGION'],
            Description=f'Encrypted copy of {snapshot_id}',
            Encrypted=True
        )
        
        print(f"✓ Created encrypted snapshot for volume {volume_id}")
        
        return {
            'status': 'success',
            'action': 'created_encrypted_snapshot',
            'resource': volume_id,
            'encrypted_snapshot': encrypted_snapshot['SnapshotId']
        }
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ Error encrypting volume {volume_id}: {e}")
        return {
            'status': 'error',
            'error': str(e),
            'resource': volume_id
        }

def send_notification(action, resource, result):
    """Send SNS notification about remediation"""
    
    status = result.get('status', 'unknown')
    
    if status == 'success':
        subject = f"✓ Auto-Remediation Success: {action}"
        message = f"""
Automatic security remediation completed successfully.

Action: {action}
Resource: {resource}
Status: {status}

Details: {json.dumps(result, indent=2)}
"""
    else:
        subject = f"✗ Auto-Remediation Failed: {action}"
        message = f"""
Automatic security remediation failed.

Action: {action}
Resource: {resource}
Status: {status}
Error: {result.get('error', 'Unknown error')}

Manual intervention may be required.
"""
    
    try:
        sns.publish(
            TopicArn=SNS_TOPIC_ARN,
            Subject=subject,
            Message=message
        )
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ Error sending notification: {e}")
```

### Phase 7: Cost Optimization (1-2 hours)

**Cost Analysis & Optimization:**

```python
# File: cost-optimization.py
"""
Analyze and optimize AWS cloud costs
"""

import boto3
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import json

class CostOptimizer:
    """AWS cost analysis and optimization"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.ce = boto3.client('ce')  # Cost Explorer
        self.ec2 = boto3.client('ec2')
        self.rds = boto3.client('rds')
        
    def get_monthly_costs(self, months=6):
        """Get cost breakdown for past months"""
        
        end_date = datetime.now().date()
        start_date = end_date - timedelta(days=30*months)
        
        response = self.ce.get_cost_and_usage(
            TimePeriod={
                'Start': start_date.isoformat(),
                'End': end_date.isoformat()
            },
            Granularity='MONTHLY',
            Metrics=['UnblendedCost'],
            GroupBy=[
                {'Type': 'DIMENSION', 'Key': 'SERVICE'}
            ]
        )
        
        print("\n=== Monthly Cost Breakdown ===")
        for result in response['ResultsByTime']:
            period = result['TimePeriod']['Start']
            print(f"\n{period}:")
            
            groups = sorted(result['Groups'], 
                          key=lambda x: float(x['Metrics']['UnblendedCost']['Amount']), 
                          reverse=True)
            
            for group in groups[:10]:  # Top 10 services
                service = group['Keys'][0]
                cost = float(group['Metrics']['UnblendedCost']['Amount'])
                if cost > 1:  # Only show costs > $1
                    print(f"  {service:40} ${cost:>10.2f}")
    
    def find_idle_resources(self):
        """Identify idle resources to shut down"""
        
        print("\n=== Idle Resources ===")
        
        # Idle EC2 instances (low CPU for 7 days)
        cloudwatch = boto3.client('cloudwatch')
        instances = self.ec2.describe_instances()
        
        idle_instances = []
        for reservation in instances['Reservations']:
            for instance in reservation['Instances']:
                if instance['State']['Name'] != 'running':
                    continue
                
                instance_id = instance['InstanceId']
                
                # Get CPU utilization
                metrics = cloudwatch.get_metric_statistics(
                    Namespace='AWS/EC2',
                    MetricName='CPUUtilization',
                    Dimensions=[{'Name': 'InstanceId', 'Value': instance_id}],
                    StartTime=datetime.now() - timedelta(days=7),
                    EndTime=datetime.now(),
                    Period=86400,  # 1 day
                    Statistics=['Average']
                )
                
                if metrics['Datapoints']:
                    avg_cpu = sum(d['Average'] for d in metrics['Datapoints']) / len(metrics['Datapoints'])
                    
                    if avg_cpu < 5:  # Less than 5% CPU
                        idle_instances.append({
                            'id': instance_id,
                            'type': instance['InstanceType'],
                            'cpu': avg_cpu
                        })
        
        if idle_instances:
            print(f"\nFound {len(idle_instances)} idle EC2 instances:")
            for inst in idle_instances:
                print(f"  {inst['id']} ({inst['type']}) - {inst['cpu']:.1f}% CPU")
        
        # Unattached EBS volumes
        volumes = self.ec2.describe_volumes(
            Filters=[{'Name': 'status', 'Values': ['available']}]
        )
        
        if volumes['Volumes']:
            print(f"\nFound {len(volumes['Volumes'])} unattached EBS volumes:")
            total_size = sum(v['Size'] for v in volumes['Volumes'])
            print(f"  Total size: {total_size} GB")
            print(f"  Estimated monthly cost: ${total_size * 0.10:.2f}")
    
    def recommend_reserved_instances(self):
        """Recommend Reserved Instance purchases"""
        
        # Get RI recommendations from Cost Explorer
        response = self.ce.get_reservation_purchase_recommendation(
            Service='Amazon Elastic Compute Cloud - Compute',
            LookbackPeriodInDays='SIXTY_DAYS',
            TermInYears='ONE_YEAR',
            PaymentOption='NO_UPFRONT'
        )
        
        print("\n=== Reserved Instance Recommendations ===")
        
        if 'Recommendations' in response:
            for rec in response['Recommendations']:
                details = rec['RecommendationDetails'][0]
                instance_type = details['InstanceDetails']['EC2InstanceDetails']['InstanceType']
                
                monthly_savings = float(rec['RecommendationSummary']['TotalEstimatedMonthlySavingsAmount'])
                
                if monthly_savings > 10:
                    print(f"\nInstance Type: {instance_type}")
                    print(f"  Monthly Savings: ${monthly_savings:.2f}")
                    print(f"  Annual Savings: ${monthly_savings * 12:.2f}")
    
    def analyze_s3_costs(self):
        """Analyze S3 storage costs and recommend lifecycle policies"""
        
        s3 = boto3.client('s3')
        cloudwatch = boto3.client('cloudwatch')
        
        print("\n=== S3 Cost Analysis ===")
        
        buckets = s3.list_buckets()
        
        for bucket in buckets['Buckets']:
            bucket_name = bucket['Name']
            
            # Get bucket size
            try:
                metrics = cloudwatch.get_metric_statistics(
                    Namespace='AWS/S3',
                    MetricName='BucketSizeBytes',
                    Dimensions=[
                        {'Name': 'BucketName', 'Value': bucket_name},
                        {'Name': 'StorageType', 'Value': 'StandardStorage'}
                    ],
                    StartTime=datetime.now() - timedelta(days=2),
                    EndTime=datetime.now(),
                    Period=86400,
                    Statistics=['Average']
                )
                
                if metrics['Datapoints']:
                    size_bytes = metrics['Datapoints'][0]['Average']
                    size_gb = size_bytes / (1024**3)
                    monthly_cost = size_gb * 0.023  # Standard storage cost
                    
                    if size_gb > 100:  # Large buckets
                        print(f"\n{bucket_name}:")
                        print(f"  Size: {size_gb:.2f} GB")
                        print(f"  Monthly cost: ${monthly_cost:.2f}")
                        
                        # Check for lifecycle policy
                        try:
                            s3.get_bucket_lifecycle_configuration(Bucket=bucket_name)
                            print(f"  ✓ Has lifecycle policy")
                        except:
                            print(f"  ⚠ No lifecycle policy - consider adding one")
                            
            except Exception as e:
                pass

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    optimizer = CostOptimizer()
    
    optimizer.get_monthly_costs()
    optimizer.find_idle_resources()
    optimizer.recommend_reserved_instances()
    optimizer.analyze_s3_costs()
    
    print("\n=== Cost Optimization Summary ===")
    print("Actions to consider:")
    print("1. Stop or terminate idle EC2 instances")
    print("2. Delete unattached EBS volumes")
    print("3. Purchase recommended Reserved Instances")
    print("4. Add S3 lifecycle policies for large buckets")
    print("5. Review and optimize database instances")
```

### Testing & Validation

**Security Testing Checklist:**

```bash
# File: test-security-setup.sh
#!/bin/bash

echo "=== AWS Security Setup Validation ==="

# Test 1: Organizations
echo -e "\n[1] Testing Organizations..."
aws organizations describe-organization > /dev/null 2>&1
if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
    echo "✓ Organizations enabled"
else
    echo "✗ Organizations not configured"
fi

# Test 2: GuardDuty
echo -e "\n[2] Testing GuardDuty..."
DETECTORS=$(aws guardduty list-detectors --query 'DetectorIds[0]' --output text)
if [ -n "$DETECTORS" ] && [ "$DETECTORS" != "None" ]; then
    echo "✓ GuardDuty enabled"
    aws guardduty get-detector --detector-id $DETECTORS | grep -q '"Status": "ENABLED"'
    if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
        echo "✓ GuardDuty is active"
    fi
else
    echo "✗ GuardDuty not enabled"
fi

# Test 3: Security Hub
echo -e "\n[3] Testing Security Hub..."
aws securityhub describe-hub > /dev/null 2>&1
if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
    echo "✓ Security Hub enabled"
else
    echo "✗ Security Hub not enabled"
fi

# Test 4: Config
echo -e "\n[4] Testing AWS Config..."
aws configservice describe-configuration-recorders | grep -q "ConfigurationRecorder"
if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
    echo "✓ Config enabled"
    
    # Check if recording
    STATUS=$(aws configservice describe-configuration-recorder-status --query 'ConfigurationRecordersStatus[0].recording' --output text)
    if [ "$STATUS" = "True" ]; then
        echo "✓ Config is recording"
    else
        echo "⚠ Config not recording"
    fi
else
    echo "✗ Config not enabled"
fi

# Test 5: CloudTrail
echo -e "\n[5] Testing CloudTrail..."
TRAILS=$(aws cloudtrail describe-trails --query 'trailList[*].Name' --output text)
if [ -n "$TRAILS" ]; then
    echo "✓ CloudTrail configured"
    for trail in $TRAILS; do
        STATUS=$(aws cloudtrail get-trail-status --name $trail --query 'IsLogging' --output text)
        if [ "$STATUS" = "True" ]; then
            echo "✓ Trail $trail is logging"
        else
            echo "⚠ Trail $trail not logging"
        fi
    done
else
    echo "✗ No CloudTrail trails found"
fi

# Test 6: VPC Configuration
echo -e "\n[6] Testing VPC Security..."
VPCS=$(aws ec2 describe-vpcs --query 'Vpcs[*].VpcId' --output text)
for vpc in $VPCS; do
    # Check Flow Logs
    FLOW_LOGS=$(aws ec2 describe-flow-logs --filter "Name=resource-id,Values=$vpc" --query 'FlowLogs[*].FlowLogId' --output text)
    if [ -n "$FLOW_LOGS" ]; then
        echo "✓ VPC $vpc has Flow Logs"
    else
        echo "⚠ VPC $vpc missing Flow Logs"
    fi
done

# Test 7: Security Groups
echo -e "\n[7] Testing Security Groups..."
OPEN_SGS=$(aws ec2 describe-security-groups --filters "Name=ip-permission.cidr,Values=0.0.0.0/0" --query 'SecurityGroups[?IpPermissions[?FromPort==`22` || FromPort==`3389`]].GroupId' --output text)
if [ -z "$OPEN_SGS" ]; then
    echo "✓ No security groups with open SSH/RDP"
else
    echo "⚠ Warning: Found security groups with 0.0.0.0/0 on port 22/3389"
    echo "  $OPEN_SGS"
fi

# Test 8: IAM
echo -e "\n[8] Testing IAM Configuration..."
# Check root account MFA
ROOT_MFA=$(aws iam get-account-summary --query 'SummaryMap.AccountMFAEnabled' --output text)
if [ "$ROOT_MFA" = "1" ]; then
    echo "✓ Root account MFA enabled"
else
    echo "✗ Root account MFA NOT enabled - CRITICAL"
fi

# Check password policy
aws iam get-account-password-policy > /dev/null 2>&1
if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
    echo "✓ Password policy configured"
else
    echo "⚠ No password policy set"
fi

echo -e "\n=== Validation Complete ==="
```

## Chapter 6.3: Complete DevSecOps Pipeline

### Executive Summary

**What You'll Build:**
- End-to-end CI/CD pipeline with security gates
- SAST (Static Application Security Testing)
- DAST (Dynamic Application Security Testing)
- SCA (Software Composition Analysis)
- Container security scanning
- Infrastructure as Code scanning
- Automated policy enforcement
- Security dashboard

**Business Value:**
- Shift-left security (catch issues early)
- Reduce vulnerabilities by 80%
- Automated compliance checking
- Faster, safer deployments
- Developer empowerment

**Time to Complete:** 10-14 hours
**Estimated Cost:** $0-500/month (mostly open source)

**vs. Commercial Tools:**
- Snyk Team: $98/developer/month
- Veracode: $50k-$200k/year
- Checkmarx: $75k-$150k/year
- **Our solution: ~$200/month**
- **Savings: $50k-$200k/year**

### Architecture Overview

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    DEVELOPER WORKFLOW                        │
│                                                              │
│  Developer → Git Commit → Push to Branch → Pull Request     │
└────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                         │
                    ┌────▼────────────────────────────┐
                    │     GitHub Actions / Jenkins    │
                    │     (CI/CD Orchestration)       │
                    └────┬────────────────────────────┘
                         │
         ┌───────────────┼───────────────┬────────────────┐
         │               │               │                │
    ┌────▼────┐    ┌────▼────┐    ┌────▼────┐    ┌─────▼─────┐
    │  SAST   │    │   SCA   │    │  IaC    │    │  Secret   │
    │SonarQube│    │ Trivy   │    │Checkov  │    │  Detect   │
    └────┬────┘    └────┬────┘    └────┬────┘    └─────┬─────┘
         │               │               │                │
         └───────────────┴───────────────┴────────────────┘
                         │
                    ┌────▼──────────┐
                    │  BUILD IMAGE  │
                    │  (Docker)     │
                    └────┬──────────┘
                         │
         ┌───────────────┼───────────────┐
         │               │               │
    ┌────▼────┐    ┌────▼────┐    ┌────▼────┐
    │Container│    │  Sign   │    │  Push   │
    │  Scan   │    │ Image   │    │   to    │
    │ (Trivy) │    │(Cosign) │    │Registry │
    └────┬────┘    └────┬────┘    └────┬────┘
         │               │               │
         └───────────────┴───────────────┘
                         │
                    ┌────▼──────────┐
                    │    DEPLOY     │
                    │  (Staging)    │
                    └────┬──────────┘
                         │
                    ┌────▼──────────┐
                    │     DAST      │
                    │  OWASP ZAP    │
                    └────┬──────────┘
                         │
                    ┌────▼──────────┐
                    │  PRODUCTION   │
                    │   DEPLOY      │
                    └───────────────┘
```

**Security Gates:**

1. **Pre-Commit:** Git hooks, secret scanning
2. **Commit:** SAST, SCA, linting
3. **Build:** Container scanning, image signing
4. **Pre-Deploy:** DAST, penetration testing
5. **Deploy:** Policy enforcement, compliance checks
6. **Runtime:** Monitoring, anomaly detection
