### Phase 3: Container & Cloud Scanning (2-3 hours)

**Step 3.1: Container Image Scanner**

```python
# File: vuln-platform/scanners/container_scanner.py
"""
Container image vulnerability scanning with Trivy
"""

import subprocess
import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class ContainerScanner:
    """Container vulnerability scanning"""
    
    def __init__(self, trivy_cmd='trivy'):
        self.trivy_cmd = trivy_cmd
    
    def scan_image(self, image_name, severity_threshold='MEDIUM'):
        """Scan container image for vulnerabilities"""
        
        logger.info(f"Scanning container image: {image_name}")
        
        results = {
            'image': image_name,
            'scan_date': datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
            'vulnerabilities': [],
            'summary': {
                'critical': 0,
                'high': 0,
                'medium': 0,
                'low': 0
            }
        }
        
        try:
            # Run Trivy scan
            cmd = [
                self.trivy_cmd, 'image',
                '--format', 'json',
                '--severity', f'{severity_threshold},HIGH,CRITICAL',
                image_name
            ]
            
            process = subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=300
            )
            
            if process.returncode == 0:
                scan_data = json.loads(process.stdout)
                
                # Process results
                for result in scan_data.get('Results', []):
                    target = result.get('Target', '')
                    
                    for vuln in result.get('Vulnerabilities', []):
                        vulnerability = {
                            'vulnerability_id': vuln.get('VulnerabilityID'),
                            'pkg_name': vuln.get('PkgName'),
                            'installed_version': vuln.get('InstalledVersion'),
                            'fixed_version': vuln.get('FixedVersion'),
                            'severity': vuln.get('Severity'),
                            'title': vuln.get('Title'),
                            'description': vuln.get('Description'),
                            'references': vuln.get('References', []),
                            'cvss_score': vuln.get('CVSS', {}).get('nvd', {}).get('V3Score', 0),
                            'target': target
                        }
                        
                        results['vulnerabilities'].append(vulnerability)
                        
                        # Update summary
                        severity = vuln.get('Severity', '').lower()
                        if severity in results['summary']:
                            results['summary'][severity] += 1
                
                logger.info(f"Found {len(results['vulnerabilities'])} vulnerabilities")
            
            else:
                logger.error(f"Trivy scan failed: {process.stderr}")
                results['error'] = process.stderr
        
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Container scan error: {e}")
            results['error'] = str(e)
        
        return results
    
    def scan_dockerfile(self, dockerfile_path):
        """Scan Dockerfile for misconfigurations"""
        
        logger.info(f"Scanning Dockerfile: {dockerfile_path}")
        
        results = {
            'dockerfile': dockerfile_path,
            'scan_date': datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
            'misconfigurations': []
        }
        
        try:
            cmd = [
                self.trivy_cmd, 'config',
                '--format', 'json',
                dockerfile_path
            ]
            
            process = subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=60
            )
            
            if process.returncode == 0:
                scan_data = json.loads(process.stdout)
                
                for result in scan_data.get('Results', []):
                    for misconfig in result.get('Misconfigurations', []):
                        results['misconfigurations'].append({
                            'id': misconfig.get('ID'),
                            'title': misconfig.get('Title'),
                            'severity': misconfig.get('Severity'),
                            'message': misconfig.get('Message'),
                            'resolution': misconfig.get('Resolution')
                        })
                
                logger.info(f"Found {len(results['misconfigurations'])} misconfigurations")
        
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Dockerfile scan error: {e}")
            results['error'] = str(e)
        
        return results
    
    def scan_running_containers(self):
        """Scan all running Docker containers"""
        
        logger.info("Scanning running containers...")
        
        results = []
        
        try:
            # Get list of running containers
            cmd = ['docker', 'ps', '--format', '{{.Image}}']
            
            process = subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=30
            )
            
            if process.returncode == 0:
                images = process.stdout.strip().split('\n')
                
                for image in images:
                    if image:
                        scan_result = self.scan_image(image)
                        results.append(scan_result)
        
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error scanning running containers: {e}")
        
        return results

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    scanner = ContainerScanner()
    
    # Scan an image
    results = scanner.scan_image('nginx:latest')
    
    print(f"Found {len(results['vulnerabilities'])} vulnerabilities")
    print(f"Summary: {results['summary']}")
```

### Phase 4: Risk Prioritization Engine (2 hours)

**Step 4.1: Risk-Based Prioritization**

```python
# File: vuln-platform/risk_engine.py
"""
Risk-based vulnerability prioritization
"""

import requests
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import logging

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class RiskEngine:
    """Calculate risk scores for vulnerabilities"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.epss_cache = {}  # EPSS (Exploit Prediction Scoring System) cache
    
    def calculate_risk_score(self, vulnerability, asset):
        """Calculate comprehensive risk score"""
        
        # Base score from CVSS (0-10)
        cvss_score = vulnerability.get('cvss_score', 0)
        
        # Asset criticality (1-10)
        asset_criticality = asset.get('criticality', 5)
        
        # Exploit availability (0-3)
        exploit_score = self.get_exploit_score(vulnerability)
        
        # EPSS probability (0-1)
        epss_score = self.get_epss_score(vulnerability.get('cve_id'))
        
        # Environment factor (0-2)
        environment_score = self.get_environment_score(asset.get('environment'))
        
        # Calculate weighted risk score (0-100)
        risk_score = (
            (cvss_score * 4) +  # 40% weight
            (asset_criticality * 2) +  # 20% weight
            (exploit_score * 10) +  # 30% weight
            (epss_score * 10) +  # 10% weight (if available)
            (environment_score * 5)  # Bonus for production
        )
        
        # Normalize to 0-100
        risk_score = min(100, risk_score)
        
        return round(risk_score, 2)
    
    def get_exploit_score(self, vulnerability):
        """Get exploit availability score"""
        
        # Check multiple sources
        score = 0
        
        # ExploitDB
        if self.check_exploitdb(vulnerability.get('cve_id')):
            score += 1
        
        # Metasploit
        if self.check_metasploit(vulnerability.get('cve_id')):
            score += 1
        
        # Known exploitation in the wild
        if vulnerability.get('exploited_in_wild'):
            score += 1
        
        return score
    
    def check_exploitdb(self, cve_id):
        """Check if exploit exists in ExploitDB"""
        
        if not cve_id:
            return False
        
        try:
            url = f'https://www.exploit-db.com/search?cve={cve_id}'
            response = requests.get(url, timeout=10)
            
            # Simple check - would need proper parsing
            return 'No Results' not in response.text
        
        except:
            return False
    
    def check_metasploit(self, cve_id):
        """Check if Metasploit module exists"""
        
        if not cve_id:
            return False
        
        # Would query Metasploit database or API
        # Simplified for example
        return False
    
    def get_epss_score(self, cve_id):
        """Get EPSS (Exploit Prediction Scoring System) score"""
        
        if not cve_id:
            return 0
        
        # Check cache
        if cve_id in self.epss_cache:
            return self.epss_cache[cve_id]
        
        try:
            url = f'https://api.first.org/data/v1/epss?cve={cve_id}'
            response = requests.get(url, timeout=10)
            
            if response.status_code == 200:
                data = response.json()
                
                if data.get('data'):
                    epss = float(data['data'][0].get('epss', 0))
                    self.epss_cache[cve_id] = epss
                    return epss
        
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Error fetching EPSS score: {e}")
        
        return 0
    
    def get_environment_score(self, environment):
        """Get environment factor score"""
        
        environment_scores = {
            'production': 2,
            'staging': 1,
            'development': 0.5,
            'test': 0.5
        }
        
        return environment_scores.get(environment, 1)
    
    def prioritize_vulnerabilities(self, vulnerabilities, assets):
        """Prioritize list of vulnerabilities"""
        
        prioritized = []
        
        for vuln in vulnerabilities:
            # Find associated asset
            asset = next(
                (a for a in assets if a['id'] == vuln.get('asset_id')),
                {}
            )
            
            # Calculate risk score
            risk_score = self.calculate_risk_score(vuln, asset)
            
            vuln['risk_score'] = risk_score
            vuln['priority'] = self.get_priority_level(risk_score)
            vuln['sla_hours'] = self.calculate_sla(risk_score, vuln.get('severity'))
            
            prioritized.append(vuln)
        
        # Sort by risk score (descending)
        prioritized.sort(key=lambda x: x['risk_score'], reverse=True)
        
        return prioritized
    
    def get_priority_level(self, risk_score):
        """Determine priority level from risk score"""
        
        if risk_score >= 80:
            return 'critical'
        elif risk_score >= 60:
            return 'high'
        elif risk_score >= 40:
            return 'medium'
        else:
            return 'low'
    
    def calculate_sla(self, risk_score, severity):
        """Calculate remediation SLA in hours"""
        
        # Critical vulnerabilities
        if risk_score >= 80 or severity == 'critical':
            return 24  # 1 day
        
        # High
        elif risk_score >= 60 or severity == 'high':
            return 168  # 7 days
        
        # Medium
        elif risk_score >= 40 or severity == 'medium':
            return 720  # 30 days
        
        # Low
        else:
            return 2160  # 90 days

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    engine = RiskEngine()
    
    # Example vulnerability
    vuln = {
        'cve_id': 'CVE-2021-44228',  # Log4Shell
        'cvss_score': 10.0,
        'severity': 'critical',
        'exploited_in_wild': True
    }
    
    # Example asset
    asset = {
        'id': 1,
        'criticality': 9,
        'environment': 'production'
    }
    
    risk_score = engine.calculate_risk_score(vuln, asset)
    print(f"Risk Score: {risk_score}")
    print(f"Priority: {engine.get_priority_level(risk_score)}")
    print(f"SLA: {engine.calculate_sla(risk_score, vuln['severity'])} hours")
```

### Phase 5: Remediation & Reporting (2 hours)

**Step 5.1: Automated Ticket Creation**

```python
# File: vuln-platform/remediation/ticket_manager.py
"""
Automated ticket creation and tracking
"""

import requests
from jira import JIRA
import logging

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class TicketManager:
    """Manage remediation tickets"""
    
    def __init__(self, system='jira', config=None):
        self.system = system
        self.config = config or {}
        
        if system == 'jira':
            self.jira = JIRA(
                server=self.config.get('url'),
                basic_auth=(
                    self.config.get('username'),
                    self.config.get('password')
                )
            )
    
    def create_ticket(self, vulnerability, asset):
        """Create remediation ticket"""
        
        logger.info(f"Creating ticket for {vulnerability.get('title')}")
        
        if self.system == 'jira':
            return self.create_jira_ticket(vulnerability, asset)
        elif self.system == 'servicenow':
            return self.create_servicenow_ticket(vulnerability, asset)
        
        return None
    
    def create_jira_ticket(self, vulnerability, asset):
        """Create Jira ticket"""
        
        # Prepare ticket data
        summary = f"[Security] {vulnerability.get('title')} on {asset.get('name')}"
        
        description = f"""
h2. Vulnerability Details

*CVE ID:* {vulnerability.get('cve_id', 'N/A')}
*Severity:* {vulnerability.get('severity')}
*CVSS Score:* {vulnerability.get('cvss_score')}
*Risk Score:* {vulnerability.get('risk_score')}

h2. Affected Asset

*Asset:* {asset.get('name')}
*IP Address:* {asset.get('ip_address')}
*Environment:* {asset.get('environment')}
*Owner:* {asset.get('owner')}

h2. Description

{vulnerability.get('description', '')}

h2. Remediation

{vulnerability.get('remediation', '')}

h2. References

{chr(10).join(vulnerability.get('references', []))}

h2. SLA

This vulnerability must be remediated within {vulnerability.get('sla_hours')} hours.
*Due Date:* {vulnerability.get('sla_due_date')}
"""
        
        # Determine priority
        priority_mapping = {
            'critical': 'Highest',
            'high': 'High',
            'medium': 'Medium',
            'low': 'Low'
        }
        
        priority = priority_mapping.get(vulnerability.get('priority'), 'Medium')
        
        # Create issue
        try:
            issue = self.jira.create_issue(
                project=self.config.get('project'),
                summary=summary,
                description=description,
                issuetype={'name': 'Bug'},
                priority={'name': priority},
                labels=['security', 'vulnerability', vulnerability.get('severity')]
            )
            
            logger.info(f"Created Jira ticket: {issue.key}")
            
            return {
                'ticket_id': issue.key,
                'ticket_url': f"{self.config.get('url')}/browse/{issue.key}",
                'status': 'created'
            }
        
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error creating Jira ticket: {e}")
            return {'error': str(e)}
    
    def update_ticket_status(self, ticket_id, status, comment=None):
        """Update ticket status"""
        
        if self.system == 'jira':
            try:
                issue = self.jira.issue(ticket_id)
                
                # Add comment if provided
                if comment:
                    self.jira.add_comment(issue, comment)
                
                # Transition issue
                transitions = self.jira.transitions(issue)
                
                for transition in transitions:
                    if transition['name'].lower() == status.lower():
                        self.jira.transition_issue(issue, transition['id'])
                        logger.info(f"Updated {ticket_id} to {status}")
                        return True
                
                return False
            
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error updating ticket: {e}")
                return False
    
    def close_ticket(self, ticket_id, resolution='Fixed'):
        """Close remediation ticket"""
        
        if self.system == 'jira':
            try:
                issue = self.jira.issue(ticket_id)
                
                # Add final comment
                self.jira.add_comment(
                    issue,
                    'Vulnerability has been remediated and verified. Closing ticket.'
                )
                
                # Close issue
                self.jira.transition_issue(
                    issue,
                    'Close',
                    resolution={'name': resolution}
                )
                
                logger.info(f"Closed ticket {ticket_id}")
                return True
            
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error closing ticket: {e}")
                return False

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    config = {
        'url': 'https://your-domain.atlassian.net',
        'username': 'your-email@example.com',
        'password': 'your-api-token',
        'project': 'SEC'
    }
    
    manager = TicketManager(system='jira', config=config)
    
    vuln = {
        'title': 'Apache Log4j RCE',
        'cve_id': 'CVE-2021-44228',
        'severity': 'critical',
        'cvss_score': 10.0,
        'risk_score': 95,
        'priority': 'critical',
        'sla_hours': 24
    }
    
    asset = {
        'name': 'web-server-01',
        'ip_address': '10.0.1.100',
        'environment': 'production',
        'owner': 'DevOps Team'
    }
    
    result = manager.create_ticket(vuln, asset)
    print(f"Ticket created: {result}")
```

## Chapter 6.9: Identity & Access Management (IAM) Platform

### Executive Summary

- Single Sign-On (SSO) with SAML/OAuth2

- Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)
- Privileged Access Management (PAM)
- Access request workflow
- Audit logging and compliance reporting
- Integration with LDAP/Active Directory

**Business Value:**
- Reduce credential-related breaches by 80%
- Centralized user management
- Automated access provisioning/deprovisioning
- Complete audit trail for compliance
- Reduced help desk tickets (password resets)

### Phase 1: Keycloak SSO Deployment (3-4 hours)

**Step 1.1: Deploy Keycloak Stack**

```yaml
# File: docker-compose-iam.yml
version: '3.8'

services:
  # PostgreSQL for Keycloak
  postgres:
    image: postgres:14
    container_name: iam-postgres
    environment:
      - POSTGRES_DB=keycloak
      - POSTGRES_USER=keycloak
      - POSTGRES_PASSWORD=${DB_PASSWORD}
    volumes:
      - postgres-data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    networks:
      - iam-network
    restart: unless-stopped

  # Keycloak SSO
  keycloak:
    image: quay.io/keycloak/keycloak:latest
    container_name: keycloak
    command: start-dev
    environment:
      - KC_DB=postgres
      - KC_DB_URL=jdbc:postgresql://postgres:5432/keycloak
      - KC_DB_USERNAME=keycloak
      - KC_DB_PASSWORD=${DB_PASSWORD}
      - KEYCLOAK_ADMIN=admin
      - KEYCLOAK_ADMIN_PASSWORD=${ADMIN_PASSWORD}
      - KC_HOSTNAME=localhost
      - KC_HTTP_ENABLED=true
      - KC_PROXY=edge
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
      - "8443:8443"
    depends_on:
      - postgres
    volumes:
      - keycloak-data:/opt/keycloak/data
    networks:
      - iam-network
    restart: unless-stopped

  # HashiCorp Vault for secrets
  vault:
    image: vault:latest
    container_name: vault
    cap_add:
      - IPC_LOCK
    environment:
      - VAULT_ADDR=http://0.0.0.0:8200
      - VAULT_DEV_ROOT_TOKEN_ID=${VAULT_TOKEN}
    ports:
      - "8200:8200"
    volumes:
      - vault-data:/vault/data
      - vault-logs:/vault/logs
    networks:
      - iam-network
    restart: unless-stopped

  # Redis for session management
  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    container_name: iam-redis
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"
    networks:
      - iam-network
    restart: unless-stopped

  # Custom IAM API
  iam-api:
    build: ./iam-platform
    container_name: iam-api
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - redis
      - keycloak
      - vault
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    environment:
      - DATABASE_URL=postgresql://keycloak:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/iam
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379
      - KEYCLOAK_URL=http://keycloak:8080
      - VAULT_ADDR=http://vault:8200
      - VAULT_TOKEN=${VAULT_TOKEN}
    volumes:
      - ./iam-platform:/app
    networks:
      - iam-network
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  postgres-data:
  keycloak-data:
  vault-data:
  vault-logs:

networks:
  iam-network:
    driver: bridge
```

**Deployment Script:**

```bash
# File: deploy-iam.sh
#!/bin/bash

set -e

echo "Deploying Identity & Access Management Platform..."

# Generate passwords
export DB_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 32)
export ADMIN_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)
export VAULT_TOKEN=$(openssl rand -base64 32)

# Save credentials
cat > .env << EOF
DB_PASSWORD=$DB_PASSWORD
ADMIN_PASSWORD=$ADMIN_PASSWORD
VAULT_TOKEN=$VAULT_TOKEN
EOF

chmod 600 .env

# Start services
docker-compose -f docker-compose-iam.yml up -d

echo "Waiting for services to start..."
sleep 60

# Initialize Vault
echo "Initializing Vault..."
```

**Step 1.2: Configure Keycloak Realms and Clients**

```python
# File: iam-platform/keycloak_setup.py
"""
Configure Keycloak realms and clients
"""

from keycloak import KeycloakAdmin
from keycloak import KeycloakOpenIDConnection
import logging

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class KeycloakSetup:
    """Setup and configure Keycloak"""
    
    def __init__(self, server_url, admin_username, admin_password):
        self.keycloak_admin = KeycloakAdmin(
            server_url=server_url,
            username=admin_username,
            password=admin_password,
            realm_name="master",
            verify=True
        )
    
    def create_realm(self, realm_name):
        """Create a new realm"""
        
        logger.info(f"Creating realm: {realm_name}")
        
        realm_config = {
            "realm": realm_name,
            "enabled": True,
            "displayName": realm_name.title(),
            "registrationAllowed": False,
            "loginWithEmailAllowed": True,
            "duplicateEmailsAllowed": False,
            "resetPasswordAllowed": True,
            "editUsernameAllowed": False,
            "bruteForceProtected": True,
            "permanentLockout": False,
            "maxFailureWaitSeconds": 900,
            "minimumQuickLoginWaitSeconds": 60,
            "waitIncrementSeconds": 60,
            "quickLoginCheckMilliSeconds": 1000,
            "maxDeltaTimeSeconds": 43200,
            "failureFactor": 5,
            "sslRequired": "external",
            "passwordPolicy": "length(12) and upperCase(1) and lowerCase(1) and digits(1) and specialChars(1)"
        }
        
        try:
            self.keycloak_admin.create_realm(payload=realm_config, skip_exists=True)
            logger.info(f"✓ Realm created: {realm_name}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error creating realm: {e}")
    
    def create_client(self, realm_name, client_id, redirect_uris):
        """Create OAuth2/OIDC client"""
        
        logger.info(f"Creating client: {client_id}")
        
        self.keycloak_admin.realm_name = realm_name
        
        client_config = {
            "clientId": client_id,
            "enabled": True,
            "protocol": "openid-connect",
            "publicClient": False,
            "redirectUris": redirect_uris,
            "webOrigins": ["+"],
            "standardFlowEnabled": True,
            "implicitFlowEnabled": False,
            "directAccessGrantsEnabled": True,
            "serviceAccountsEnabled": True,
            "authorizationServicesEnabled": True,
            "attributes": {
                "access.token.lifespan": "3600",
                "refresh.token.max.reuse": "0"
            }
        }
        
        try:
            client_uuid = self.keycloak_admin.create_client(payload=client_config, skip_exists=True)
            
            # Get client secret
            client_secret = self.keycloak_admin.get_client_secrets(client_uuid)
            
            logger.info(f"✓ Client created: {client_id}")
            logger.info(f"  Client Secret: {client_secret['value']}")
            
            return client_secret['value']
        
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error creating client: {e}")
            return None
    
    def create_role(self, realm_name, role_name, description=""):
        """Create realm role"""
        
        logger.info(f"Creating role: {role_name}")
        
        self.keycloak_admin.realm_name = realm_name
        
        role_config = {
            "name": role_name,
            "description": description,
            "composite": False,
            "clientRole": False
        }
        
        try:
            self.keycloak_admin.create_realm_role(payload=role_config, skip_exists=True)
            logger.info(f"✓ Role created: {role_name}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error creating role: {e}")
    
    def create_user(self, realm_name, username, email, password, roles=None):
        """Create user with roles"""
        
        logger.info(f"Creating user: {username}")
        
        self.keycloak_admin.realm_name = realm_name
        
        user_config = {
            "username": username,
            "email": email,
            "enabled": True,
            "emailVerified": True,
            "credentials": [
                {
                    "type": "password",
                    "value": password,
                    "temporary": False
                }
            ],
            "requiredActions": []
        }
        
        try:
            user_id = self.keycloak_admin.create_user(payload=user_config)
            
            # Assign roles
            if roles:
                for role_name in roles:
                    role = self.keycloak_admin.get_realm_role(role_name)
                    self.keycloak_admin.assign_realm_roles(
                        user_id=user_id,
                        roles=[role]
                    )
            
            logger.info(f"✓ User created: {username}")
            return user_id
        
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error creating user: {e}")
            return None
    
    def enable_mfa(self, realm_name):
        """Enable MFA for realm"""
        
        logger.info(f"Enabling MFA for realm: {realm_name}")
        
        self.keycloak_admin.realm_name = realm_name
        
        # Get realm
        realm = self.keycloak_admin.get_realm(realm_name)
        
        # Update authentication flows
        realm["otpPolicyType"] = "totp"
        realm["otpPolicyAlgorithm"] = "HmacSHA1"
        realm["otpPolicyDigits"] = 6
        realm["otpPolicyPeriod"] = 30
        
        try:
            self.keycloak_admin.update_realm(realm_name, payload=realm)
            logger.info("✓ MFA enabled")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error enabling MFA: {e}")
    
    def setup_complete_environment(self):
        """Setup complete IAM environment"""
        
        logger.info("Setting up complete IAM environment...")
        
        # Create realm
        self.create_realm("corporate")
        
        # Create clients
        self.create_client(
            "corporate",
            "webapp",
            ["http://localhost:3000/*", "https://app.company.com/*"]
        )
        
        self.create_client(
            "corporate",
            "mobile-app",
            ["myapp://callback"]
        )
        
        # Create roles
        roles = [
            ("admin", "System Administrator"),
            ("user", "Regular User"),
            ("manager", "Team Manager"),
            ("auditor", "Security Auditor"),
            ("developer", "Developer")
        ]
        
        for role_name, description in roles:
            self.create_role("corporate", role_name, description)
        
        # Create sample users
        self.create_user(
            "corporate",
            "admin",
            "admin@company.com",
            "AdminPassword123!",
            roles=["admin"]
        )
        
        self.create_user(
            "corporate",
            "user1",
            "user1@company.com",
            "UserPassword123!",
            roles=["user"]
        )
        
        # Enable MFA
        self.enable_mfa("corporate")
        
        logger.info("✓ Complete IAM environment setup finished!")

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import os
    
    setup = KeycloakSetup(
        server_url=os.getenv('KEYCLOAK_URL', 'http://localhost:8080'),
        admin_username='admin',
        admin_password=os.getenv('ADMIN_PASSWORD')
    )
    
    setup.setup_complete_environment()
```

### Phase 2: Multi-Factor Authentication (2-3 hours)

**Step 2.1: MFA Implementation**

```python
# File: iam-platform/mfa.py
"""
Multi-Factor Authentication implementation
"""

import pyotp
import qrcode
from io import BytesIO
import base64
from twilio.rest import Client
import logging

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class MFAManager:
    """Manage multi-factor authentication"""
    
    def __init__(self, twilio_account_sid=None, twilio_auth_token=None, twilio_phone=None):
        self.twilio_client = None
        self.twilio_phone = twilio_phone
        
        if twilio_account_sid and twilio_auth_token:
            self.twilio_client = Client(twilio_account_sid, twilio_auth_token)
    
    def generate_totp_secret(self, username, issuer="MyApp"):
        """Generate TOTP secret for user"""
        
        secret = pyotp.random_base32()
        
        # Generate provisioning URI
        totp = pyotp.TOTP(secret)
        provisioning_uri = totp.provisioning_uri(
            name=username,
            issuer_name=issuer
        )
        
        # Generate QR code
        qr = qrcode.QRCode(version=1, box_size=10, border=5)
        qr.add_data(provisioning_uri)
        qr.make(fit=True)
        
        img = qr.make_image(fill_color="black", back_color="white")
        
        # Convert to base64
        buffer = BytesIO()
        img.save(buffer, format="PNG")
        qr_code_base64 = base64.b64encode(buffer.getvalue()).decode()
        
        return {
            'secret': secret,
            'provisioning_uri': provisioning_uri,
            'qr_code': qr_code_base64
        }
    
    def verify_totp(self, secret, code):
        """Verify TOTP code"""
        
        totp = pyotp.TOTP(secret)
        return totp.verify(code, valid_window=1)
    
    def send_sms_code(self, phone_number):
        """Send SMS verification code"""
        
        if not self.twilio_client:
            logger.error("Twilio not configured")
            return None
        
        # Generate 6-digit code
        code = pyotp.random_base32()[:6]
        
        try:
            message = self.twilio_client.messages.create(
                body=f"Your verification code is: {code}",
                from_=self.twilio_phone,
                to=phone_number
            )
            
            logger.info(f"SMS sent to {phone_number}: {message.sid}")
            
            return code
        
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error sending SMS: {e}")
            return None
    
    def generate_backup_codes(self, count=10):
        """Generate backup codes"""
        
        codes = []
        
        for _ in range(count):
            # Generate 8-character alphanumeric code
            code = pyotp.random_base32()[:8]
            codes.append(code)
        
        return codes

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    mfa = MFAManager()
    
    # Generate TOTP secret
    result = mfa.generate_totp_secret("user@example.com")
    
    print(f"Secret: {result['secret']}")
    print(f"Scan this QR code with Google Authenticator")
    
    # Verify code
    code = input("Enter TOTP code: ")
    if mfa.verify_totp(result['secret'], code):
        print("✓ Code verified!")
    else:
        print("✗ Invalid code")
```

### Phase 3: Role-Based Access Control (2-3 hours)

**Step 3.1: RBAC Engine**

```python
# File: iam-platform/rbac.py
"""
Role-Based Access Control engine
"""

from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, DateTime, ForeignKey, Table, Boolean
from sqlalchemy.ext.declarative import declarative_base
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from datetime import datetime
import logging

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

Base = declarative_base()

# Association tables
user_roles = Table('user_roles', Base.metadata,
    Column('user_id', Integer, ForeignKey('users.id')),
    Column('role_id', Integer, ForeignKey('roles.id'))
)

role_permissions = Table('role_permissions', Base.metadata,
    Column('role_id', Integer, ForeignKey('roles.id')),
    Column('permission_id', Integer, ForeignKey('permissions.id'))
)

class User(Base):
    """User model"""
    __tablename__ = 'users'
    
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    username = Column(String(255), unique=True, nullable=False)
    email = Column(String(255), unique=True, nullable=False)
    keycloak_id = Column(String(255), unique=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    updated_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    
    roles = relationship("Role", secondary=user_roles, back_populates="users")

class Role(Base):
    """Role model"""
    __tablename__ = 'roles'
    
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = Column(String(100), unique=True, nullable=False)
    description = Column(String(500))
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    
    users = relationship("User", secondary=user_roles, back_populates="roles")
    permissions = relationship("Permission", secondary=role_permissions, back_populates="roles")

class Permission(Base):
    """Permission model"""
    __tablename__ = 'permissions'
    
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    resource = Column(String(100), nullable=False)  # e.g., "users", "reports"
    action = Column(String(50), nullable=False)  # e.g., "read", "write", "delete"
    description = Column(String(500))
    
    roles = relationship("Role", secondary=role_permissions, back_populates="permissions")

class RBACEngine:
    """Role-Based Access Control engine"""
    
    def __init__(self, db_session):
        self.db = db_session
    
    def assign_role(self, user_id, role_name):
        """Assign role to user"""
        
        user = self.db.query(User).filter_by(id=user_id).first()
        role = self.db.query(Role).filter_by(name=role_name).first()
        
        if user and role:
            if role not in user.roles:
                user.roles.append(role)
                self.db.commit()
                logger.info(f"Assigned role '{role_name}' to user '{user.username}'")
                return True
        
        return False
    
    def revoke_role(self, user_id, role_name):
        """Revoke role from user"""
        
        user = self.db.query(User).filter_by(id=user_id).first()
        role = self.db.query(Role).filter_by(name=role_name).first()
        
        if user and role:
            if role in user.roles:
                user.roles.remove(role)
                self.db.commit()
                logger.info(f"Revoked role '{role_name}' from user '{user.username}'")
                return True
        
        return False
    
    def check_permission(self, user_id, resource, action):
        """Check if user has permission"""
        
        user = self.db.query(User).filter_by(id=user_id).first()
        
        if not user or not user.is_active:
            return False
        
        # Check all user's roles for permission
        for role in user.roles:
            for permission in role.permissions:
                if permission.resource == resource and permission.action == action:
                    return True
        
        return False
    
    def get_user_permissions(self, user_id):
        """Get all permissions for user"""
        
        user = self.db.query(User).filter_by(id=user_id).first()
        
        if not user:
            return []
        
        permissions = set()
        
        for role in user.roles:
            for permission in role.permissions:
                permissions.add(f"{permission.resource}:{permission.action}")
        
        return list(permissions)
    
    def create_role_with_permissions(self, role_name, description, permission_list):
        """Create role with permissions"""
        
        # Create role
        role = Role(name=role_name, description=description)
        self.db.add(role)
        self.db.flush()
        
        # Add permissions
        for resource, action in permission_list:
            # Check if permission exists
            permission = self.db.query(Permission).filter_by(
                resource=resource,
                action=action
            ).first()
            
            if not permission:
                permission = Permission(resource=resource, action=action)
                self.db.add(permission)
                self.db.flush()
            
            role.permissions.append(permission)
        
        self.db.commit()
        logger.info(f"Created role '{role_name}' with {len(permission_list)} permissions")
        
        return role

# Example: Setup standard roles
def setup_standard_roles(rbac_engine):
    """Setup standard organizational roles"""
    
    roles_config = {
        'admin': {
            'description': 'System Administrator',
            'permissions': [
                ('users', 'create'),
                ('users', 'read'),
                ('users', 'update'),
                ('users', 'delete'),
                ('roles', 'create'),
                ('roles', 'read'),
                ('roles', 'update'),
                ('roles', 'delete'),
                ('settings', 'read'),
                ('settings', 'update'),
                ('audit_logs', 'read')
            ]
        },
        'manager': {
            'description': 'Team Manager',
            'permissions': [
                ('users', 'read'),
                ('users', 'update'),
                ('reports', 'create'),
                ('reports', 'read'),
                ('reports', 'update'),
                ('team', 'read'),
                ('team', 'update')
            ]
        },
        'user': {
            'description': 'Standard User',
            'permissions': [
                ('profile', 'read'),
                ('profile', 'update'),
                ('reports', 'read'),
                ('documents', 'read')
            ]
        },
        'auditor': {
            'description': 'Security Auditor',
            'permissions': [
                ('audit_logs', 'read'),
                ('reports', 'read'),
                ('users', 'read'),
                ('roles', 'read')
            ]
        }
    }
    
    for role_name, config in roles_config.items():
        rbac_engine.create_role_with_permissions(
            role_name,
            config['description'],
            config['permissions']
        )
```

### Phase 4: Privileged Access Management (3-4 hours)

**Step 4.1: HashiCorp Vault Integration**

```python
# File: iam-platform/pam.py
"""
Privileged Access Management with HashiCorp Vault
"""

import hvac
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class PAMManager:
    """Manage privileged access with Vault"""
    
    def __init__(self, vault_url, vault_token):
        self.client = hvac.Client(url=vault_url, token=vault_token)
        
        if not self.client.is_authenticated():
            raise Exception("Vault authentication failed")
    
    def enable_database_secrets(self):
        """Enable database secrets engine"""
        
        try:
            self.client.sys.enable_secrets_engine(
                backend_type='database',
                path='database'
            )
            logger.info("✓ Database secrets engine enabled")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Database secrets engine may already be enabled: {e}")
    
    def configure_database(self, name, connection_url, username, password):
        """Configure database connection"""
        
        config = {
            'plugin_name': 'postgresql-database-plugin',
            'allowed_roles': ['readonly', 'readwrite'],
            'connection_url': connection_url,
            'username': username,
            'password': password
        }
        
        try:
            self.client.secrets.database.configure(
                name=name,
                config=config
            )
            logger.info(f"✓ Database '{name}' configured")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error configuring database: {e}")
    
    def create_database_role(self, role_name, db_name, creation_statements, default_ttl=3600):
        """Create dynamic database role"""
        
        config = {
            'db_name': db_name,
            'creation_statements': creation_statements,
            'default_ttl': default_ttl,
            'max_ttl': default_ttl * 2
        }
        
        try:
            self.client.secrets.database.create_role(
                name=role_name,
                **config
            )
            logger.info(f"✓ Database role '{role_name}' created")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error creating database role: {e}")
    
    def get_dynamic_credentials(self, role_name):
        """Get dynamic database credentials"""
        
        try:
            creds = self.client.secrets.database.generate_credentials(
                name=role_name
            )
            
            logger.info(f"✓ Generated credentials for role '{role_name}'")
            
            return {
                'username': creds['data']['username'],
                'password': creds['data']['password'],
                'lease_id': creds['lease_id'],
                'lease_duration': creds['lease_duration']
            }
        
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error generating credentials: {e}")
            return None
    
    def enable_ssh_secrets(self):
        """Enable SSH secrets engine"""
        
        try:
            self.client.sys.enable_secrets_engine(
                backend_type='ssh',
                path='ssh'
            )
            logger.info("✓ SSH secrets engine enabled")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"SSH secrets engine may already be enabled: {e}")
    
    def configure_ssh_ca(self):
        """Configure SSH Certificate Authority"""
        
        try:
            # Generate CA keys
            self.client.secrets.ssh.configure_ca(
                mount_point='ssh'
            )
            logger.info("✓ SSH CA configured")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error configuring SSH CA: {e}")
    
    def create_ssh_role(self, role_name, allowed_users="*", default_user="ubuntu", ttl=3600):
        """Create SSH role"""
        
        config = {
            'key_type': 'ca',
            'allowed_users': allowed_users,
            'default_user': default_user,
            'ttl': ttl,
            'max_ttl': ttl * 2,
            'allow_user_certificates': True
        }
        
        try:
            self.client.secrets.ssh.create_role(
                name=role_name,
                **config
            )
            logger.info(f"✓ SSH role '{role_name}' created")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error creating SSH role: {e}")
    
    def sign_ssh_key(self, role_name, public_key, valid_principals):
        """Sign SSH public key"""
        
        try:
            result = self.client.secrets.ssh.sign_ssh_key(
                name=role_name,
                public_key=public_key,
                valid_principals=valid_principals
            )
            
            logger.info(f"✓ SSH key signed for role '{role_name}'")
            
            return result['data']['signed_key']
        
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error signing SSH key: {e}")
            return None
    
    def store_secret(self, path, secret_data):
        """Store static secret"""
        
        try:
            self.client.secrets.kv.v2.create_or_update_secret(
                path=path,
                secret=secret_data
            )
            logger.info(f"✓ Secret stored at '{path}'")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error storing secret: {e}")
    
    def retrieve_secret(self, path):
        """Retrieve static secret"""
        
        try:
            secret = self.client.secrets.kv.v2.read_secret_version(
                path=path
            )
            return secret['data']['data']
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error retrieving secret: {e}")
            return None
    
    def rotate_secret(self, path):
        """Rotate secret"""
        
        # Implementation would depend on secret type
        logger.info(f"Rotating secret at '{path}'")
        pass

# Usage example
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import os
    
    pam = PAMManager(
        vault_url=os.getenv('VAULT_ADDR', 'http://localhost:8200'),
        vault_token=os.getenv('VAULT_TOKEN')
    )
    
    # Setup database secrets
    pam.enable_database_secrets()
    
    pam.configure_database(
        name='postgres-prod',
        connection_url='postgresql://{{username}}:{{password}}@postgres:5432/mydb',
        username='vault',
        password='vaultpass'
    )
    
    # Create readonly role
    pam.create_database_role(
        role_name='readonly',
        db_name='postgres-prod',
        creation_statements=[
            "CREATE ROLE \"{{name}}\" WITH LOGIN PASSWORD '{{password}}' VALID UNTIL '{{expiration}}';",
            "GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO \"{{name}}\";"
        ],
        default_ttl=3600
    )
    
    # Get dynamic credentials
    creds = pam.get_dynamic_credentials('readonly')
    print(f"Username: {creds['username']}")
    print(f"Password: {creds['password']}")
    print(f"Valid for: {creds['lease_duration']} seconds")
```

### Phase 5: Access Request Workflow (2 hours)

**Step 5.1: Self-Service Access Portal**

```python
# File: iam-platform/access_requests.py
"""
Access request and approval workflow
"""

from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, DateTime, Text, Enum as SQLEnum, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import enum
import logging

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class RequestStatus(enum.Enum):
    PENDING = "pending"
    APPROVED = "approved"
    REJECTED = "rejected"
    EXPIRED = "expired"

class AccessRequest(Base):
    """Access request model"""
    __tablename__ = 'access_requests'
    
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey('users.id'), nullable=False)
    resource = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    access_level = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    justification = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    status = Column(SQLEnum(RequestStatus), default=RequestStatus.PENDING)
    requested_duration = Column(Integer)  # hours
    requested_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    approved_by = Column(Integer, ForeignKey('users.id'))
    approved_at = Column(DateTime)
    expires_at = Column(DateTime)
    rejection_reason = Column(Text)
    
    user = relationship("User", foreign_keys=[user_id])
    approver = relationship("User", foreign_keys=[approved_by])

class AccessWorkflow:
    """Manage access request workflow"""
    
    def __init__(self, db_session):
        self.db = db_session
    
    def create_request(self, user_id, resource, access_level, justification, duration_hours=8):
        """Create new access request"""
        
        request = AccessRequest(
            user_id=user_id,
            resource=resource,
            access_level=access_level,
            justification=justification,
            requested_duration=duration_hours
        )
        
        self.db.add(request)
        self.db.commit()
        
        logger.info(f"Access request created: #{request.id}")
        
        # Send notification to approvers
        self.notify_approvers(request)
        
        return request
    
    def approve_request(self, request_id, approver_id):
        """Approve access request"""
        
        request = self.db.query(AccessRequest).filter_by(id=request_id).first()
        
        if not request or request.status != RequestStatus.PENDING:
            return False
        
        request.status = RequestStatus.APPROVED
        request.approved_by = approver_id
        request.approved_at = datetime.utcnow()
        request.expires_at = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(hours=request.requested_duration)
        
        self.db.commit()
        
        logger.info(f"Access request #{request_id} approved")
        
        # Grant access
        self.grant_access(request)
        
        # Notify requester
        self.notify_requester(request, approved=True)
        
        return True
    
    def reject_request(self, request_id, approver_id, reason):
        """Reject access request"""
        
        request = self.db.query(AccessRequest).filter_by(id=request_id).first()
        
        if not request or request.status != RequestStatus.PENDING:
            return False
        
        request.status = RequestStatus.REJECTED
        request.approved_by = approver_id
        request.approved_at = datetime.utcnow()
        request.rejection_reason = reason
        
        self.db.commit()
        
        logger.info(f"Access request #{request_id} rejected")
        
        # Notify requester
        self.notify_requester(request, approved=False)
        
        return True
    
    def grant_access(self, request):
        """Grant temporary access"""
        
        # Implementation depends on resource type
        # Could integrate with Vault for dynamic credentials
        # Could assign temporary role in Keycloak
        
        logger.info(f"Granting access to {request.resource} for user {request.user_id}")
        
        # Example: Store temporary access grant
        # This would be checked by authorization middleware
        pass
    
    def revoke_expired_access(self):
        """Revoke access for expired requests"""
        
        now = datetime.utcnow()
        
        expired = self.db.query(AccessRequest).filter(
            AccessRequest.status == RequestStatus.APPROVED,
            AccessRequest.expires_at <= now
        ).all()
        
        for request in expired:
            request.status = RequestStatus.EXPIRED
            
            # Revoke access
            logger.info(f"Revoking expired access for request #{request.id}")
            
            # Implementation would revoke actual access
        
        self.db.commit()
        
        return len(expired)
    
    def notify_approvers(self, request):
        """Notify approvers of new request"""
        
        # Send email/Slack notification
        logger.info(f"Notifying approvers for request #{request.id}")
    
    def notify_requester(self, request, approved):
        """Notify requester of decision"""
        
        status = "approved" if approved else "rejected"
        logger.info(f"Notifying user {request.user_id} - request {status}")
```

### Phase 1: SAST Platform Deployment (3-4 hours)

**Step 1.1: Deploy SonarQube Stack**

```yaml
# File: docker-compose-appsec.yml
version: '3.8'

services:
  # PostgreSQL for SonarQube
  postgres:
    image: postgres:14
    container_name: appsec-postgres
    environment:
      - POSTGRES_USER=sonar
      - POSTGRES_PASSWORD=${DB_PASSWORD}
      - POSTGRES_DB=sonarqube
    volumes:
      - postgres-data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    networks:
      - appsec-network
    restart: unless-stopped

  # SonarQube
  sonarqube:
    image: sonarqube:community
    container_name: sonarqube
    depends_on:
      - postgres
    environment:
      - SONAR_JDBC_URL=jdbc:postgresql://postgres:5432/sonarqube
      - SONAR_JDBC_USERNAME=sonar
      - SONAR_JDBC_PASSWORD=${DB_PASSWORD}
    ports:
      - "9000:9000"
    volumes:
      - sonarqube-data:/opt/sonarqube/data
      - sonarqube-extensions:/opt/sonarqube/extensions
      - sonarqube-logs:/opt/sonarqube/logs
    networks:
      - appsec-network
    restart: unless-stopped

  # OWASP ZAP for DAST
  zap:
    image: owasp/zap2docker-stable
    container_name: owasp-zap
    command: zap-webswing.sh
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
      - "8090:8090"
    networks:
      - appsec-network
    restart: unless-stopped

  # Dependency-Check
  dependency-check:
    image: owasp/dependency-check:latest
    container_name: dependency-check
    volumes:
      - ./projects:/src
      - dependency-check-data:/usr/share/dependency-check/data
    networks:
      - appsec-network

  # AppSec Dashboard (custom)
  appsec-dashboard:
    build: ./appsec-dashboard
    container_name: appsec-dashboard
    depends_on:
      - postgres
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    environment:
      - DATABASE_URL=postgresql://sonar:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/appsec
      - SONARQUBE_URL=http://sonarqube:9000
      - ZAP_URL=http://zap:8080
    volumes:
      - ./appsec-dashboard:/app
    networks:
      - appsec-network
    restart: unless-stopped

  # Semgrep for custom rules
  semgrep:
    image: returntocorp/semgrep:latest
    container_name: semgrep
    volumes:
      - ./projects:/src
      - ./semgrep-rules:/rules
    networks:
      - appsec-network

volumes:
  postgres-data:
  sonarqube-data:
  sonarqube-extensions:
  sonarqube-logs:
  dependency-check-data:

networks:
  appsec-network:
    driver: bridge
```

**Deployment Script:**

```bash
# File: deploy-appsec-lab.sh
#!/bin/bash

set -e

echo "Deploying Application Security Testing Lab..."

# Generate password
export DB_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 32)

# Save credentials
cat > .env << EOF
DB_PASSWORD=$DB_PASSWORD
EOF

chmod 600 .env

# Create directories
mkdir -p projects semgrep-rules appsec-dashboard

# Start services
docker-compose -f docker-compose-appsec.yml up -d

echo "Waiting for services to start..."
sleep 60

# Get SonarQube initial token
echo "Generating SonarQube token..."
TOKEN=$(curl -u admin:admin -X POST "http://localhost:9000/api/user_tokens/generate?name=initial-token" | jq -r '.token')

echo ""
echo "✓ AppSec Testing Lab deployed!"
echo ""
echo "SonarQube: http://localhost:9000"
echo "  Initial credentials: admin/admin"
echo "  Token: $TOKEN"
echo ""
echo "OWASP ZAP: http://localhost:8080"
echo ""
echo "Dashboard: http://localhost:3000"
```

---

**Step 1.2: Semgrep Custom Rules**

```yaml
# File: semgrep-rules/security-rules.yml
rules:
  - id: hardcoded-secret
    patterns:
      - pattern: password = "..."
      - pattern: api_key = "..."
      - pattern: secret = "..."
    message: Hardcoded secret detected
    severity: ERROR
    languages: [python, javascript, java]

  - id: sql-injection
    patterns:
      - pattern: execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = " + $USER_INPUT)
      - pattern: cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {$TABLE} WHERE ...")
    message: Possible SQL injection vulnerability
    severity: ERROR
    languages: [python, javascript]

  - id: command-injection
    patterns:
      - pattern: os.system($USER_INPUT)
      - pattern: subprocess.call($USER_INPUT, shell=True)
      - pattern: exec($USER_INPUT)
    message: Command injection vulnerability
    severity: ERROR
    languages: [python]

  - id: xss-vulnerability
    patterns:
      - pattern: innerHTML = $USER_INPUT
      - pattern: document.write($USER_INPUT)
      - pattern: eval($USER_INPUT)
    message: Cross-site scripting (XSS) vulnerability
    severity: ERROR
    languages: [javascript]

  - id: insecure-random
    patterns:
      - pattern: random.random()
      - pattern: Math.random()
    message: Using insecure random number generator for security
    severity: WARNING
    languages: [python, javascript]

  - id: weak-crypto
    patterns:
      - pattern: hashlib.md5(...)
      - pattern: hashlib.sha1(...)
    message: Weak cryptographic algorithm
    severity: WARNING
    languages: [python]

  - id: unvalidated-redirect
    patterns:
      - pattern: redirect($USER_INPUT)
      - pattern: window.location = $USER_INPUT
    message: Unvalidated redirect vulnerability
    severity: ERROR
    languages: [python, javascript]

  - id: missing-csrf-protection
    patterns:
      - pattern: |
          @app.route(...)
          def $FUNC(...):
            ...
      - pattern-not: |
          @csrf.exempt
    message: Endpoint missing CSRF protection
    severity: WARNING
    languages: [python]

  - id: debug-mode-enabled
    patterns:
      - pattern: DEBUG = True
      - pattern: app.debug = True
    message: Debug mode should not be enabled in production
    severity: ERROR
    languages: [python]

  - id: unsafe-yaml-load
    patterns:
      - pattern: yaml.load($DATA)
      - pattern-not: yaml.load($DATA, Loader=yaml.SafeLoader)
    message: Unsafe YAML deserialization
    severity: ERROR
    languages: [python]
```

### Phase 2: Automated Security Testing (3-4 hours)

**Step 2.1: Security Testing Orchestrator**

```python
# File: appsec-dashboard/security_scanner.py
"""
Orchestrate security testing across multiple tools
"""

import subprocess
import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class SecurityScanner:
    """Orchestrate security scans"""
    
    def __init__(self, project_path, project_name):
        self.project_path = project_path
        self.project_name = project_name
        self.results = {
            'sast': [],
            'dast': [],
            'sca': [],
            'secrets': []
        }
    
    def run_semgrep(self):
        """Run Semgrep SAST scan"""
        
        logger.info("Running Semgrep scan...")
        
        try:
            cmd = [
                'semgrep',
                '--config', '/rules/security-rules.yml',
                '--json',
                self.project_path
            ]
            
            result = subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=600
            )
            
            if result.returncode == 0 or result.returncode == 1:  # 1 = findings
                data = json.loads(result.stdout)
                
                findings = []
                for finding in data.get('results', []):
                    findings.append({
                        'tool': 'Semgrep',
                        'type': 'SAST',
                        'rule_id': finding['check_id'],
                        'severity': finding['extra']['severity'],
                        'message': finding['extra']['message'],
                        'file': finding['path'],
                        'line': finding['start']['line'],
                        'code': finding['extra'].get('lines', '')
                    })
                
                self.results['sast'].extend(findings)
                logger.info(f"Semgrep found {len(findings)} issues")
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Semgrep scan failed: {e}")
    
    def run_bandit(self):
        """Run Bandit for Python"""
        
        logger.info("Running Bandit scan...")
        
        try:
            cmd = [
                'bandit',
                '-r', self.project_path,
                '-f', 'json',
                '-ll'  # Only medium and high severity
            ]
            
            result = subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=300
            )
            
            if result.stdout:
                data = json.loads(result.stdout)
                
                findings = []
                for finding in data.get('results', []):
                    findings.append({
                        'tool': 'Bandit',
                        'type': 'SAST',
                        'rule_id': finding['test_id'],
                        'severity': finding['issue_severity'],
                        'message': finding['issue_text'],
                        'file': finding['filename'],
                        'line': finding['line_number'],
                        'code': finding['code']
                    })
                
                self.results['sast'].extend(findings)
                logger.info(f"Bandit found {len(findings)} issues")
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Bandit scan failed: {e}")
    
    def run_dependency_check(self):
        """Run OWASP Dependency-Check"""
        
        logger.info("Running Dependency-Check scan...")
        
        try:
            cmd = [
                'dependency-check',
                '--project', self.project_name,
                '--scan', self.project_path,
                '--format', 'JSON',
                '--prettyPrint'
            ]
            
            result = subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=900
            )
            
            # Parse JSON output
            if result.returncode == 0:
                # Read generated report
                report_path = f'dependency-check-report.json'
                
                try:
                    with open(report_path, 'r') as f:
                        data = json.load(f)
                    
                    findings = []
                    
                    for dependency in data.get('dependencies', []):
                        for vulnerability in dependency.get('vulnerabilities', []):
                            findings.append({
                                'tool': 'Dependency-Check',
                                'type': 'SCA',
                                'cve': vulnerability.get('name'),
                                'severity': vulnerability.get('severity'),
                                'description': vulnerability.get('description'),
                                'dependency': dependency.get('fileName'),
                                'cvss_score': vulnerability.get('cvssv3', {}).get('baseScore', 0)
                            })
                    
                    self.results['sca'].extend(findings)
                    logger.info(f"Dependency-Check found {len(findings)} vulnerabilities")
                    
                except FileNotFoundError:
                    logger.warning("Dependency-Check report not found")
                    
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Dependency-Check failed: {e}")
    
    def run_secret_scan(self):
        """Scan for hardcoded secrets"""
        
        logger.info("Scanning for secrets...")
        
        try:
            # Using Gitleaks
            cmd = [
                'gitleaks',
                'detect',
                '--source', self.project_path,
                '--report-format', 'json',
                '--report-path', 'gitleaks-report.json'
            ]
            
            result = subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=300
            )
            
            try:
                with open('gitleaks-report.json', 'r') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                
                findings = []
                for finding in data:
                    findings.append({
                        'tool': 'Gitleaks',
                        'type': 'Secret',
                        'rule_id': finding.get('RuleID'),
                        'description': finding.get('Description'),
                        'file': finding.get('File'),
                        'line': finding.get('StartLine'),
                        'secret': finding.get('Secret')[:20] + '...'  # Truncate
                    })
                
                self.results['secrets'].extend(findings)
                logger.info(f"Found {len(findings)} potential secrets")
                
            except FileNotFoundError:
                logger.warning("No secrets report found")
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Secret scan failed: {e}")
    
    def run_zap_scan(self, target_url):
        """Run OWASP ZAP DAST scan"""
        
        logger.info(f"Running ZAP scan on {target_url}...")
        
        try:
            from zapv2 import ZAPv2
            
            zap = ZAPv2(proxies={'http': 'http://zap:8080', 'https': 'http://zap:8080'})
            
            # Spider
            scan_id = zap.spider.scan(target_url)
            while int(zap.spider.status(scan_id)) < 100:
                logger.info(f"Spider progress: {zap.spider.status(scan_id)}%")
                import time
                time.sleep(5)
            
            # Active scan
            scan_id = zap.ascan.scan(target_url)
            while int(zap.ascan.status(scan_id)) < 100:
                logger.info(f"Scan progress: {zap.ascan.status(scan_id)}%")
                time.sleep(10)
            
            # Get alerts
            alerts = zap.core.alerts(baseurl=target_url)
            
            findings = []
            for alert in alerts:
                findings.append({
                    'tool': 'OWASP ZAP',
                    'type': 'DAST',
                    'name': alert.get('alert'),
                    'risk': alert.get('risk'),
                    'confidence': alert.get('confidence'),
                    'url': alert.get('url'),
                    'description': alert.get('description'),
                    'solution': alert.get('solution')
                })
            
            self.results['dast'].extend(findings)
            logger.info(f"ZAP found {len(findings)} vulnerabilities")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"ZAP scan failed: {e}")
    
    def generate_report(self):
        """Generate consolidated security report"""
        
        report = {
            'project': self.project_name,
            'scan_date': datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
            'summary': {
                'total_findings': sum(len(v) for v in self.results.values()),
                'sast_findings': len(self.results['sast']),
                'dast_findings': len(self.results['dast']),
                'sca_findings': len(self.results['sca']),
                'secret_findings': len(self.results['secrets'])
            },
            'findings': self.results
        }
        
        # Calculate severity distribution
        severity_count = {'CRITICAL': 0, 'HIGH': 0, 'MEDIUM': 0, 'LOW': 0}
        
        for category in self.results.values():
            for finding in category:
                severity = finding.get('severity', finding.get('risk', 'UNKNOWN')).upper()
                if severity in severity_count:
                    severity_count[severity] += 1
        
        report['severity_distribution'] = severity_count
        
        # Save report
        report_file = f'{self.project_name}_security_report_{datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")}.json'
        
        with open(report_file, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(report, f, indent=2)
        
        logger.info(f"Report saved: {report_file}")
        
        return report
    
    def run_full_scan(self, include_dast=False, target_url=None):
        """Run complete security scan"""
        
        logger.info(f"Starting full security scan for {self.project_name}")
        
        # SAST
        self.run_semgrep()
        self.run_bandit()
        
        # SCA
        self.run_dependency_check()
        
        # Secrets
        self.run_secret_scan()
        
        # DAST (optional)
        if include_dast and target_url:
            self.run_zap_scan(target_url)
        
        # Generate report
        report = self.generate_report()
        
        logger.info("✓ Full security scan complete")
        
        return report

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    scanner = SecurityScanner(
        project_path='/src/myapp',
        project_name='MyWebApp'
    )
    
    report = scanner.run_full_scan(
        include_dast=True,
        target_url='http://localhost:8000'
    )
    
    print(f"Total findings: {report['summary']['total_findings']}")
    print(f"Critical/High: {report['severity_distribution']['CRITICAL'] + report['severity_distribution']['HIGH']}")
```
