## Chapter 6.10: Application Security Testing Lab - Continued

### Phase 3: API Security Testing (2-3 hours)

**Step 3.1: API Security Scanner**

```python
# File: appsec-dashboard/api_security.py
"""
API security testing automation
"""

import requests
import json
import logging
from urllib.parse import urljoin

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class APISecurityTester:
    """Automated API security testing"""
    
    def __init__(self, base_url, api_spec=None):
        self.base_url = base_url
        self.api_spec = api_spec  # OpenAPI/Swagger spec
        self.findings = []
    
    def test_authentication(self):
        """Test authentication mechanisms"""
        
        logger.info("Testing authentication...")
        
        tests = [
            {
                'name': 'Missing authentication',
                'method': 'GET',
                'endpoint': '/api/users',
                'headers': {},
                'expected_status': [401, 403]
            },
            {
                'name': 'Invalid token',
                'method': 'GET',
                'endpoint': '/api/users',
                'headers': {'Authorization': 'Bearer invalid_token'},
                'expected_status': [401, 403]
            },
            {
                'name': 'Expired token',
                'method': 'GET',
                'endpoint': '/api/users',
                'headers': {'Authorization': 'Bearer eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9...'},
                'expected_status': [401, 403]
            }
        ]
        
        for test in tests:
            url = urljoin(self.base_url, test['endpoint'])
            
            try:
                response = requests.request(
                    method=test['method'],
                    url=url,
                    headers=test['headers'],
                    timeout=10
                )
                
                if response.status_code not in test['expected_status']:
                    self.findings.append({
                        'category': 'Authentication',
                        'severity': 'HIGH',
                        'title': f"{test['name']} - Unexpected response",
                        'description': f"Expected {test['expected_status']}, got {response.status_code}",
                        'endpoint': test['endpoint']
                    })
            
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error testing {test['name']}: {e}")
    
    def test_authorization(self):
        """Test authorization controls"""
        
        logger.info("Testing authorization...")
        
        # Test IDOR (Insecure Direct Object Reference)
        endpoints = [
            '/api/users/1',
            '/api/users/2',
            '/api/orders/1',
            '/api/orders/2'
        ]
        
        for endpoint in endpoints:
            url = urljoin(self.base_url, endpoint)
            
            try:
                # Test access with different user tokens
                response = requests.get(
                    url,
                    headers={'Authorization': 'Bearer user_1_token'},
                    timeout=10
                )
                
                if response.status_code == 200:
                    # Check if user can access other user's data
                    if '/users/2' in endpoint or '/orders/2' in endpoint:
                        self.findings.append({
                            'category': 'Authorization',
                            'severity': 'HIGH',
                            'title': 'Insecure Direct Object Reference (IDOR)',
                            'description': 'User can access resources belonging to other users',
                            'endpoint': endpoint
                        })
            
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error testing authorization on {endpoint}: {e}")
    
    def test_injection(self):
        """Test for injection vulnerabilities"""
        
        logger.info("Testing for injection vulnerabilities...")
        
        # SQL Injection payloads
        sql_payloads = [
            "' OR '1'='1",
            "1' UNION SELECT NULL--",
            "1'; DROP TABLE users--",
            "admin'--"
        ]
        
        # Test endpoints
        test_endpoints = [
            '/api/users?id={payload}',
            '/api/search?q={payload}',
            '/api/login'
        ]
        
        for endpoint_template in test_endpoints:
            for payload in sql_payloads:
                if '{payload}' in endpoint_template:
                    endpoint = endpoint_template.format(payload=payload)
                    url = urljoin(self.base_url, endpoint)
                    
                    try:
                        response = requests.get(url, timeout=10)
                        
                        # Check for SQL error messages
                        error_indicators = [
                            'sql syntax',
                            'mysql',
                            'postgresql',
                            'ora-',
                            'sqlite',
                            'syntax error'
                        ]
                        
                        response_text = response.text.lower()
                        
                        for indicator in error_indicators:
                            if indicator in response_text:
                                self.findings.append({
                                    'category': 'Injection',
                                    'severity': 'CRITICAL',
                                    'title': 'SQL Injection vulnerability detected',
                                    'description': f'SQL error message exposed with payload: {payload}',
                                    'endpoint': endpoint
                                })
                                break
                    
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(f"Error testing injection on {endpoint}: {e}")
    
    def test_rate_limiting(self):
        """Test rate limiting"""
        
        logger.info("Testing rate limiting...")
        
        endpoint = '/api/login'
        url = urljoin(self.base_url, endpoint)
        
        # Send 100 requests rapidly
        request_count = 100
        blocked_count = 0
        
        for i in range(request_count):
            try:
                response = requests.post(
                    url,
                    json={'username': 'test', 'password': 'test'},
                    timeout=5
                )
                
                if response.status_code == 429:  # Too Many Requests
                    blocked_count += 1
            
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error in rate limit test: {e}")
        
        if blocked_count == 0:
            self.findings.append({
                'category': 'Rate Limiting',
                'severity': 'MEDIUM',
                'title': 'No rate limiting detected',
                'description': f'Sent {request_count} requests without rate limiting',
                'endpoint': endpoint
            })
    
    def test_cors(self):
        """Test CORS configuration"""
        
        logger.info("Testing CORS configuration...")
        
        url = urljoin(self.base_url, '/api/users')
        
        try:
            response = requests.get(
                url,
                headers={'Origin': 'https://evil.com'},
                timeout=10
            )
            
            # Check Access-Control-Allow-Origin header
            acao = response.headers.get('Access-Control-Allow-Origin')
            
            if acao == '*':
                self.findings.append({
                    'category': 'CORS',
                    'severity': 'MEDIUM',
                    'title': 'Overly permissive CORS policy',
                    'description': 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin is set to *',
                    'endpoint': '/api/*'
                })
            elif acao == 'https://evil.com':
                self.findings.append({
                    'category': 'CORS',
                    'severity': 'HIGH',
                    'title': 'CORS reflects arbitrary origins',
                    'description': 'Server reflects Origin header value',
                    'endpoint': '/api/*'
                })
        
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error testing CORS: {e}")
    
    def test_sensitive_data_exposure(self):
        """Test for sensitive data in responses"""
        
        logger.info("Testing for sensitive data exposure...")
        
        endpoints = [
            '/api/users/me',
            '/api/profile',
            '/api/config'
        ]
        
        sensitive_patterns = [
            'password',
            'secret',
            'api_key',
            'token',
            'private_key',
            'ssn',
            'credit_card'
        ]
        
        for endpoint in endpoints:
            url = urljoin(self.base_url, endpoint)
            
            try:
                response = requests.get(url, timeout=10)
                
                if response.status_code == 200:
                    response_text = response.text.lower()
                    
                    for pattern in sensitive_patterns:
                        if pattern in response_text:
                            self.findings.append({
                                'category': 'Data Exposure',
                                'severity': 'MEDIUM',
                                'title': f'Potential {pattern} exposure',
                                'description': f'Response may contain sensitive {pattern} data',
                                'endpoint': endpoint
                            })
            
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error testing {endpoint}: {e}")
    
    def run_full_test(self):
        """Run complete API security test suite"""
        
        logger.info("Starting comprehensive API security tests...")
        
        self.test_authentication()
        self.test_authorization()
        self.test_injection()
        self.test_rate_limiting()
        self.test_cors()
        self.test_sensitive_data_exposure()
        
        logger.info(f"API security testing complete. Found {len(self.findings)} issues")
        
        return self.findings

# Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    tester = APISecurityTester(base_url='http://localhost:8000')
    
    findings = tester.run_full_test()
    
    for finding in findings:
        print(f"{finding['severity']}: {finding['title']}")
```

### Phase 4: CI/CD Integration (1-2 hours)

**Step 4.1: Security Pipeline Integration**

```yaml
# File: .github/workflows/security-scan.yml
name: Security Scan

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main, develop ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main ]
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 2 * * 1'  # Weekly on Monday

jobs:
  security-scan:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    
    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v3
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0  # Full history for accurate analysis
      
      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v4
        with:
          python-version: '3.9'
      
      - name: Install security tools
        run: |
          pip install bandit semgrep gitleaks
          npm install -g snyk
      
      - name: Run Semgrep
        run: |
          semgrep --config=auto --json --output=semgrep-results.json .
      
      - name: Run Bandit
        run: |
          bandit -r . -f json -o bandit-results.json || true
      
      - name: Run Gitleaks
        run: |
          gitleaks detect --source . --report-format json --report-path gitleaks-results.json || true
      
      - name: Run Dependency-Check
        uses: dependency-check/Dependency-Check_Action@main
        with:
          project: 'MyProject'
          path: '.'
          format: 'JSON'
      
      - name: SonarQube Scan
        uses: sonarsource/sonarqube-scan-action@master
        env:
          SONAR_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SONAR_TOKEN }}
          SONAR_HOST_URL: ${{ secrets.SONAR_HOST_URL }}
      
      - name: Upload results to DefectDojo
        if: always()
        run: |
          python scripts/upload_to_defectdojo.py \
            --engagement ${{ github.run_number }} \
            --results semgrep-results.json bandit-results.json
      
      - name: Security Gate
        run: |
          python scripts/security_gate.py \
            --threshold-critical 0 \
            --threshold-high 5 \
            --results semgrep-results.json bandit-results.json
```

**Security Gate Script:**

```python
# File: scripts/security_gate.py
"""
Security quality gate for CI/CD
"""

import json
import sys
import argparse

def check_security_gate(results_files, threshold_critical, threshold_high):
    """Check if security findings exceed thresholds"""
    
    total_critical = 0
    total_high = 0
    
    for results_file in results_files:
        try:
            with open(results_file, 'r') as f:
                data = json.load(f)
            
            # Parse different tool formats
            if 'results' in data:  # Semgrep
                for finding in data['results']:
                    severity = finding['extra']['severity'].upper()
                    if severity == 'ERROR':
                        total_critical += 1
                    elif severity == 'WARNING':
                        total_high += 1
            
            elif 'results' in data and isinstance(data['results'], list):  # Bandit
                for finding in data['results']:
                    severity = finding['issue_severity'].upper()
                    if severity == 'HIGH':
                        total_high += 1
                    elif severity == 'MEDIUM':
                        total_high += 1
        
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error processing {results_file}: {e}")
    
    print(f"\nSecurity Scan Results:")
    print(f"  Critical: {total_critical}")
    print(f"  High: {total_high}")
    print(f"\nThresholds:")
    print(f"  Critical: {threshold_critical}")
    print(f"  High: {threshold_high}")
    
    if total_critical > threshold_critical:
        print(f"\n❌ FAILED: Critical findings ({total_critical}) exceed threshold ({threshold_critical})")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    if total_high > threshold_high:
        print(f"\n❌ FAILED: High findings ({total_high}) exceed threshold ({threshold_high})")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    print("\n✅ PASSED: Security quality gate")
    sys.exit(0)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--results', nargs='+', required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--threshold-critical', type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument('--threshold-high', type=int, default=10)
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    check_security_gate(
        args.results,
        args.threshold_critical,
        args.threshold_high
    )
```
